### 6.10 As built (M48a): Skins + Pixel
Built on the preview commit `308fbae` (Skins, ArtSkin, the owner-approved grids, the hooks in rect_visual / pickup / explosion / kill_pop / exit_gate / arena / elite_mark, pixel snapping and physics interpolation while the look is on, `--look=pixel` and F10). The approved art is unchanged: the grids gained `ROLES` and the two `ui_panel` ids only. Nothing of M48b (1.7.1's reveal, `SkinMorph`, the bugs, S9) is built. `BUILD = "M48a"`.
- **`Palette` (`systems/palette.gd`, 150 lines):** **78 roles**, 1.7's 14 (`Palette.CORE`) plus 64 finer ones (one per colour that was hard-coded, so tier 0 stays exact: `standable`, `depth`, `shadow`, `outline`, `gem`, `crate`, `gate`, `hunter_body`, `orbit_shard`, `well_field`, `burn`, `lancer_line`, `dust`, `hit`, `muzzle`, `dash`, `spark`, `pip_back`, `vignette`, the 5 `turn_*`, `hint`, `marker`, 21 UI roles, 16 mascot roles). Each role is a constant (the tier-0 value, today's colour) and a `TIER_0` entry. `Palette.color(role)` is live: `Skins.current.role(role)` when the look names it, else tier 0 (magenta for an unknown role). Role names avoid mutation ids (`hunter_body`, `well_field`; `elite` is 1.7's and is exempt, below).
- **Every hard-coded colour routed (41 files):** `entities/` enemy_lancer, explosion, pickup, player, projectile, rect_visual; `levels/` arena, exit_gate; `mutations/` elite/elite_mark, hunter/hunter_body, orbit/orbit_shards, well/well_field; `weapons/traits/burn_line_zone.gd`; `paths/` arsenal/hand_widget, auto/level_widget, path_runner, survival/ammo_widget; `rules/edit_inverted/inverted_hint.gd`; `systems/` feedback, screen_fx, transition, turn_overlay; `ui/` debrief_screen, debug_overlay, dev_session, edit_marker, hud, lock_icon, mascot/mascot, mascot/mascot_body, mascot/bodies/{blob,caret,cursor,window}_body, patch_history, patch_notes, pick_panel, prompt_panel, report_screen, stage_card, start_screen. A file's colour constant became an alias (`const COLOR := Palette.GATE`), an alpha variant `Color(Palette.SHADOW, 0.6)`; the draws a look recolours read `Palette.color(&"...")` live (the gate arrow, the elite pips, the hunter and shard colours, wells, burn lines, the lancer line, the projectile trail, every feedback burst and dash line, the vignette). A lint (`test_no_hard_coded_colour_outside_palette`) allows only white tints (`Color(1, 1, 1, a)`, `Color.WHITE`) outside `palette.gd` and `systems/skins/`.
- **Pixel's roles (`grids.gd` `ROLES`, 31):** the 14 core roles plus `standable`, `depth`, `gem`, `crate`, `gate`, `hunter_body`, `orbit_shard`, `well_field`, `burn`, `lancer_line`, `dust`, `hit`, `muzzle`, `dash`, `spark`, `pip_back`, `vignette`, each from the sprites' own ramps (bg `#3a2a4f`, lancer line `#e63fc4`, well `#b84ee0`, dash `#3ff2d2`...). The UI and mascot roles keep tier 0.
- **The remaining hooks:**
  - `feedback.burst()`: with a look, the particles carry `ArtSkin.particle_texture()` (the 4x4 `particle` sprite made neutral grey, each cell's luminance over its ramp P's, so the burst's role colour is the hue; the grid itself is unchanged) at one whole scale per burst, `max(1, round(size / 3))`. Death puffs, kill pops and wall sparks take the sprite's main colour (`ArtSkin.main_color(id)`, its ramp's P) instead of the RectVisual's.
  - `WeaponGlyph.draw`: with a look, the picture is `shot_<icon>_player` at the largest whole scale that fits, dimmed by the ink's brightness, so Arsenal's cards, weapon pickups and the shots show one picture. Weapon pickups draw it 20 px above the gun sprite (16 px box).
  - The lancer's line is a `LanceLine` (`entities/lance_line.gd`, a Line2D whose `strength` setter colours it from `lancer_line`): the lancer is a walker behaviour, kept off Palette by lint 2. The dash ghost and the jump shadow stay flat shapes (no sprite id in 1.7's list) and take `dash` / `shadow`.
  - The wells, burn lines, hunter body and orbit shards read their Palette roles (the hunter and shards are drawn by their sprites; their RectVisual colour feeds the puffs).
- **Pixel's UI panel:** `ArtSkin.ui_panel(lit := false) -> StyleBoxTexture`, the 8x8 `ui_panel` / `ui_panel_lit` grids pre-scaled 3x (24x24, margins 6 px drawn 1:1, centre tiled), cached. `Skins.dress_button(b)` (normal / disabled plain; hover, pressed, focus, hover_pressed lit) and `Skins.dress_panel(c)`; no look: nothing changes. Worn by Arsenal's hand (its own copy per lit state, modulate per card: alpha for a joining card, `UI_DIM` for the others), the dev chat's cards and its list / play buttons, the pick panel's cards, and the debrief's cards and buttons.
- **The mascot's pixel style (`ui/mascot/pixel_body_view.gd`, `PixelBodyView`):** the unchanged MascotBody drawn into a SubViewport at a third of its scale (corner: 76x76 px; debrief: 200x200) and shown by a Sprite2D at 3x, nearest. The bubble stays outside it (crisp text). `Mascot._rebuild_body()` and `DebriefScreen._set_body()` wrap the body while a look is on; `point_at_screen` maps the target into the viewport. The view covers `REACH` 100 body units each side, more than `worst_bounds()` (tested), and `keep_out()` returns the same rect with the look on and off (tested). Re-renders: on an expression change, and at 8 Hz (`STEP_HZ`) while the body's clock runs; a frozen body never re-renders (tested).
- **Live swap:** `Skins.SKINNED` group (RectVisual, Pickup, ExitGate, Arena, the elite ring, Arsenal's hand); `Skins.use()` bumps `version`, calls `queue_redraw` on the group, and emits `Skins.hub.skin_changed` (the corner mascot and the debrief rebuild their bodies, keeping expression, talking, pointing and glitch via `Mascot.carry_state`; the debrief re-dresses its cards and buttons, `Skins.redress_button`). A RectVisual re-reads its id at the new version. Switches: `--look=pixel`, F10, and the F2 console's new **LOOK** row (`off` / `pixel`, `Skins.use_named`). `Skins.bake_msec` records the bake.
- **Lints and checks (`tests/gut/test_pixel_skin.gd`, 10 -> 22 tests, 28 after the review):** (1) `LOOK_READERS` gains `systems/palette.gd` and `systems/skins/skins.gd`; (2) enemy_walker / common / lancer / bomber / shooter, spawner, survival drops and every `components/` and `enemies/` script read no `Palette`, `Skins` or `ArtSkin`; (3) `palette.gd`, `systems/skins/` and its art read no `Tuning`, `WorldRules`, `RunState.rules`, gameplay RNG, `randf` / `randi` / `RandomNumberGenerator` / `get_instance_id` (comments stripped); no image file under a game folder; every role used in code exists, Pixel names the 14 core roles; the hard-coded-colour lint; the UI panel; particles and glyphs; the mascot style; the live swap; the bake under 150 ms.
- **Deviations:**
  - **Scene colours stay scene data:** the RectVisual colours in `player.tscn` and the 5 enemy scenes are not routed; Palette's `player` and `enemy` mirror player and chaser, and with a look the effects take the sprite's colour.
  - **UI roles are read once when a screen is built** (tier-0 constants); only the world draws follow a live swap. A screen already open keeps its buttons' style until rebuilt, except the debrief, which re-dresses itself on a swap (review).
  - **No `LookPreset` fields and no `look_1_pixel.tres`:** v9.2 parks the try-on and `fork_look`; the look is switched by `Skins` directly. `systems/look.gd` is untouched. The skin class is `ArtSkin` (preview), its API `draw` / `draw_silhouette` / `draw_tile` / `ui_panel` / `particle_texture` / `role` / `main_color`, and it bakes on the main thread (8.5 ms headless, 14.0 ms at a windowed boot), not on `WorkerThreadPool`.
  - **`skin_changed` lives on `Skins.hub`**, not EventBus (several tests pin EventBus's signal list).
  - **The mascot re-renders at 8 Hz while it moves**, not only on an expression change: rendered once per expression, the talking mouth, the bounce and the glitch would freeze.
  - **The mutation lint skips `systems/palette.gd`** (1.7's role `elite`), as it skips `systems/skins/`. The no-image check exempts the six v6 dev-session stills (`devsession/stills/*.png`, older than the rule) and `addons/`.
  - **Art note for the owner:** `shot_ring_player` and `shot_hole_player` are the same square ring, so the orbit and well cards show one picture. Left as approved; a one-grid change if wanted.
  - Particles under Pixel are one size per burst (whole scales keep them crisp), not 0.6-1.3x of it.
- **Numbers (hard):**
  - Hashes, look off: G1 v6 4 runs 0 failures; G2 gauntlet 4/0; G3 campaign vs `ref_m43.3` 16/0; G4 S4 vs `ref_m42.2` 4/0; G7 S7 per-10-frame vs `ref_m47` 4/0; G16 S8 Arsenal / Auto vs `ref_m47` and Survival vs `ref_m47_2` 2/0 each. **Look on (`--look=pixel`):** campaign 16/0, S4 4/0, S7 2/0, S8 × 3 2/0 each (`.check/pixel_art/gates/`, `.check/m48a/g4_*`, `p4_*`, `g7_*`, `p7_*`).
  - Ids: 43 baked (39 sprites, the aim arrows and `ui_panel` / `ui_panel_lit` included, plus 4 tiles), every required and optional id resolves, the grids parse with 0 problems, two bakes byte-identical. **Atlas hash (`ArtSkin.digest()`, images + roles): `f8bf9165125a2035d8b86fbaa854ce093b659688871f095fd65953077a4f1451`.** Bake 8.5 ms (GUT, headless), 14.0 ms (windowed boot), gate 150.
  - Silhouette IoU (`render_sheet.gd`): worst enemy pair **0.665** (tank / hunter; gate < 0.7); player vs each enemy at most **0.598** (vs splitter; gate < 0.6). Contrast vs background `#3a2a4f` (L 0.031): player 9.14:1, player shot 10.49:1, enemy shot 4.16:1, orbit shard 11.59:1, gem 7.97:1; player shot vs enemy shot hue 172 deg.
  - GUT, the affected files (not the full suite): 375 tests in 18 files, all pass: test_pixel_skin 22, readability 19, mutations 19, mascot 11, hud_layout 10, arsenal 36, lancer 3, polish 4, dev_session 40, survival 23, weapons 36, turn_based 12, s8_day_one 20, auto_scale 21, campaign 48, stage2_door 21, entities 9, basics_added 21; no SCRIPT ERROR / Parse Error lines.
- **Windowed:**
  - **Tier 0** (`.check/ref_m42.2/capture_tier0.gd`, idle machine) vs `.check/ref_m47_2/tier0_1..5.png`: seeds 2, 3, 4 **0 px**; seed 1 151 px (x 636-667, y 382-403, the player's particles), seed 5 33 px (x 653-665, y 481-500); the masked pixel (685, 484) is the same. A repeat of seeds 1 and 5 on the same tree differs from the first by 167 / 40 px in the same places (capture noise, as since M43.1), so tier 0 is unchanged.
  - **Bench** (`tools/bench.gd --auto`, 55 enemies at the cap, 4 weapons + nova + well at rank 3, 60 gems, vsync off, RX 7900 XTX): tier 0 1029 / 1075 fps (0.97 / 0.93 ms), Pixel 753 / 767 fps (1.33 / 1.30 ms): **+0.37 ms** (gate +1.0), far over 60 fps.
  - `.check/sprites_pixel.png` (1600x614: every id and frame at 1x and 4x, the UI panels included) and `.check/m48a/` captures with `--look=pixel` (`capture_pixel.gd`, seed 1, invincible bot): `s8_arsenal.png` (+ `_hand_zoom`, `_mascot_zoom`), `s8_auto.png`, `s8_survival.png` at 31 s, `s8end_arsenal.png` (S8's debrief) and `s7_debrief.png` (fork_path's cards). Seen: the arena, bodies, shots and pickups in the approved art; Arsenal's three cards as plum 9-slices with the equipped one gold-rimmed and each weapon's shot sprite as its picture; the cursor mascot in chunky 3x pixels in the corner and in the debrief, its bubble text crisp; the debrief's path cards and "To be continued" button in the pixel panel, the focused card lit.
- **Review (12 findings, 8 upheld, all fixed):** each pinned by a test that fails without the fix (7: a new enforced gate).
  1. **The debrief ignored a live swap** (F10 works while it is open): it now listens to `Skins.hub.skin_changed`, rebuilds the big body in the new style (state kept), rebuilds the cards (input delay and focus kept) and re-dresses the buttons (`Skins.redress_button` drops the old panel). Test `test_the_debrief_follows_a_live_look_swap`.
  2. **A swap dropped the mascot's talking / pointing / glitch:** `Mascot.carry_state(from, to)` copies the expression and its clock, `t`, talking, the point lean and the glitch onto the rebuilt body (MascotBody's code unedited). Test `test_a_look_swap_keeps_the_mascot_talking_pointing_and_glitch`.
  3. **The warm particle sprite skewed every burst's hue** (cyan dash bursts drew green): `particle_texture()` is now the grid made neutral grey (`ArtSkin.particle_image()`; P is white). The approved grid and the atlas hash are unchanged. Test `test_the_particle_sprite_is_neutral_so_bursts_keep_their_colour`.
  4. **The Rework card mixed the flat box (at rest) with the pixel panel (hover):** with a look it wears the panel in every state, tinted `REWORK_TINT`. Test `test_the_rework_card_wears_one_style_with_a_look` (test_dev_session).
  5. **Arsenal's pixel cards lost the join flash:** `panel_modulate(lit, a, join_flash(i))` brightens the panel's modulate by the flash (0 under reduce flashing, as tier 0). Test `test_the_pixel_hand_keeps_the_join_flash`.
  6. **fork_mascot's card previews stayed vector bodies:** with a look each preview is a `PixelBodyView` too. Covered by the debrief swap test.
  7. **The IoU gate was printed, never enforced:** `render_sheet.gd` now adds an error (exit 1) for a pair at or over its limit or a missing id, `run_gates.sh` runs it on the real grids (`g0_sheet_iou.log`), and `test_silhouettes_stay_apart` ports the mask IoU into GUT (headless).
  8. **The particle / glyph test never exercised the hooks:** it now bursts and checks the CPUParticles2D's texture (none with no look), and `test_weapon_glyphs_draw_the_look_pictures` draws every key through `WeaponGlyph.draw` on a probe (`WeaponGlyph.skin_draws` counts sprite draws: all 7 with the look, 0 without).

### 6.11 As built (M48b): the transformation + S9 (v9.2)
Built on M48a (`7d1d559`) from `.check/m48b/map.md`, every default taken. 1.7.1's per-id reveal, `SkinMorph`, the three bugs, the `skin` / `specimen` / `release` cues, `--skins=off`, S8's two new lines and S9 "Fresh paint" as data. Out of scope as planned: the edit cues and `edit_screen_wrap` (M48.1), `fork_layout` (M50), the try-on / `fork_look` / `look` cue (parked). `BUILD = "M48b"`.
- **Reveal state (`systems/skins/skins.gd`, presentation only):** `revealed` (id set), `_all` (default true: every CLI / F10 / console swap reveals everything, M48a unchanged), `_morph` (id -> [t0, length] on `RunState.real_time()`), the live bug (`_glitch_kind`, `_glitch_until`), `disabled` (`--skins=off`). API: `is_on(id)`, `on(id)` (the skin or null), `role_on(role)` (Palette), `all_on()`, `target_ids` / `is_target`, `reveal(group|id|ids, morph_s)`, `reveal_all()`, `reset_reveal(manual)`, `morph_of` / `morphing`, `glitch(kind, s)` / `glitch_kind` / `drawn_id` / `flipped`, `apply_session(session)`. Groups: `player` (player, both aim arrows), `shots` (the 14 shot ids, gem, ammo, heal, battery, weapon, crate, blast, pop, particle, orbit_shard), `arena` (floor_a/b, wall, standable, gate), `ui` (the two panels), `mascot` (a pseudo id), `enemies` (arms the per-spawn reveal; lights `elite`), each of the 9 enemy ids, `all`. `ROLE_ID` maps each Pixel role to the id it follows (arena roles -> floor_a, enemy / danger -> chaser, shot and effect roles -> particle, ...). A reveal bumps `version`, redraws `SKINNED`, and emits `hub.skin_changed` only when the mascot or the UI changed.
- **Hooks:** every `Skins.current != null` test became `Skins.is_on(<id>)` (rect_visual, pickup, explosion, kill_pop, exit_gate, elite_mark, hand_widget, feedback particles + body colour, weapon_glyph, mascot, debrief x2, dev_session, `dress_button` / `dress_panel`; Palette through `role_on`). `apply_motion` stays on `current` (interpolation from the session's start).
- **`SkinMorph` (`systems/skins/skin_morph.gd`, new class):** 4 equal steps: today's rect (the node's own fill and size), a 4x4 mosaic (`ArtSkin.mosaic(id)`: each 4x4 cell block the mean of its opaque pixels), the outline (`ArtSkin.outline(id)`: edge pixels in the ramp's P), then the sprite with a white 4-point sparkle fading over the last step. Reduce flashing: step k crossfades into k+1, no sparkle. Mosaic and outline are derived on demand (cached `CanvasTexture`s, `ArtSkin.draw_morph`), not part of `digest()`. The aim arrow appears whole once the player's morph ends. Lengths (SessionRunner): the session's first `morph` cue 3 s, later ones 1 s, `arena` 3 s, `enemies` auto-reveals 1 s; shots, ui, mascot and all are instant.
- **Arena:** `painted_rows()` (rows from the top, `floor(u * 22)`); mid-morph today's arena draws, then the skin over the painted rows (floor, floor_b, depth, walls clipped, the rim on those rows; the standable edge once whole). `_process` redraws while morphing or during `tiles` (and once after). `tile_key(c, r)` for tests.
- **Bugs:** draw-time remaps capped at 3 s (`Skins.BUG_MAX`): `swap` = every enemy body (8 ids) draws `player`; `tiles` = the floor drawn as `wall`, the blocks as `floor_a`, the real blocks keep their rim; `flip` = enemy sprites at y scale -1. Rims, telegraphs, HP bars and the aim arrow never change. `RectVisual.draw_key()` / `skin_version()` for tests.
- **Cues (`campaign/cue.gd`):** `skin <group|id|all> [morph]`, `skin bug <swap|tiles|flip> <0<s<=3>`, `spawn <id> <n> specimen`, `release`. SessionDef refuses: a skin cue without `skin` + `look_reveal manual`, manual without a skin, an unknown skin, a release before any specimen, a specimen never released or spawned after the release, a non-walker specimen (`SPECIMEN_BASES`: chaser, tank, bomber, lancer), `spawn_after < 0`, `reveal_lines` without a `skin enemies` cue.
- **SessionDef fields (D1):** `skin` (`""` / `pixel`), `look_reveal` (`all` / `manual`), `spawn_after` (s), `reveal_lines`. `Skins.apply_session` runs in `main._ready` right after `read_args`: `pixel` puts Pixel on (manual: nothing revealed; a retry replays the transformation); `""` takes off a skin a session put on and keeps a CLI / F10 one; `--skins=off` keeps everything off for the process (F10, CLI, sessions and cues are no-ops). On run end a manual session calls `reveal_all()` (the debrief and an early death end fully revealed).
- **Runtime:** SessionRunner `_fire_skin` (the morph lengths; `skin enemies` connects `EventBus.entity_spawned` and meets the enemies already on screen in tree order; each enemy id met for the first time and not named by a cue says the next `reveal_lines` line, wrapping, and morphs in over 1 s: sim-side, identical with skins on or off). `TopdownGenre.spawn_specimens(archetype, n)` (4 tiles right of `player_start`, 1.5 tiles apart in y, snapped to the nearest reachable tile centre, no jitter, no RNG; `spawn_archetype` untouched) and `release_specimens()`. `EnemyWalker.specimen` (group `specimen`; `_physics_process` holds still, no rim, no contact); `Health.take` returns false for a specimen. `Spawner.hold` / `_held` (S9: 9 s of spawner time; 0 never enters the branch). `CameraRig._init` sets physics process mode when interpolation is already on (the engine forced it anyway, with a warning that GUT counts as an error). `run_hash.gd` and `bot_runs.gd` skip `--skins=off` (bot_runs also `--look=`).
- **Data:** `campaign/sessions/s2_03_paint.tres` (S9, index 9, v0.8.1 -> v0.8.2, `bump patch`, `survive 150` on every path, S8's basics / enemies / spawn 1.3 / 1.3, weapons pistol, shotgun, laser, `skin pixel`, `look_reveal manual`, `spawn_after 9`, the path basics re-added silently at 0 s (D12), the transformation script of the map (specimen 1 s, chaser morph 3 s, "My first asset!" 6.5 s, release 9 s, player 14 s, shots + enemies 20 s, swap 28 s, arena 40 s, tiles 44 s, ui 55 s, flip 62 s, each bug's line 0.5 s after it, mascot 70 s with a line per body, all 72 s, "That's us now" 74 s), the laser at 80 s, nova's line at 90 s on Auto, 4 reveal lines, 6 debrief lines, notes "A new look: Pixel", "The laser"). S8: "It still looks like a prototype" / "I'm going to learn to draw" at 80 / 86 s (Survival 74 / 80 s) and a last debrief line "Next time, I'll have something to show you. A drawing. My first."; no try-on, no fork. `campaign.tres` lists S9 (S8 now offers Next session, S9 "To be continued"). `lines_stage2.md` regenerated (a "Reveal lines" section). `stage2_reactions.json` + 3 S9 rows (25 / 18 / 25 reactions). `check.ps1`: S8 `choice=next_session`, S9 `won t=150.0 choice=to_be_continued v0.8.1->v0.8.2`, `bot_s9_<path>`; `export.ps1` boots S9 per path.
- **Deviations:**
  - **D1: `skin` + `look_reveal` fields, not `look = &"look_pixel"`** (M48a parked LookPreset; `look` stays the legacy juice take, `""` in Stage 2).
  - **D13: no caret line.** S9 has `face = true` and `fork_mascot` offers cursor / window / blob only, so a `mascot:caret` line could never be said; the three bodies keep theirs.
  - **The atlas digest now sorts ids as Strings.** M48a's `digest()` sorted StringNames, i.e. by interning order (pointers), so `f8bf9165...` became `3e35a102...` as soon as new code named ids earlier, with every image and role byte-identical (per-id MD5s compared with HEAD). Sorted as Strings, M48a's grids and M48b's both hash to **`e233951dadbfd53f30a9e1a1ca11efba4090d8abfc69c823e112506d65313755`** (checked on a HEAD worktree); `test_the_atlas_hash_is_m48as` pins it. `grids.gd` untouched.
  - D7: `swap` dresses every enemy body on screen, not only new ones. D9: the specimen 4 tiles right of the start. D11: S9's debrief has no fork until M50. D2: a skin-less session after S9 in the same process drops the session's skin.
  - S9's spawner hold counts spawner time (enabled physics frames), not the goal clock; the two agree in play.
- **Numbers (hard):**
  - Tier 0 (no look): G1 v6 4/0; G2 gauntlet 4/0; G3 campaign vs `ref_m43.3` 16/0; G4 S4 vs `ref_m42.2` 4/0; G7 S7 vs `ref_m47` 4/0; **G16 S8 with its two new lines**: Arsenal / Auto vs `ref_m47`, Survival vs `ref_m47_2`, 2/0 each (say cues never move the hash: no new S8 ref). Look on (`--look=pixel`): campaign 16/0, S4 4/0, S7 2/0, S8 x 3 2/0 each.
  - **S9 refs `.check/ref_m48b/s9_<path>_hash.jsonl`** (every 10 frames, seeds 1 2; 905 / 904 / 905 hashes; kills 139 / 139, 227 / 224, 129 / 127): replayed twice, 2/0 per path each time; **`--skins=off` 2/0 per path** (visuals never touch the simulation); `--look=pixel` 2/0 per path.
  - Silhouettes unchanged: worst enemy pair 0.665 (< 0.7), player vs enemy at most 0.598 (< 0.6), render_sheet 0 errors.
  - Flashes: 3 step changes per morph (1 s and 3 s), at most 3 in any 1 s window sampled at 60 Hz; reduce flashing: sparkle 0, a crossfade.
  - GUT (the affected files, not the full suite): **462 tests in 23 files, all pass**: test_s9_paint 17 (new), pixel_skin 30, s8_day_one 21, campaign 48, stage2_door 21, answers 19, readability 19, mutations 19, mascot 11, hud_layout 10, arsenal 36, survival 23, auto_scale 21, entities 9, dev_session 41, polish 4, cooking 8, reactions 24, basics_added 21, weapons 36, lancer 3, turn_based 12, run_loop 9; no SCRIPT ERROR / Parse Error lines.
  - Bots (`.check/m48b/bot_*.log`): invincible `3i` wins at `t=150.0` on Arsenal, Auto and Survival; the campaign chain (1i, Arsenal): S8 `next_session` v0.8.0 -> v0.8.1, S9 `won t=150.0 choice=to_be_continued v0.8.1->v0.8.2`, 10 sessions, 0 failures. Mortal seeds 1-8 (report only; hit kinds not broken down): Arsenal 8/8 deaths (51.6-143.5 s), Auto 5/8 (101.5-140.1 s), Survival 1/8 (113.1 s): a spread over 30 points, flagged for the S9 clip / G14b (Arsenal plays its first minute on the pistol under S9's 1.3 spawns).
- **Windowed:** `.check/m48b/capture_s9.gd` (inert save, seed 1, invincible bot, Arsenal): stills `.check/m48b/arsenal/s9_<t>.png` at 3.4 / 4.1 / 4.9 / 5.8 s (the specimen's placeholder, mosaic, outline, sprite + sparkle), 14.6 (the player's outline), 29.0 (swap), 41.5 (the arena half painted), 45.0 (tiles), 56.0 (the UI), 62.8 (flip), 71.0 (the mascot); 76 frames `clip_00..75.png` (1 frame/s over S9's first 75 s). Seen: the red square specimen turns into a red mosaic block, a red outline, then the horned chaser with a small sparkle; the player as a cyan outline mid-morph; at 29 s every enemy wears the player's sprite (the real player is told apart only by its aim arrow; rims kept); at 41.5 s the top rows are purple brick and starry floor, the rest today's grey grid; at 45 s the floor is all brick and the blocks dark with their rims; at 56 s Arsenal's card wears the gold pixel panel; at 62.8 s the chaser is upside down; at 71 s the cursor mascot is in chunky pixels (its surprised eyes are noisy at a third of its scale; clean again at 75 s). Bench (`tools/bench.gd --auto --look=pixel`, 55 enemies at the cap, windowed): Pixel **744 fps**, tier 0 1098 fps.
- **Review (5 findings, 5 upheld, all fixed; each new test fails with its fix reverted, checked for 1, 4 and 5; 2 and 3 are pinned through new functions):**
  1. **A reveal after everything is drawn re-morphed it.** `Skins.reveal()` now returns at once while `_all` (a CLI / F10 / console look, or after `skin all`): F10 twice before 14 s no longer turns the player, the elite or the arena back into placeholders. SessionRunner disarms the per-spawn reveal on `skin all` (`_disarm_enemies`: disconnects `entity_spawned`, a later `skin enemies` does not re-arm), decided from the cue, not from Skins, so the lines stay the same with skins on or off. Tests: `test_a_reveal_after_everything_is_drawn_starts_no_morph`, `test_skin_all_ends_the_reveal_lines`.
  2. **A morph hid the hit flash and the telegraph.** `RectVisual.morph_overlay(u, now)`: the placeholder step fills with today's tier-0 fill (white on a hit, the telegraph's lerp), the mosaic / outline / sprite steps get the white silhouette, under the upright / flip transform, before the rim and bar. Test: `test_a_morph_keeps_the_hit_flash_and_the_telegraph`.
  3. **Reduce flashing's last step pulsed to about 75 %.** `SkinMorph.layers(u)`: no step is ever drawn twice; the last step is the sprite alone at full; the outline stays opaque under the incoming sprite; the placeholder and mosaic fade out only where the shapes differ. Test: `test_the_reduce_flashing_crossfade_never_draws_a_step_twice`.
  4. **The specimen / release check read list positions.** `problems()` now compares cue times and conditions: specimen spawns and releases must be `at` cues; each specimen needs an unconditioned (or same-condition) release later in time (the same second only when listed after it); a release needs an earlier specimen; a release narrower than its specimen's condition is refused. `["at 9: release", "at 1: spawn chaser 1 specimen"]` is now valid; `at 0.5` release, `answer:path_auto` release and `kills 3: release` are refused (in `test_the_session_refuses_bad_transformation_data`).
  5. **`apply_session` claimed a CLI / F10 Pixel.** `_by_session` is set only when the session itself put the skin on; a skin-less session after S9 keeps the player's look and reveals it whole (`reveal_all`). Test: `test_a_cli_look_survives_s9_then_a_plain_session`.
  - Also found by the full suite (the build ran only the affected files): `test_enemy_db.test_one_list_in_the_code` failed (SessionDef's `SPECIMEN_BASES` and Skins' `ENEMY_IDS` / `BODY_IDS` repeated the enemy list): now `EnemyDB.WALKERS` (SessionDef reads it) and Skins derives its bodies from `EnemyDB.all()` plus the sprite-only `splitter_mini`, `hunter` (and `elite`). `test_s7_tryout`'s two owner-save tests expected the campaign done after S8: they now complete S9 (v0.8.2) first.
  - After the fixes: every hash gate 0 failures (tier 0, `--look=pixel`, S9 x 3 paths replayed twice, `--skins=off` and `--look=pixel` against the M48b refs: no ref changed), the full GUT suite, `check.ps1 -BotRuns`, and the preview export `builds/pixel_preview/PatchNotes.exe` (logs in `.check/m48b/review/`).

### 6.12 As built (M47.3): options and pause (v9.1, built after M48b)
*Built on the M48b tree; the row predates M48a/M48b, so the menus follow the as-built code (6.10, 6.11): the Pixel UI panel when the `ui` reveal group is on, neutral otherwise; F10 still toggles the look and F2 is still the debug console. Map and defaults: `.check/m47_3/map.md` (D1-D19). Not committed.*
- **`Settings`** (`core/settings.gd`, static): `SPEC` holds the 14 keys of 1.16 (types, defaults, ranges or allowed sets); `get_value / set_value / commit / valid / parse / defaults / apply / window_applies / type_cps / shake_mult / key_hints / chatter`; `Settings.hub.changed(key)` (a static class has no signal: the `Skins.hub` pattern). **Deviation (D1): the new keys are sparse.** `new_state()` keeps exactly `{reduce_flashing, asked}` (pinned by test_campaign), a new key is written once the player changes it; `SaveGame.parse()` merges `Settings.parse()` after its strict 2-key check, so a wrong new key is dropped and reads its default, an old non-bool still quarantines, unknown keys are still dropped. `set_value` never writes; `commit()` (OptionsScreen on Back) writes once. `apply()` runs deferred from `InputSetup._ready()` (the later autoloads are not in the tree yet) and again in StartScreen after the real save is read / goes live.
- **Deviation: `--fixed-fps` is not detectable from a script** (the engine consumes it; `OS.get_cmdline_args()` never shows it). Window and frame settings are skipped headless and under any `-s` tool, which covers every `--fixed-fps` run here (GUT, hashes, bots, captures); `apply(true)` forces them (the windowed fullscreen check).
- **`AudioBuses`** (`systems/audio_buses.gd`): SFX, UI, Voice made once by name, sending to Master. Voice = `compile`, `glitch`, `edit_warn`; UI = `click`, `prompt`, `release_candidate`; SFX = the rest (D5). Each player stays at -6 dB. The raw start screen's Volume slider is `vol_master`, its Fullscreen toggle `window_mode` 1 / 0 (both go live and commit, like Less flashing). "Mute when unfocused": `InputSetup._notification` focus out / in.
- **`PauseMenu`** (`ui/pause_menu.gd`, layer 98, ALWAYS, reason `&"menu"`): `allowed(game)` / `can_open()` refuse with no game, a genre over, the debrief, the stage card, `transition.busy`, an event holding the clock, `&"event"` / `&"history"` / `&"dev_session"` / `&"report"` pauses, B2 in a turn, the v6 prompt, the report or the v6 dev session (the last four are additions to 1.16's list); it opens over a pick panel and in B2's wait. A refused Esc is not consumed (the build log still closes on it). Full (Stage 2 on): Resume / Options / Restart session (confirm "Restart this session? Counts as a try.", Yes / No) / Quit to title, wrapping focus. Raw (Stage 1, the v6 loop): one grey monospace line `PAUSED   [Escape] resume   [Q] quit to start`, Q by position (AZERTY prints A), pad Y quits too. While open, `_input` / `_unhandled_input` swallow every non-GUI event; on close the human input gets `require_fresh_press()`.
- **The menu is transparent to the simulation (D7, not spelled in 1.16):** `_freeze(on)` holds the hit stop (`Feedback.hold`: its real-time timer's time left is kept and re-armed on close), the fingerprint's pick clock (`Fingerprint.hold`), the speech queue (`SessionRunner.hold_speech`) and the typing (`Mascot.hold`); BotInput skips its tick and PickPanel its lock (and `can_choose()` is false) while `&"menu"` is set. `RunState.menu_frames` counts the menu's frames and `real_time()` subtracts them (D6: so the presentation clocks and S9's morphs stop under the menu too); `real_frames` stays raw. `RunState.clear_pauses()` drops every reason and every time factor.
- **Quit / Restart** (`Main.menu_quit / menu_restart`): one campaign telemetry line, schema 4, `outcome "quit"`, `choice "abandon"` / `"menu_retry"` (D9; the v6 loop logs nothing on quit); Restart counts the attempt (`CampaignDB.retry`) and reloads with a new seed; Quit changes to `StartScreen.TITLE_SCENE` with the save untouched. `Main.menu_telemetry_always` lets a test's copy log.
- **`OptionsScreen`** (`ui/options_screen.gd`): 4 tabs (Q / E or LB / RB through `weapon_prev/next`, no new action), Back on Esc / `ui_cancel` / pad B; Video rows greyed "(ignored in this build)" where `window_applies()` is false; Controls read-only with layout keys (AZERTY: "Z Q S D", "A / Wheel up") and Xbox names, then "Remapping comes in a later patch."; Accessibility: reduce flashing, shake (note "capped at 50% while Reduce flashing is on"), text speed Slow 30 / Normal 45 / Fast 70 / Instant, key hints, mascot chatter.
- **Consumers (D16):** camera shake `shake_scale * Settings.shake_mult()` (defaults = today's 1 / 0.5); `text_speed` drives the corner mascot's and the debrief's typing only (`Mascot.TYPE_CPS` 45 stays for Reactions and the stage card); `key_hints` off hides the debrief hint and the pick card's `[key]` (`PickPanel.card_text(..., hints)`); `chatter` off drops mascot lines below `SessionRunner.PRIORITY` (Reactions still fire and log); the mascot body re-reads `gentle` on `Settings.hub.changed`. The debrief reads `ui_accept` and `choose_1..3` (input only; the physical 1 / 2 on AZERTY).
- **`tools/run_hash.gd --menu=<at>:<hold>,...`** (`at` = unpaused tick or `pick`); prints `menus=<n>/<wanted>` per seed, a missing menu fails. **Deviation:** Survival offers no pick in S8, so its third menu is at tick 1800.
- **`BUILD = "M47.3"`.** Not built here (M47.4): TitleScreen, menu_stack, routing, door_beat, the polished debrief, UiTheme; no Options button on the raw start column (D17).
- **Numbers:** every hash gate 0 failures (v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign 16/16 vs ref_m43.3, S4 4/4 vs ref_m42.2, S7 4/4 vs ref_m47, S8 arsenal / auto 2/2 vs ref_m47, survival 2/2 vs ref_m47_2; the same with `--look=pixel`; S9 x 3 paths replayed twice, `--skins=off` and `--look=pixel` vs ref_m48b; the silhouette sheet exit 0). **Pause determinism:** S8 x 3 paths and S4, 8 seeds each, two patterns (`30:1,600:120,pick:1` and `30:120,600:1,pick:120`; S4 without the pick), every run equal to its no-menu base with every menu opened on time. GUT: test_settings 17/17, test_pause_menu 13/13 and 25 existing files unedited all pass. The owner's backup save loads (`loaded`, settings unchanged, v0.8.0). A fullscreen setting survives a second process (`window_mode=1 fullscreen=true`). Captures: `.check/m47_3/` (4 option tabs, pause over S8 x 3 paths and over a pick, the raw line, S9 at 56 s; each with the look off and `--look=pixel`; `fullscreen_restart.png`). Gates: `.check/m47_3/gates.sh`.
- **Review (3 findings, 2 upheld, all fixed; each new test in `test_settings` fails with its fix reverted):** (1) `Settings.apply()` runs on every go-live (Start, the Volume slider, the Fullscreen toggle) and re-applied `window_scale`, snapping a moved or resized window back to 1280x720 centred: `window_scale` is now applied only when it differs from the last scale set on a windowed window (`_scale_applied`; boot still applies it once) (`test_window_scale_is_applied_once_not_on_every_apply`). (2) A window scale picked while fullscreen or borderless was dropped until the next boot: `_apply_window("window_mode")` now applies `window_scale` when the new mode is windowed (`test_window_scale_picked_in_fullscreen_lands_on_windowed`). Test seams: `Settings.scale_applies` (resize count) and `Settings.test_window_mode` (the mode `_apply_window` sees; -1 = the display's). The third finding was not upheld.


### 6.13 As built (M47.4): the front door, seen (v9.1, built after M48b + M47.3)
*Built on the M47.3 tree; the row predates M48a/M48b, so the menus follow the as-built code: the title's menus, the beat's ghost menu and the polished chat window take the look's UI panel when its `ui` group is on (F10 here, `--look=pixel`), the neutral UiTheme otherwise; F10 still toggles the look, F2 is still the debug console. Map and defaults: `.check/m47_4/map.md` (D1-D17). Not committed.*
- **Routing** (`StartScreen._ready`): `CampaignDB.front_door_open()` (not the legacy loop) builds `TitleScreen` (child "Title"), else the raw column byte-for-byte as before. Open = S7 (`FRONT_DOOR_SESSION`) done (the stub-era done S7 counts) or attempted, the save past S7, or standing at S7 with `front_door_seen` (quit mid-S7). The end-of-Stage-1 save (at S7, not played) keeps the raw screen: S7's beat shows it change (D1). StartScreen keeps every static and hook; its `continue_button`, `new_button`, `save_label`, `flash_panel`, `replay_session`, `replay_path` are the title's controls; `refresh_campaign()` / `_start_campaign()` are null-safe (`flashing`, `seed_field`); `var title` is null on the raw screen. The boot line ends with ", title" when the title is built (check.ps1 / export.ps1 regexes still match).
- **`TitleScreen`** (`ui/title/title_screen.gd`): `TitleBackdrop` (the grid scrolling 12 / 6 px/s on frame time), `TitleLogo` ("PATCH NOTES" from a 5x7 block font, `GLYPHS` P A T C H N O E S; cells drop column-major via `assembled`; with the look's UI on: 3 px grid, no gap, the look's `accent`), the version chip, the campaign line, a pause chip and "build M47.4". Menu on a `MenuStack`: Continue / New game (confirm "Start over? Your progress goes; your settings stay." Yes / No; no confirm on an unstarted campaign) / Replay (once S8 is done: session, path, Seed, Replay, Back) / Options (OptionsScreen; `_go_live()` first, its Back commits and pops) / Quit. The mascot (save's body, x1.3) on the right says one `TITLE_LINES` line per launch, picked by `done.size() % 4` (no RNG). **Deviation:** the second draft reads "Look! A real menu." ("Look." trips test_readability's look lint). The raw column's Seed lives in Replay; Continue / New game play a random seed on the title (D-open 5).
- **`MenuStack`** (`ui/menus/menu_stack.gd`): `push(menu, focus)` hides the menu below, `pop()` returns focus to the opener, Back on `ui_cancel` / `pause` / pad B (none at the root), a menu with a `closed` signal (OptionsScreen) handles its own Back and the stack pops on `closed`; static `wrap_focus()`, `first_focusable()`. PauseMenu not migrated (D10).
- **The unveil** (D4): `front_door_open()` and not `front_door_seen` -> a `DoorBeat` cut (3.0-9.0 s, no lines) over the title, skippable after 0.5 s, then "guilty: You missed it. I made this while you were away.". **Deviation (not spelled in 1.16):** the flag is written when the unveil starts, so the title goes live on the real save for that one write (`_go_live()`, as Start would); without it the snapshot's flag was lost on Continue and S7's card replayed the full beat (not "exactly once"). `New game` (`reset_campaign()`, split out of `_new_campaign()` so a test can call it without a scene change) erases `front_door_seen` from the kept settings (D2).
- **`DoorBeat`** (`ui/title/door_beat.gd`): every pose a function of `elapsed` (frame time; `step()`); `LENGTH` 12.0, `LINE_AT` [0.3, 7.0, 11.2] (review fix; was [0.3, 9.2, 10.6]), wipe 3.0-4.0 (one `glitch.gdshader` pulse; reduce flashing: a crossfade, strength 0), logo 4.0-6.0, buttons slide into the title's menu rects 6.0-7.5, the four rows fly into "Options" 7.5-9.0, pause chip at 9.0. The replica is built from StartScreen's texts (never instantiates it). `SessionDef.door_beat` (PackedStringArray, Cooking group) + `door_beat_lines()`; problems: empty line, condition, Line / `{key:}` problem, more than `DOOR_BEAT_MAX` (3 = `DoorBeat.LINE_AT.size()`), stage 1. `CampaignDB.door_beat_due(s)`: S7's card is due and (`jumped` or not seen); `CampaignDB.jumped` is set by a `--session=` jump.
- **StageCard**: `setup_door(lines)` before `setup_beat()`; the door first (mascot on the right third, its lines with keys expanded), `front_door_seen` set + committed at the door's start, a key after 0.5 s ends the door (the cook beat starts, re-timed from the door's end), `skip()` closes all (the bot is unchanged). The beat and cooking boxes sit in a framed build window ("patchnotes · build · v0.8.0", siblings, nothing reparented).
- **Polished debrief** (`DebriefScreen.style`, `&"polished"` from stage 2): the column moved into `_window` (plain: `StyleBoxEmpty`, 0 margins, bar hidden: S4's debrief equals before within 4 px); polished: framed window (`UiTheme.window_style()` or the look's panel), bar "patchnotes · dev chat · v0.8.0", first bubble with a 40 px still avatar + speaker tag (the body's change name), typing dots ("…" under reduce flashing), framed cards with a 6 px real-time focus lift and a `key_label(choose_N)` chip, a changelog (`changelog()`: Added / Changed / Fixed; unprefixed notes and picks go to Added) with `_notes.text` still `notes_text()` (hidden), way-on buttons with a key chip child (`choices()` unchanged). **S7 data:** first debrief line "happy: Like the new chat? I redecorated.", notes + "Added a title screen.", "Added options (audio, video, controls, accessibility).", "Added a pause menu." (no new version), the 3 door lines; `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated (door beat + the title section).
- **`UiTheme`** (`ui/theme/ui_theme.gd`): neutral (`default_font` / size = the fallback's) + variations TitleButton, MenuPanel, ChatWindow, ChatBar, KeyChip; applied to the title, DoorBeat, PauseMenu's root, the debrief's root and the card's root controls; never the project theme, the HUD or the corner mascot.
- **Edited existing tests (deviations):** `test_s7_tryout` :94 (the 4 notes); `test_pause_menu` :261 pinned `"M47.3"` as the telemetry build, now `Main.BUILD`.
- **`BUILD = "M47.4"`.**
- **Review (5 findings, 5 upheld, all fixed):** (1) after a key ends S7's door, a second key skipped the whole cook beat at once: `StageCard._input` now gives the cook beat its own 0.5 s from the door's end (`elapsed - _door_end >= SKIP_AFTER`). (2) with the look's UI on, the beat's ghost buttons popped into the Pixel panel when the unveil handed over: `DoorBeat._dress_ghost` dresses each ghost button (and Options) as `TitleScreen._dress` does once it is titled (per `Skins.version`, so F10 mid-beat redresses). (3) the title grid jumped at the hand-over: `_end_unveil` sets `backdrop.scroll` from `DoorBeat.backdrop_scroll()`. (4) `tests/gut/test_front_door.gd.uid` added. (5) door line 2 was cut by line 3 about 0.6 s after typing: `LINE_AT` is now [0.3, 7.0, 11.2], so each line types at the slowest text speed (30 cps) and stays up for `SessionRunner.read_seconds()` before the next (line 2 now lands as the rows start to fly into Options; line 3 after the pause chip, carried into the cook beat). New tests in test_front_door (4, each failed before its fix): the double tap, the reading time, the ghost dress with Pixel on and off, the grid hand-over. Tidy (builder note): after the door, the mascot (the last door line still up) waits 16 px under the cook beat's build window (`BEAT_FRAME_H`, `BELOW_FRAME`) and goes back under the title when the card shows (`test_the_last_door_line_waits_under_the_build_window`).
- **Numbers:** every hash gate 0 failures (v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign 16/16 vs ref_m43.3, S4 4/4 vs ref_m42.2, S7 4/4 vs ref_m47 (its card now carries the door beat; the bot skips it), S8 arsenal / auto 2/2 vs ref_m47, survival 2/2 vs ref_m47_2; the same with `--look=pixel`; S9 x 3 paths replayed twice, `--skins=off` and `--look=pixel` vs ref_m48b; silhouette sheet exit 0). Pause determinism: S8 x 3 paths and S4, 8 seeds, both patterns, every run equal to its base, menus 3/3 (S4 2/2). GUT: test_front_door 26/26 (raw-screen snapshot `tests/gut/start_screen_raw_m47_3.json`, 3 variants recorded at M47.3 HEAD; routing; the unveil once; menu / MenuStack; door beat <= 12 s, key at 0.4 s ignored / 0.6 s skips, 1 flash rise in any 1 s window, none under reduce flashing; polished / plain debrief; UiTheme re-measures `tests/gut/ui_measure_m47_3.json` equal) and 25 existing files pass (two edited, above). The owner's backup save loads (`loaded`, settings unchanged, v0.8.0, front door open, unveil due). Windowed captures: `.check/front_door/` (before_*, title_raw, title_polished + title_unveil_00..29, title_polished_2, options_from_title, debrief_raw(_lines), debrief_polished(_lines, _notes), beat_start / beat_000..074 / beat_end; each also `_pixel`); `debrief_raw` vs `before_debrief_s4`: 3 px differ (> 2 LSB), `title_raw` vs before: only the build number. Gates: `.check/m47_4/gates.sh`.

### 6.14 As built (M47.5a): always on, hosting and the terminal beat (v9.3)
*Built on the M47.4 tree (47260c3) from `.check/always_on/design.md` (every default D1-D16 and the open questions' defaults taken). Feedback-size milestone: one builder plus the gates, then one review pass (below). Captures: `.check/m47_5a/`. Tag `m47.5a`.*
- **`GameHost`** (`ui/always_on/game_host.gd` + `.tscn`, `class_name GameHost extends Control`): Back (black), `GameView` (SubViewportContainer, stretch off, ALWAYS), `GameViewport` (SubViewport, ALWAYS, `size_2d_override` 1280x720 + stretch, `size` = the game column), `Glitch` (glitch.gdshader) and `Fade` over the column, `Terminal`. The session's Main gets `host` and `PROCESS_MODE_PAUSABLE` before `add_child`. `layout_for(window)`: k = min(H/720, (W-360)/1280), the column centred vertically, the terminal the rest (480 at 1760x720; 360 at 1920x1080 with k 1.22, game 1560x878), font 16 × clamp(k, 1, 2). Live (`current_scene == self`, never a tool host): the root's content scale off and `Settings.set_window_base(1760x720)`; `_exit_tree` restores both (the title and the cooking screen see 1280x720 again). `host_for_tools(game, view_size)`: never live, no window change. `auto_way_on()` = live and `GameHost.auto_beats` (static; bot_runs sets it false).
- **The swap** (`next_session(restart)`): `$ hot-reload game ...` (or `$ restart <id> (try N) ...`), live: a 0.25 s glitch pulse (reduce flashing: a crossfade), then `_swap`; otherwise `_swap` deferred. `_swap` removes the old Main and **frees it at once** (see deviations), adds a fresh `main.tscn`, prints `... ok`, `--- session N · Title · vX ---`, `$ run <id>`, then `hold()` unless `GameHost.auto_beats` is false (bot chains; a tool host with `auto_beats` on holds too, as the swap test relies on): `&"handoff"` + `you> _` until the first `move_*` / `shoot` / `dash` / `jump` press (`you> play`); a stage card open first is left to its own key. `skip_handoff()` for tools. Mouse motion over the terminal reaches the game as an event clamped to the column's edge (`push_input`); the aim itself (`get_global_mouse_position`) follows the real mouse past the edge. `relayout` sizes the viewport before the container (stretch off: the container's minimum is the viewport's size). `_exit_tree` also drops a held `&"handoff"`.
- **`TerminalPanel`** (`ui/always_on/terminal.gd`): `#0a0a0c` back, 1 px grey left rule, title row `patchnotes  dev console  vX`, a ScrollContainer (PASS, quiet bar) of Labels, `UiTheme.mono_font()` (a code-made SystemFont: Cascadia Mono, Consolas, DejaVu Sans Mono, Courier New, monospace), line spacing 4. Rows: say (dim header `[hh:mm] <name>@patchnotes[ (expr)]` shared by the same speaker/expression within 10 s, then the text indented, `visible_characters` typing, a cyan `▌` on the newest cue line, chatter (priority < 2) dim and clipped to one row), cmd (cyan), note (`  + Added ...` / `~ Changed` / `- Fixed`), think (`<name> is thinking ⠋ . . .`, braille when the font has U+280B else `| / - \`, 1.2 s, 0.4 s at instant text), choice (yellow, clickable), you (`you> _`, blinking; steady under reduce flashing, read live by `caret_on()`), sys (dim). Play mode: `live_say` / `live_cut` from `Mascot.line_started` / `line_cut` (a new line completes the one typing, ` —` when cut). Beat mode: `say_row()` hands the debrief its label; `cmd` / `notes` / `sys` / `think` / `ask` / `prompt` queue (one row per 0.06 s, a think holds the queue). `finish()`, `is_busy()`, `rows()`, 300-row cap, clock 14:00 + 3 min × `current_index()` + real minutes since the terminal started. Real time only (ticks_usec), no keys read.
- **`Mascot`**: signals `line_started(line, priority)` / `line_cut(line)`, `bubble_enabled` (false when hosted: the corner body, its queue and timing stay), `silence(hide := true)` (hosted: the body stays for the beat).
- **`DebriefScreen.view = &"terminal"`** (Main sets `view`, `terminal`, `corner`, `auto_way_on` when hosted): the root stays hidden; `is_open()` reads `_open`; lines become terminal rows the debrief still times; `_beat_body()` sends every expression and mouth to the corner body (`big_body` is never made); the fork: hidden cards (the API's) + `[n] <name>  <pitch>` rows + `you> _` + raw preview boxes over the frozen game (`_preview()` shared with the cards: the live mascot bodies, path stills, take images; none when no option has a picture); a pick prints `you> N`, clears the boxes, and a mascot pick lands at once on the corner (`setup(true, id)`, surprised, glitch 0.35) and renames the prompt (`GameHost.speaker_for`). `_finish`: a death (not overrun, not moved on) asks `[1] again` / `[2] move on (I'll take this one)`; else think, `$ build vX ... ok`, `$ patch apply vX`, the changelog, then with `auto_way_on` the first choice 0.4 s after the rows are typed. `choices()` / `choose()` keep NEXT / LAST / RETRY / MOVE_ON; terminal view latches one way on per open. Keys stay the debrief's (Enter during the patch rows shows them at once). Screen view unchanged.
- **`Main`**: `host`, `_reload(seed, restart)` (host → `next_session`, else `reload_current_scene`), `is_live_game()` also true for a live host's session, the guards in `_end_session` / `menu_restart` / `menu_quit` use it, `&"handoff"` set before `debrief.close()` when hosted and the session reloads (never when it leaves: `_leave_to(path)` clears every pause, then changes scene, `Main.leave_hook` for tests), `start_new_build` (F5) guarded by `is_live_game()` and routed through `_reload`, `_restart_text()`. **`BUILD = "M47.5a"`.**
- **Routing**: `StartScreen.GAME_SCENE` is the host (`res://ui/always_on/game_host.tscn`), `LEGACY_SCENE` (`main.tscn`) for the v6 loop; title / raw column unchanged.
- **Elsewhere**: `PauseMenu.allowed` refuses during `&"handoff"`; `Settings.set_window_base()`, `clamped_size()` (one uniform factor); `Palette.TERM_*` constants (tier-0 constants, not look roles: role counts unchanged).
- **Tools**: `run_hash --shell [--viewport=WxH] [--handoff=N]` (campaign only; the v6 loop refuses `--shell`); `bot_runs`: `_wait_for_game` through the host (`_game_in`, instance ids), `_skip_card` calls `skip_handoff`, `GameHost.auto_beats = false`; `check.ps1`: hosted campaign (seeds 1 2) vs ref_m43.3 and hosted S8 at 1560x878 with `--handoff=120` vs ref_m47 (skipped when the local refs are missing).
- **Tests**: new `tests/gut/test_always_on.gd` (24; 17 built + 7 from the review): layout at 1760x720 / 1920x1080, the 1280x720 rect at a 1560x878 view and the process modes, every pause reason freezes the game not the host, typing while paused leaves `game_time`, Esc through the container, S3's three forks one key each (choose_2 then choose_1 ×2) landing on the corner + rename + boxes, the S0 rows, the latch and the death rows, play-mode cut, mascot routing with no bubble, row cap / spinner / steady caret, clock and shared header, the swap (old game whole on the swap frame, freed after, handoff held until a move, no pause menu meanwhile), bot hosts (no hold, `skip_handoff`), the reduce-flashing crossfade, the click point equal at k 1 and 1.22, window base set/restore and the uniform clamp; from the review: a live host's replay Next leaves nothing paused, the host leaving drops a held handoff, F5 swaps the same seed in through the host, the caret reads reduce flashing live, a key closes S7's StageCard through the container, `choose_1` picks on an Arsenal PickPanel through the container, real mouse events (`Input.parse_input_event`) land on the same canvas and world point at k 1 and 1.22 and motion over the terminal reaches the game clamped to x 1279. No existing test edited.
- **Deviations:** (1) **No ready-moment hold in bot chains** (`auto_beats` false): the old reload gave the new session one physics tick before the bot attached; holding removed it and moved the mortal chain's S2 death from 25.5 s to 17.9 s. Without the hold the chain lines are byte-identical; the live host holds as designed, and `run_hash --handoff` proves the hold itself moves no hash. (2) **The old Main is freed in the swap, not `queue_free`d**: the swap already runs a frame later (deferred or after the glitch), so a same-frame reader still sees it whole; freeing it before the new `_ready` (as the old reload did) keeps its nodes off `EventBus.run_started` and the groups. (3) **ForkBoxes live in the debrief's own layer** (inside the game view), not in the host. (4) **The first session of a boot is not held** (the player just pressed Continue); only swaps hold. (5) The choose latch is terminal-view only (the screen view's tests choose twice on purpose). (6) An extra `$ build vX ... ok` row before `$ patch apply` (the mockup's). (7) The dash and enemy forks show no boxes (their takes have no images; the boxes appear only when an option has a picture).
- **Numbers:** unhosted: v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign 16/16 vs ref_m43.3, S4 4/4 vs ref_m42.2, S7 4/4 vs ref_m47, S8 arsenal / auto 2/2 vs ref_m47, survival 2/2 vs ref_m47_2, the same with `--look=pixel`, S9 × 3 paths replayed twice + `--skins=off` + `--look=pixel` vs ref_m48b, silhouette sheet exit 0; pause determinism (S4 + S8 × 3, 8 seeds, two menu patterns) 0 failures and its four base files byte-identical to M47.4's. Hosted (`--shell`, default view and 1560x878): campaign 16/16, S4 4/4, S7 4/4, S8 × 3 2/2, S9 × 3 2/2 + `--skins=off`; `--shell --handoff=120`: S4 and S8 × 3, 4 seeds each, equal to the unheld bases. Bot chains through the host: invincible (arsenal, kept save) and mortal (survival) session lines byte-identical to M47.4's (`real=` stripped), 10 telemetry lines each, the final save line equal. The owner's backup save and `save_m43_owner_shape.json` load and continue in the host (S7, ticking). GUT (build): test_always_on 17/17 and 28 affected files 0 failures; after the review see below.
- **Captures** (`.check/m47_5a/`, windowed 1760x720, inert save): `s0_playing`, `s0_goal_lines`, `s0_thinking`, `s0_patch_apply`, `s0_hot_reload`, `s1_ready`, `s1_moving`, `s3_fork_asked`, `s3_fork_answered`, `s3_fork_after`, `s4_death` (script `capture_always_on.gd`).
- **Review (11 findings, 8 upheld, all fixed)** (outputs `.check/m47_5a_review/`): (1+3, one bug reported twice) **the handoff pause leaked to the title**: a hosted replay's Next / Move on and the campaign's end set `&"handoff"` before `debrief.close()` and then changed scene, so the title (and the cooking screen's title) came back paused; now the reason is set only when the session reloads, `_leave_to()` clears every pause before the scene change, and `GameHost._exit_tree` drops a held handoff. (2+5, twice) **F5 did nothing hosted**: `start_new_build` guarded on `current_scene == self`; now `is_live_game()` and `_reload(seed)` (handoff set, the host swaps the same seed in). (4) **The caret's reduce flashing was read once per host**: `TerminalPanel.caret_on()` reads the setting live (`gentle` stays as a test override; the host no longer sets it). (6) **No test drove the StageCard or a PickPanel through the container**: two hosted tests (a key closes S7's card; `choose_1` picks on an Arsenal panel). (7) **The click-point test sent no event**: replaced by real `InputEventMouseMotion`s through `Input.parse_input_event` at both k (the column's 101 px offset included), checking the event the game receives, the viewport's canvas point and the world point, plus a motion over the terminal reaching the game clamped to x 1279. Writing it found a real bug: `relayout` set the container's size before the viewport's, and with stretch off the container's minimum is the viewport size, so going from 1080p back to the base kept a 1560x878 column (and its rect swallowed the clamp); the viewport is now sized first (no hash moves: hash runs lay out once). The test also showed the aim (`get_global_mouse_position`) follows the real mouse past the edge; only the pushed event is clamped (text above corrected). (8) **6.14 said the hold was live only**: corrected (the hold runs unless `auto_beats` is false). Not upheld: 3 findings. After the fixes: all 66 hash summaries 0 failures + sheet exit 0, pause determinism 0 failures with its four base files byte-identical to M47.4's, hosted `--shell` (default and 1560x878) and `--handoff=120` all equal; owner backup + `save_m43_owner_shape.json` continue in the host; full GUT 56 scripts, 1023/1023; `check.ps1 -BotRuns` CHECK PASSED with no expectation changed (mortal chain S2 still dies at 25.5 s).

### 6.15 As built (M47.5b): always on, the story flow (v9.3)
*Built on the M47.5a tree (5e0399a) from `.check/always_on/design.md` (every default D1-D16 and the open questions' defaults Q1-Q8 taken). Feedback-size milestone: one builder plus the gates, no review pass (feedback-speed-over-ceremony). Captures: `.check/m47_5b/`. Not committed.*
- **Boot straight into the host** (`ui/start_screen.gd`, D3/D12/D13): `is_boot_scene()` (the screen Godot booted or changed scene to; never a tool's or test's `add_child` copy, never the legacy loop; `test_boot` for tests). Booted with the front door closed and a writable save, `_ready` builds nothing and defers `_enter_shell()`: `SaveGame.end_replay()`, `_go_live()`, `GameHost.first_run_prompt` (`first_run_due()`: not asked, no `--autostart`, and no campaign session done yet; review), `CampaignDB.active`, `Main.next_seed = -1`, then `continue_target()` (the host, or the cooking screen). `-- --new-game` resets the campaign first (settings kept), once per process (`new_game_pending`, review). The boot line gains `, always on` (check.ps1 / export.ps1 regexes still match). From the door on: the title (M47.4 routing); a read-only save keeps the raw column. `enter_hook` stands in for the scene change in tests.
- **The installer's first-run prompt** (`GameHost`, D13): before S0's first frame, under `&"handoff"`: `patchnotes first run: reduce flashing and screen shake?` `[1] no` `[2] yes` `you> _`; the host reads `choose_1/2` (or a click on a row), saves `reduce_flashing` + `asked`, prints `reduce flashing on|off ([Escape] then [L] to change)` (the raw pause's keys by layout; review), then the session's rows and S0 starts. The AI says nothing in it.
- **Chapter mode** (`GameHost`, 2.6): a new Main whose `stage_card` is open puts the host in chapter mode: `layout_for(window, true)` (the game alone, 16:9, centred, k from the whole window; 1920x1080 -> k 1.5), the terminal hidden, the hot-reload's `ok` printed. On `stage_card.closed`: the always-on layout, `--- chapter 2 · The shooter ---`, the session's rows, then `you> _` when the handoff is held. The card is unchanged.
- **The death countdown** (`DebriefScreen`, D2): after a non-overrun death the terminal asks `[1] again` / `[2] move on (I'll take this one)`; once those rows are typed, `restarting in 3`, `3 . 2`, `3 . 2 . 1` (a new number a second), then `choose(RETRY)`. It runs only with `auto_way_on` and `host.played` (a move / shot / dash / jump since the host's last swap; the ready-moment release counts), so a player who walked away stops at `you> _` after at most one restart. Any choose (1, 2, a click) stops it. Telemetry `choice=retry`, one attempt, as Retry.
- **Raw pause before the front door** (`PauseMenu`, D11): `PAUSED   [Esc] resume   [Q] quit game   [F] fullscreen   [-/+] volume   [L] less flashing` (keys by position) and a status row (`fullscreen off · volume 100% · less flashing off`). F toggles Settings' `window_mode`, -/+ (and the keypad's) move `vol_master` by 0.1, L toggles `reduce_flashing`; each commits at once; - / + also match the typed character (`volume_step()`: AZERTY's `-` on the 6 key; review). Q in a campaign session before the door quits the app (`Main.quit_target()` -> `QUIT_GAME`, `abandon` telemetry first); the v6 loop and from the door on keep "quit to start" / the title.
- **Rows from systems** (D16): `RuleBook.edit_landed` (reused) + `last_warned` / `last_by` -> `Main._on_edit_row`: `$ <cmd_text> ... ok`, else `$ patch <title> ... ok`; quiet cues and an event's own edits print nothing. New `EventPlayer.event_started(id)` -> the event's `cmd_text` only (S5: `$ set gravity_scale 0.9 ... ok`). New `SessionRunner.skin_cued(target)` -> `$ skin reveal <target> ... ok` (`bug` cues silent). Data: `cmd_text` on `RuleEdit` and `ScriptedEvent` (all 10 edits: `set walls.bounce on`, `set enemy.scale 1.8`, `set time_scale 0.6`, ...; `wrong_variable`). Never a raw id.
- **S9 recolour** (D15): `TerminalPanel` keeps colour tokens (`raw_colors()` = Palette's `TERM_*`; `pixel_colors()` = the Pixel look's roles `ui_text` / `accent` / `pickup` / `danger` plus its ramps' outline and wall); `dress()` follows the pause menu's rule (`Skins.is_on(&"ui_panel")`) on `Skins.hub.skin_changed` and at every session attach, and recolours the rows already shown and the back. Colours only (font, layout and frame stay raw for M48.5).
- **Telemetry** (2.8): a hosted live line (`_end_session`, `_menu_telemetry`) adds `"flow": "always_on"` and `"beat_real_s"` (real seconds from the debrief opening; 0 for the menu's lines). Same line, same moment, same `choice` values.
- **S7 data** (D9): door line 1 `"thinking: Before I build three shooters, this game needs a front door."`; the debrief's `"happy: Like the new chat? I redecorated."` cut; `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated. **`BUILD = "M47.5b"`.**
- **Tests**: `test_always_on` part 2 (+10, 34 in all): boot routing (copy -> raw column; booted -> nothing built, the host, the prompt flag; asked -> no prompt; door open -> title; legacy never shelled), `_enter_shell` ends a replay, the installer (rows, held before the first frame, `choose_2` saves yes, asked once), chapter mode (layouts, terminal hidden, rows after the card, then `you> _`), the countdown (after the rows, 3 . 2 . 1, one restart, one attempt, never twice), no countdown without input and `2` moves on, one telemetry line per attempt with `flow` / `beat_real_s` and otherwise the unhosted keys, quit before / after the door and the raw keys, edit / event / skin rows (quiet and event edits silent, no raw ids), the Pixel recolour at the `ui` reveal. **Listed deviations in existing tests:** test_front_door (`DOOR_LINES[0]`; :569's `said[0]` for the cut S7 line), test_pause_menu:152/155 (raw line text).
- **Deviations:** (1) **No clip and no 1920x1080 fullscreen capture**: stills only, at 1760x720 (the chapter layout at 1080p is covered by `layout_for` tests). (2) The first session after a boot is still not held (M47.5a deviation 4), so S7's chapter after a boot ends without `you> _` (after a swap it shows). (3) An event's own edits print nothing (the design names quiet edits only): S5's speed warning posing as gravity would otherwise print the gravity edit and give the accident away. (4) The raw pause adds a second status row (the design names only the key line). (5) `StartScreen.test_boot` / `enter_hook` and `GameHost.first_run_prompt` are test / hand-off statics.
- **Numbers:** hashes unchanged (unhosted): v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign 16/16 vs ref_m43.3, S4 4/4 vs ref_m42.2, S7 4/4 vs ref_m47, S8 arsenal / auto 2/2 vs ref_m47, survival 2/2 vs ref_m47_2, the same with `--look=pixel`, S9 x 3 paths replayed twice + `--skins=off` + `--look=pixel` vs ref_m48b, sheet exit 0; pause determinism (S4 + S8 x 3, 8 seeds, two menu patterns) 0 failures, its four base files byte-identical to M47.5a's. Hosted `--shell` (default and 1560x878): campaign 16/16, S4 4/4, S7 4/4, S8 x 3 2/2, S9 x 3 2/2 + `--skins=off`; `--handoff=120` S4 and S8 x 3 equal. Bot chains through the host: invincible (arsenal) and mortal (survival) `[botrun]` session lines byte-identical to M47.5a's (`real=` / `read≈` / `total≈` stripped; the invincible save path differs, its final save line is equal), 10 telemetry lines each. Owner backup + `save_m43_owner_shape.json`: the boot opens the title (door open), Continue reaches S7 in the host and ticks. Headless boots (`--inert-save`, `--autostart`, `--new-game --inert-save`): `start screen ... inert save ..., always on`, then `session s1_00_nothing (playable`. GUT: test_always_on 34/34; 31 affected files 663/663 (full suite not run). After the review: gates (`.check/m47_5b/gates.sh`) re-run, 66 hash runs + sheet, 0 failures, pause-determinism bases byte-identical to M47.5a's; test_always_on 39/39; `tools/check.ps1 -BotRuns` CHECK PASSED (full GUT 56 scripts, 1038/1038; real save folder unchanged; no check.ps1 / bot_runs expectation changed); `tools/export.ps1` EXPORT PASSED (the exported `--autostart` boot prints `start screen ... inert save, answers -, always on` then `session s1_00_nothing`; S4, S5, S7, S8 x 3, S9 x 3 boots clean).
- **Captures** (`.check/m47_5b/`, windowed 1760x720, inert save; script `capture_always_on.gd`): `boot_first_run_prompt`, `boot_s0_playing`, `s0_goal_lines`, `s0_thinking`, `s0_patch_apply`, `s0_hot_reload`, `s1_ready`, `s1_moving`, `s3_fork_asked`, `s3_fork_answered`, `s4_death_countdown`, `s4_restarted`, `s7_chapter_door`, `s7_chapter_later`, `s7_after_chapter`, `s9_start_raw`, `s9_ui_reveal_pixel_terminal`.
- **Real save incident (owner's call):** a first version of the boot-routing tests built StartScreens with `save_live` still on, so `_enter_shell()` made the real save live and a GUT run wrote `%APPDATA%/Godot/app_userdata/Patch Notes/save/save.json` (2026-11-25 22:27:47). Campaign progress unchanged (v0.6.0, S0-S5 done, next `s1_06_playground`, mascot blob); `settings.asked` became `false` and `vol_master: 1.0`, `window_mode: 0` were added (`fps_cap`, `reduce_flashing` untouched; `owner_backup_0120` holds neither key, so they were most likely absent). **Restore, once the owner agrees:** set `settings.asked` to `true` and remove `vol_master` and `window_mode`. Cause fixed (`_start_screen()` turns `save_live` off; `after_each` resets the statics); the file's timestamp has not moved since, through every GUT, gate, bot, check and export run. Since the review, `first_run_due()` also skips the installer for a save with any session done, so this save would not get the question at S6 either way.
- **Review (9 findings, 8 upheld, all fixed):** (1) `-- --new-game` reset the campaign on every return to the title in the same process (Quit to title, the campaign's end, a replay's end): `StartScreen.new_game_pending` is used up by the first boot; test `test_new_game_resets_once_per_process_not_on_every_return_to_the_title`. (2) The owner's real save is still altered: not touched here (owner's OK needed); the restore is recorded above, and `first_run_due()` (no prompt once a session is done) removes its symptom; test `test_the_installer_is_for_a_first_run_only_not_a_save_with_progress`. (3) Raw pause `-` ignored on AZERTY: `PauseMenu.volume_step()` also reads the typed character / logical key (`-`, `+`, `=`), position and keypad kept; test `test_the_raw_volume_keys_read_the_typed_character_on_azerty`. (4) The installer's answer pointed to Options, which do not exist before the door: `reduce flashing on|off ([Escape] then [L] to change)` from the layout labels (`GameHost.first_run_answer_line()`); test `test_the_installer_answer_points_at_the_raw_pause_keys_not_options`. (5) `test_enter_shell_ends_a_replay` was vacuous: the replay now begins after the screen's own `_ready` and is asserted on before `_enter_shell()` (fails with the `end_replay()` line removed). (6) The incident was not recorded: the entry above. (7) The export smoke was not run: run, EXPORT PASSED (Numbers). (8) No test pressed Q live: `test_q_in_a_live_hosted_session_before_the_door_quits_the_game_with_one_abandon_line` (a `_hosted_live` S4, `Main.leave_hook`, a real KEY_Q into the open raw pause: path `QUIT_GAME`, one `abandon` line with `flow`). Tests 1, 3 and 5 were checked failing with their fix reverted.
### 6.16 As built (M50.1a): packs and deep ranks (Arsenal depth, design 10)
*Built on the M47.4 tree in the `m50.1a` worktree, from `.check/arsenal_depth/design.md` (the M50.1a half of section 10; every default and open-question default taken). Fusion, FUSE, recipes and the misfuse are M50.1b. Tag `m50.1a` (not pushed).*
- **Packs** (`paths/arsenal/pack_meter.gd`, `paths/arsenal/booster_pack.gd`): `PathDef.pack_every` 6 (Arsenal; 0 = the old thresholds), `pack_taste` 2 (S7's taste deals 2 packs then stops), `rare_chance` 0.2, `rank_cap` (0 = none), `rank_curve` `classic` / `deep`. `RunState.pack_rng` (seeded `hash([seed, "pack"])`) gives **exactly 6 draws per pack** in a fixed order. Card 1 is held (G1, the most-fired card under `rank_cap`); card 2 is the queued give (G4), else a NEW card while the hand has room (G3), else any; card 3 any; no (id, rarity) twice (G2: with fewer ids, an id comes back at its other rarity, else the pack is shorter). A held card ranks +1 (rare +2); a new one joins at rank 1 (rare 3). Rare cards have a gold `card_rare` border (Palette tier 0 `CARD_RARE`; the Pixel atlas is unchanged, so the look falls back to tier 0's gold).
- **Hand of 2** (`arsenal.tres` `max_slots` 2): a NEW card on a full hand opens the replace step ("Replace Pistol 7" per slot, then "Back to the pack" on key 3; Back returns to the same pack). `Loadout.replace_slot` keeps the slot index and the active slot. `PathRunner.give(w, rank)` on a full pack hand queues the weapon as card 2 of the next packs (G4, up to `GIVE_PACKS` 3, logged `give_queued`; rank >= 3 = rare); before the `cards` basic the old replace applies (`give_replaced`). After a block of packs, the next opens `PACK_GAP` 2 s later.
- **Deep ranks** (`weapons/weapon_curve.gd`, `WeaponCurve`): odd ranks raise damage x1 / 1.5 / 2 / 3 / 4 / 6 (ranks 1-11), then +1 per odd rank; ranks 2 / 4 / 6 unlock the weapon's mechanics A / B / C (`WeaponDef.mechanics`, `mechanic_values`); even ranks from 8 strengthen them in turn, 3 steps each, then +10% overflow (`WeaponTrait.value_at`). `breakpoint_text` names what a rank one-shots against the Stage 2 roster (e.g. "one-shots chasers and lancers, tank in 4"). `Loadout.rank_cap` / `curve` from the path; `upgrade(id, by)`. **The classic curve is unchanged** (Auto, Survival, v6: every hash gate equal).
- **Mechanics:** pistol heavy_round / seeker / **chain** (on a kill the shot re-aims at the nearest live enemy within 5 T, 3-6 hops; `ProjectileMod.after_hit` -> keep flying); shotgun knockback_cone / point_blank / **shrapnel** (a pellet that hits nothing drops a caltrop, 1 damage, cap 30); laser burn_line (deep cap 12 zones) / ricochet / **overcharge** (every 4th-2nd shot also fires a wall-to-wall beam, 0.5x damage per 0.25 s, one at a time). Each leveled trait's step 1 is today's constant.
- **HUD:** `HandWidget.ROWS` 2, a rank badge and mechanic dots per card ("[1] Pistol 5 ●●○", "[1] Pistol 7 ●●●"), the meter reads "pack in n".
- **Data:** S7 at 17 "Six kills, I'll deal you a pack." and `picked 1` "See? Same card, stronger gun."; S8 at 10 "Remember the cards? Now they come in packs. Six kills, one pack, take one card.", new at 16 and at 34 lines (Arsenal only, appended so cue indices do not move); S9 `answer:path_arsenal at 11: give shotgun` + "Your shotgun's back. I drew it a card." `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **Bots / hashes:** `BotRuns.pack_choice` (static, no RNG; rare held card, NEW with room, never NEW on a full hand (Back), else the most-fired held card) is shared with `tools/run_hash.gd`. Probes `no_fuse` (no-op until M50.1b), `skip_new`, `human_pace` (pack_every 9). `--loadout` ranks up to 99. Per-run `packs= pack_gap_median= rares= replaces= backs= max_rank= hand=`; the `[botrun] arsenal:` line reads the pack band. `CampaignDB.path_record` adds `packs`, `pack_picks`, `ranks_end`, `max_rank`.
- **Deviations:** (1) the pick-panel `PICK_LINES` are muted on a pack path (a line per pack every ~5 s would flood the mascot). (2) caltrop, beam and chain arc are code-drawn in the `player_shot` role (no new Pixel grids; the atlas hash stays M48a's). (3) Overcharge fires the beam in addition to the shot. (4) The burn-line zone cap applies on the deep curve only. (5) Bot rule 3 (recipe pairs) is M50.1b. (6) With a 2-weapon pool and a full hand, G2 makes card 3 the other rarity of a shown id, so almost every S8 pack holds a rare pistol card (bot: rares = packs in S8) and ranks climb past 30 by 120 s; per design, the tuning order (rare_chance 0.15, x6 -> x5, pack_every 7) only starts if Arsenal beats both other paths by more than 3 on S9.
- **Edited tests:** test_arsenal (2 slots, packs, deep text, give queueing, "pack in 6"), test_hud_layout (2 cards + rank badge), test_s7_tryout (2 packs at 6 / 12 kills; the picked line), test_s8_day_one (audit row, pack line), test_front_door (reads `tests/gut/ui_measure_m50_1a.json`: M47.3's measurements with the hand of 2 re-recorded, width 256). New: `tests/gut/test_arsenal_depth.gd` (30).
- **Review (6 findings, 5 upheld, all fixed):** (1) a chain shot hopped off a corpse (an enemy killed by something else that frame, still in the space): `Projectile` now runs `after_hit` only when this shot's damage landed (`test_chain_never_hops_off_a_corpse`, fails without the fix); (2) test_front_door expected M47.3's 3-card `hand_rect`: re-recorded in `ui_measure_m50_1a.json`; (3) `WeaponCurve.ROSTER` was a second enemy list (test_enemy_db): the breakpoint text reads `EnemyDB.READING` and Tuning `<id>_hp` (`WeaponCurve.roster_hp`); (4) the full suite is now run and recorded below; (5) the HUD example read "Pistol 7 ●●○" (rank 7 has all three: ●●●).
- **`BUILD = "M50.1a"`.**
- **Numbers:** GUT test_arsenal_depth 29/29, test_arsenal 36/36, test_paths 41/41, test_hud_layout 10/10, test_mascot 11/11, test_s7_tryout 29/29, test_s8_day_one 21/21, test_s9_paint 22/22, test_survival 23/23, test_pixel_skin 30/30, test_pause_menu 13/13, test_weapons 36/36, test_campaign 48/48, test_readability 19/19, test_run_state 15/15, test_playtest_build 8/8, test_boot 5/5; after the review, test_arsenal_depth 30/30, test_front_door 26/26, test_enemy_db 6/6 and the **full suite: Scripts 56, Tests 1029, all passed** (233 s). `tools/check.ps1 -BotRuns`: CHECK PASSED with no edit (no Arsenal expectation changed: S7 `card=2` and the swap seconds, S8 120 s and S9 150 s wins all hold). Hash gates (`.check/m50_1a/gates.sh`), all 0 failures: v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign 16/16 vs ref_m43.3, S4 4/4 vs ref_m42.2, S8 auto / survival 2/2 vs ref_m47 / ref_m47_2, S9 auto / survival replayed twice, `--skins=off`, `--look=pixel` vs ref_m48b, the pixel variants, silhouette sheet exit 0; pause determinism S8 x 3 paths and S4, 8 seeds, both patterns. **New refs** `.check/ref_m50_1a/` (S7 4 seeds, S8 / S9 Arsenal 2 seeds), each replayed twice with 0 failures (plus `--look=pixel`, `--skins=off`); re-recorded after the review fixes, byte-identical (no seed hits a corpse). `bot_s7_auto_stub` line identical to M48b.
