### 6.17 As built (M50.1b): fusion (Arsenal depth, design 10)
*Built on M50.1a in the `m50.1a` worktree, from `.check/arsenal_depth/design.md` (the M50.1b half of section 10; every default and open-question default taken) and the owner's fusion decisions (a choice, hand of 2, both at rank 4, fused rank = ceil of the average, keeps ranking up, a failed-fusion beat; speed over ceremony). Not committed.*
- **Recipes** (`weapons/fusions/*.tres`, `WeaponDef.parents` / `inherits` / `built_in`): `WeaponDB.fusions()`, `recipe(a, b)` (either order), `fusion_built(id)`, `fusion_problems()` (an unbuilt row reports "not built yet (<where>)"; a built one must cover its 4 inherits, which must be the parents' rank-2 and rank-4 mechanics). `all()` / `ids()` / `problems()` stay the base weapons. **Prism** (shotgun + laser) is built: aimed, 5 beams, spread 18, pierce 2; mechanics knockback_cone, burn_line_prism (centre beam only), point_blank, ricochet. Cluster (M50), Satellite (S11), Pulse / Horizon (Auto depth) and Black Moon (S11 + Auto depth) are rows only.
- **Fused levels:** `WeaponCurve.levels_of(def, rank)`: a fusion has all 4 mechanics from rank 1 and strengthens from rank 6 (rank 5 [1,1,1,1], 7 [2,1,1,1], 9 [2,2,1,1]); damage follows the deep curve.
- **FUSE** (`BoosterPack.fuse_option`, `FUSE_RANK` 4, needs the `fusion` basic): a pack shows a 4th option "FUSE: Laser 4 + Shotgun 5 -> Prism 5" with what it keeps and what it loses (the parents' rank-6+ mechanics). It is free: `Loadout.fuse` puts the fused card in the active parent's slot (else the lower), frees the other and sums the shots; the panel shows "Fusing... Prism 5" for 0.4 s (`PickPanel.FUSE_TIME`, real time, the game stays paused) and the **same pack** reopens with its cards recomputed for the new hand (`BoosterPack.recompute`: rarities kept, a parent's card folds into the fusion, duplicates dropped, nothing drawn from `pack_rng`). A parent's card later feeds the fusion (`fold`, `Loadout.holder`). G1 steers card 1 to the lower half of a recipe pair below 4. The recipe is learned (`SaveGame.learn_recipe`, a sparse `campaign.recipes` key, known fusion ids only; a runtime patch note "New recipe: Prism") and listed in the pause menu ("Recipes: Prism (shotgun + laser)").
- **The link** (`HandWidget`): between the 2 cards of a built recipe pair, dim with a "4" until both reach 4, then lit and pulsing (steady under reduce_flashing); hidden before the `fusion` basic and for any other pair. A fused card draws in a second tone with its parents' names.
- **The misfuse** (`PathRunner.fuse_fail`, cue `fuse fail`): an overlay only, the Loadout untouched. The hand shows one wobbling "Pistgun?" card; every other shot leaves left / right / back (`Loadout.MISFUSE_TURNS`) in the `shot_misfuse_player` skin; switching is locked and no pack opens; after 6 s of game time it ends with the patch note "Fixed: cards fusing into a gun with guns on it." Skipped (logged `fuse_fail_skipped`) with fewer than 2 cards.
- **Grammar / data:** cue action `fuse fail`, whens `fused N` and `fuse_ready N` (held `SAY_GAP` 4 s after the previous say cue), line conditions `fused` / `misfused` (from the run log). S9 on Arsenal: the shared "at 80: give laser" and its line became `!answer:path_arsenal` (Auto / Survival unchanged); appended Arsenal cues at 78 (the trick), 79 `fuse fail`, 82 / 86 (misfused lines), 86 `give laser 3` (gold card; 90 before the review), 94 `add fusion`, `fuse_ready 1`, `fused 1`; a debrief line on `answer:path_arsenal&fused`. `arsenal.tres` basics + `fusion`; `Unlocks.CAMPAIGN_BASICS` + `fusion`. F2 shows a FUSIONS row (unbuilt ones disabled). `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **Bot:** `BotRuns.pack_choice` (1) FUSE when offered (not with `no_fuse`), (2) the lower recipe half, (3) on a full hand a NEW card that makes a recipe, replacing the non-partner. `BotInput` lets a fused card answer for its parents. Per run `fusions= misfuse=`; a `[botrun] fusion:` line.
- **Deviations:** (1) two new Pixel grids (`shot_prism_player`, `shot_misfuse_player`): the atlas digest moved (test_pixel_skin updated). (2) A fusion's traits are its `mechanics` list (no separate field); inherits are covered by name prefix (`burn_line_prism` covers `burn_line`). (3) Recompute drops duplicate (id, rarity) cards, so a pack after FUSE can be shorter. (4) Misfuse shots neither spin nor squeak. (5) The 82 / 86 lines use the `misfused` condition (silent if skipped). (6) G1 and bot rules (2) / (3) do not wait for the `fusion` basic. (7) On S9 the laser gift lands on a full hand: in the captures it took the pistol's slot (Pistol 17 gone), which is what makes the recipe pair; since the review the replace step only lets the pistol go (below).
- **Tests:** new `tests/gut/test_fusion.gd` (17); edited test_arsenal (basics), test_arsenal_depth (bot rule 3), test_s9_paint (Arsenal's laser at 90), test_pixel_skin (digest). GUT: test_fusion 17/17, test_arsenal 36/36, test_arsenal_depth 30/30, test_paths 41/41, test_hud_layout 10/10, test_mascot 11/11, test_s7_tryout 29/29, test_s8_day_one 21/21, test_s9_paint 22/22, test_survival 23/23, test_pixel_skin 30/30, test_pause_menu 13/13, test_weapons 36/36, test_campaign 48/48, test_save 27/27, test_settings 19/19, test_front_door 26/26, test_enemy_db 6/6, test_reactions 24/24, test_basics_added 21/21, test_auto_scale 21/21, test_answers 19/19, test_playtest_build 8/8, test_boot 5/5 (full suite not run).
- **Hash gates** (`.check/m50_1b/gates.sh`), all 0 failures: v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign 16/16 vs ref_m43.3 (and `--look=pixel`), S4 4/4 vs ref_m42.2, S8 Auto / Survival vs ref_m47 / ref_m47_2, S9 Auto / Survival vs ref_m48b replayed twice + `--skins=off` + `--look=pixel`, silhouette sheet exit 0, pause determinism S8 x 3 paths and S4 (8 seeds, both patterns). Against M50.1a: S7 4/4 and S8 Arsenal 2/2 equal, S9 Arsenal 2/2 differ (the fusion beats). New refs `.check/ref_m50_1b/` (S7, S8 / S9 Arsenal), each replayed twice with 0 failures (plus `--look=pixel`, and `--skins=off` on S9).
- **Bots (seeds 1-8):** S8 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won (packs 18-20, max rank 31-39), mortal 2/8 deaths (as M50.1a). S9 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won, fused 8/8 (median 113 s, 103-128 s), misfuse restored 8/8, Prism ends at rank 11-23. **S9 mortal deaths: Arsenal 3/8** (M48b 8/8, M50.1a 0/8; deaths at 120-148 s, all after the fusion), Auto 5/8, Survival 1/8 (unchanged). Logs `.check/m50_1b/bot_*.log`.
- **Captures** (`.check/m50_1b/capture.gd`, windowed, inert save, `--look=pixel`, S9 Arsenal seed 1, invincible bot): `pack_screen.png`, `hand_of_2.png`, `high_rank_pistol.png` (Pistol 11), `misfuse.png` / `misfuse_shots.png` (the tilted "Pistgun?" card with glitch bars; pistol shots leaving sideways), `fuse_option.png` (the 4th option framed, keeps listed, the lit link dot between Laser 4 and Shotgun 5), `fusing.png` ("Fusing... Prism 5"), `after_fuse.png` (the same pack, Prism cards, "Pistol 1 NEW joins your hand", Hand 1/2), `prism_firing.png` (Prism 7, ricochet lines), `high_rank_prism.png` (Prism 21).
- **`BUILD = "M50.1b"`.**
- **Review (7 findings, 5 upheld, all fixed):** (1) *S9's timing target was missed* (invincible median 113.5 s on 16 seeds against <= 110 s; human_pace was never run). The design's fallback is applied: Arsenal's `give laser 3` and its line move from 90 to 86 s, and the 11 s shotgun is now a gold card (`give shotgun 3`, "I drew it a gold card"). After the change, 16 seeds: invincible fused 16/16 with a median of 104.6 s (97.6-115.2); `human_pace` fused 16/16 with a median of 112.8 s. S9 Arsenal ref re-recorded. (2) *The tutorial dead-ended for a human who kept the high-rank pistol.* `BoosterPack.mark_keeps` now gives a NEW card a `keep` (the held card it forms a built recipe with). This applies on a full hand that is not already a recipe pair, when exactly one card links, and on a path whose basics hold `fusion`. In the replace step that card reads "Keep Shotgun 5: it links with the Laser", is framed lit and disabled, and only the other card can go. The new bot probe `--probe=human` (human_pace plus "replace the lowest rank the step allows") fused 16/16, median 112.8 s. (3) *A stale `fuse_ready` line could play after the fusion.* `Cue.expired` drops `fuse_ready n` once n fusions are made, and SessionRunner checks it before `due`. (4) *A skippable test branch.* test_fuse_reopens now forces the pack (Pistol NEW, Shotgun, rare Laser) and always asserts the folded cards and the NEW pistol filling the freed slot. (5) *Validation.* `SessionDef.problems()`: `fused` / `fuse_ready` need the `fusion` basic, `fuse` needs the `cards` basic, a fusion cue in a path session must be Arsenal-only, and an intro line cannot read `fused` / `misfused` (`reads_fusion`). New negative grammar cases. Also fixed: test_prism_burns_only_on_the_centre_beam leaked the centre beam's deferred burn zone at exit, which made check.ps1 fail on "6 resources still in use". New tests: test_the_replace_step_keeps_the_new_cards_recipe_partner, test_mark_keeps_only_for_one_built_partner_on_a_full_non_pair_hand, test_a_fuse_ready_line_is_dropped_once_the_pair_was_fused, test_session_problems_gate_the_fusion_cues_and_lines (test_fusion 21/21). After the review: mortal S9 (8 seeds) Arsenal 3/8 deaths (34.8, 94.8, 111.7 s; fused 6/8), Auto 5/8, Survival 1/8. Full GUT 1050/1050; all gates 0 failures; check.ps1 -BotRuns passes with no expectation changes. Two earlier attempts failed only on the real-save guard: a Godot session outside this worktree wrote the save while they ran.

### 6.18 As built (merge): M50.1a/b (Arsenal depth) into always-on (M47.5a/b)
*The two were built in parallel on 47260c3 (M47.4): always-on on `main`, Arsenal depth in the `m50.1a` worktree. Merged with `git merge --no-ff refs/heads/m50.1a` (a tag `m50.1a` shares the name; the bare name resolves to the tag, M50.1a only). Gates: `.check/m50_merge/gates.sh`.*
- **Text conflicts:** this As-built (M50.1a/b were 6.14/6.15 on their branch, now 6.16/6.17); `main.gd` (`BUILD = "M50.1b"`, M47.5b's `QUIT_GAME` kept); `ui/pause_menu.gd` (M47.5b's raw-line settings and `_raw_status` plus M50.1b's `Recipes` label, both kept); `campaign/sessions/s2_01_tryout.tres` (M50.1a's pack cues, M47.5b's debrief without "Like the new chat?"). `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated with `tools/lines_file.gd`.
- **Semantic fixes:** (1) the terminal beat prints `DebriefScreen.changelog()`, which M50.1b had not taught the run's `patch_note` lines (only `notes_text()`), so the misfuse fix and "New recipe: Prism" never reached the always-on terminal: `changelog()` now appends them after the session's notes, and "Fixed: ..." files under Fixed (test_fusion asserts it, and the terminal row `  - Fixed cards fusing ...`). (2) `tools/check.ps1`'s hosted S8 check compares Arsenal with `.check/ref_m50_1b` instead of `ref_m47`. Fusion and fuse_ready lines need no change: they go through `Mascot.say`, whose `line_started` the hosted terminal shows while the bubble is off; the pack panel stays in the game column.
- **Refs:** S7, S8 Arsenal and S9 Arsenal compare with `ref_m50_1b` (copied from the worktree with `ref_m50_1a`), hosted (`--shell`, both view sizes, handoff 120) too; every other ref unchanged.

### 6.19 As built (M47.6a)
The play gate and the voice, per design `.check/stage1_rework/design.md` section 6 (rev. 2) and 0.8.

**Built:** `ChatInput` (the terminal's `you>` input: `play` any time, the empty Enter on the ghost and a bare `p` armed 0.4 s after the gate's lines on a fresh press, movement keys swallowed at an empty prompt and pulsing the ghost, Esc handed on as `pause_pressed`); `GameView` (no key reaches the game while the chat is focused, the pause key excepted); the gate in `GameHost` (every live start incl. the first after a boot or Continue, `before_play` lines typed, canned replies counted never logged, a dim "(type play)" after 10 s from S2 on, `--autostart` = `auto_play`); `before_play` / `end_line` on `SessionDef` with the 2-row bubble lint; the `tried` cue (S0's "oh. it wants to go."); `before_play_default` and `chat_replies` on the campaign; the mascot bubble back on during hosted play (each line logged dim and instant in the terminal, off at report-ready, clamped to 2 rows); `MoveKeys` (layout-aware keycaps bottom-left, lit by input, S1 only for 6 s); `StrainFX` (lean, stretch and a 1 px shake toward a key the square cannot follow; presentation only); the death countdown removed (Enter armed, `1` again at once, `2` move on); the pause menu's handoff refusal lifted at the gate.

**Deviations:** tool hosts do not set `auto_play` (a static set from a factory would leak into later hosts; run_hash already calls `skip_handoff`); S0 / S1 debriefs rewritten and S3 gains a won line (S3's "I can talk now" left, the chat says it); the S8 Survival "guns come in crates" cue and the Arsenal draft's "Remember the cards" cue shortened to pass the 2-row lint; an `again` canned reply added; one `before_play_default` (the capitalised post-upgrade one is M47.6b); no quick-reply pills yet (M47.6b); `play_wait_s` / `chat_typed` kept on the host, into telemetry in M47.6c1; the body pick move (Q9) left for M47.6b; StrainFX is a child of the player and writes only `Visual`; the mascot's typing pace still follows the full line (a clamped bubble shows all at once).

**Numbers:** GUT on the affected files all green (34 files incl. the new `test_play_gate` 18/18, `test_always_on` 39, `test_campaign` 48); every hash ref equal (`.check/m47_6a/gates.sh`: g0 exit 0, all campaign / shell / viewport / handoff runs 0 failures); the bot chain's `[botrun]` lines equal to the pre-change baseline apart from `said=` (S0 4 -> 3, S1 3 -> 2) and S0 `cues=1 -> 2` (the new `tried` cue).

**Windowed:** `.check/m47_6a/*.png` (installer with the strain, the S0 gate, the four strains, S0 in play, the S1 gate and keys lit, the S4 death ask waiting with no countdown).

**Review (6 findings, 5 upheld, all fixed):** (1) "again" typed on QWERTY lost its `a` (move_left, swallowed at the empty prompt) and got the fallback reply: ChatInput keeps the last swallowed key's character (`typed_lead`) and GameHost retries the reply with it when the text alone gets the fallback, so "you> again" and the again reply (`test_again_typed_on_qwerty_gets_the_again_reply`). (2) Two `you>` prompts at the installer question and the death ask: while an ask's `you> _` row waits the input row is hidden, its space kept (a gate always shows it) (`test_one_you_prompt_while_an_ask_waits`). (3, 4) Pause-menu Restart at a play gate skipped the next gate and counted a never-played attempt: `next_session` carries the play only when it was given (`not held`), and `menu_restart` at a gate writes no `menu_retry` line and counts no attempt (`test_restart_from_the_gate_gates_again_and_counts_no_attempt`, which also checks a retry after play still carries it). All three tests fail without their fix. (5) The full gates: `check.ps1 -BotRuns` CHECK PASSED with GUT 1110/1110 (59 scripts), the hash gates re-run as `.check/m47_6a_fix/gates.sh` (g0 exit 0, 70 summaries 0 failures, no ref changed), a PLAYTEST.md entry added. No bot expectation changed.

### 6.20 As built (M47.6b)
The dev desktop and the chat upgrade, per design `.check/stage1_rework/design.md` section 6 (rev. 2), 2.1, 2.8 and the owner's answers (section 10).

**Built:** `DevDesktop` (code-drawn: the dark desktop with its 32 px grid, the game window with its title bar "Patch Notes - vX (debug)" and status bar (FPS, nodes, "S4 hits: ON", 1280x720), the chat window ("dev-chat", after the upgrade the chat app's rounded frame "dev-chat - Blob"), the taskbar with its two tabs and "build: vX  hh:mm"; no real product names; mouse ignored; hidden in chapter mode); `GameHost.BASE` 1824x848 with `TITLE_H` / `STATUS_H` / `TASKBAR_H` / `MARGIN` and `layout_for()` returning `game_window` / `chat_window` / `taskbar` (k = min((H - 128) / 720, (W - 408) / 1280), the 2D rect still 1280x720 at every k); `ChatPanel` (tabs with "terminal (legacy)" collapsed, the avatar header named for the body, the `build.log - tail -f` strip for `$` rows, AI / you bubbles, dividers, a patch card with ADDED / CHANGED / FIXED tags, the thinking dots, quick-reply pills, the rounded input with a send button); `TerminalPanel.style` RAW / CHAT (one way, `set_style` / `upgrade()` / `upgraded`, the chat as its renderer so every caller and `rows()` stay as they were); `CampaignDB.chat_style()` derived from the save (S2's id in `done`, or an index past it, so the M42.2 fixture opens on the chat); `SessionDef.chat = &"upgrade"` on S2 with its lint (one upgrade session); S2's beat: "also this window is ugly.", the body pick (`fork_mascot` moved from S3, owner answer Q9), the patch rows, "one sec.", `build dev_chat_ui`, `retire terminal`, the crossfade (0.3 s), "New window. Round corners. Proud of them."; `CampaignDB.late_forks()` (a save that finished S2 before the move gets the body at its next beat); the chat's pills for a fork, a death ask and `play` at a gate (keys 1-3 still work); capitalised gate lines and canned replies after the upgrade (`before_play_default_chat`, `chat_replies_chat`); the chat placeholder "Talk to Blob..."; Palette `DESK_*` / `CHAT_*` tokens (pixel colours derived at the ui reveal) and `UiTheme.sans_font()`.

**Deviations:** the chat is a renderer inside `TerminalPanel` (the design's fallback), not a separate panel object: `main.gd` / `DebriefScreen` keep their wiring and `rows()` keeps the terminal's strings; the 1080p k is 1.18125 (the width bounds it: 1920 - 48 - 360 = 1512), not the design's "about 1.33"; S3 now has `face = true` and the reactions' quiet-until-first-kill rule keys on the session's place (through S3, `Reactions.QUIET_UNTIL_KILL_THROUGH`) instead of `face`, so S3's reactions stay as before; the bot picks the body at S2 from S2's seed (chain 1i: window, was blob; the mortal chain: its own) and keeps the dash forks' divisors (`FORK_DIV`), so every S3-S6 dash check holds; check.ps1's S3 pick check is split into an S2 body check and an S3 dash check; the earlier terminal rows are not copied into the chat (collapsed under the legacy tab); the `collapsed, not deleted` note and the header lines clip at the 360 px column.

**Numbers:** GUT on the affected files all green (new `test_dev_chat` 17/17, `test_always_on` 40, `test_campaign` 48, `test_play_gate` 21, 35 files in all); every hash ref equal (`.check/m47_6b/gates.sh`: g0 exit 0, 72 campaign / v6 / pixel / shell / viewport / handoff / pause-determinism summaries 0 failures, no ref recorded or changed); the invincible chain's `[botrun]` lines equal to M47.6a's apart from S2 `pick=mascot_window` and S3 `pick=dash_grapple,dashdir_aim` (was `mascot_blob,dash_grapple,dashdir_aim`) and the save's mascot (window); S3 `said=` / `reactions=` unchanged; the mortal chain 0 failures.

**Windowed:** `.check/m47_6b/*.png` (the installer and the S0 gate on the desktop, the S2 body fork in the terminal, the upgrade in three frames, the S3 gate with the `play` pill, S3 in play, an S4 fork as pills, an S4 death ask as pills, S7's chapter with no desktop, S4's gate at 1920x1080). Two capture findings fixed with a test: the chat overflowed its 360 px window at 1080p (`test_the_chat_fits_its_window_at_1080p`, fails without the fix), and a square focus ring showed inside the rounded input.

**Review (4 findings, 3 upheld, all fixed):** (1) in the chat a fork's descriptions showed only inside one-line pills that cut them off (S3 dash, S4 enemy, S7 path fork): each option with a description now also posts a wrapped card in the chat ("[n] name" over its full description, a click picks it), and the pills keep only "[n] name" (`ChatPanel.add_option`, `TerminalPanel.pill_label`; `test_a_forks_full_descriptions_show_in_the_chat_at_1080p`, fails without the cards); (2) `tools/campaign_shots.gd` still asked the body at S3: it now shoots S2's body fork and pick, then S3's corner, dash, dash direction and notes, and fails loudly on a wrong fork; (3) docs: a PLAYTEST.md entry for M47.6b and an "M47.6b defaults" bullet in section 9. ### 6.21 As built (M47.6c1)
Stage 1's endings, per design `.check/stage1_rework/design.md` section 6 (rev. 2), 2.3, 2.6, 3 and the owner's answers (section 10: Q1 S5 raises only the new enemy, Q2 Enter on the ghost `play`, Q9 the body pick at S2's end, built in M47.6b).

**Built:** `SessionDef` goals `clear` and `script` (`ENDINGS`), `ends` (the kinds that end a session, the goal first), `end_after` (a script ending's quiet tail, game seconds), `enemy_total` (the spawner's budget, -1 none), `end_kinds()` / `has_end()` / `cue_spawn_total()`, and their lint (valid, unique ends holding the goal; `clear` needs `enemy_total >= 0`; `enemy_total` is for `clear` only; `end_line` needs a script; `time_limit` is now for kills and clear; a `kills N` spawn cue past the set can never fire); `spawn_weights` built (keys an archetype or `fork:<id>`, summing below 1; the rest of the roll stretched over today's chances); `Spawner.budget` / `spawned` / `budget_spent()` (cue spawns never count); `RunLoop.set_endings()`, `wave_closed()` (budget spent and no `at` / `kills` spawn cue to come), `enemies_left()` (alive + entering the tree), `cleared()`, `script_done()` (every `at` cue fired, no event holding the goal, the rule book idle), `quiet_tail()` (the spawner rests once a budgeted session's script window opens), `end_reason` (cleared / script / limit / died / won / stopped) and the record's `end`, `enemy_total`, `spawned`; a clear set reached under a held goal waits like a kills goal (`goal_waited`); a clear session's `time_limit` is a hidden cap (`limit`, stopped); `RunState.wave_closed` and the latched `survivors_chase` (3 or fewer left of a closed set: a shooter drops its kite range and comes at you; chasers, lancers and bombers already do); the bot hunts the last ones once the set is closed (`hits` in); `SessionRunner.pending_spawns()` / `script_cues_done()`; `Arena.grow_layout()` (S4's walls grow middle-out over 2.5 s of game time, one collision per block on its tick, bodies pushed to the nearest free tile, the reach map cleared per block, a 0.2 s scale-in or a fade under reduce flashing); reactions `wave_done` ("That's all of them. Clean up.") and `last_few n` (two left, the last one), Stage 1 only (S3-S6); `session_half` reads the fixed set; no campaign HUD shows goal text (`RunState.session != null`); `end_line` said when the script window opens; the hosted telemetry line gains `play_wait_s`, `chat_typed`, `chat_style`; `[botrun]` lines end with `end=<reason> spawned=<n>/<budget> start=<x>,<y>`; `run_hash --campaign` plays S0-S6 and fails loudly on a clear session still playing at its last frame.

**The sessions:** S0 and S1 end 5 s after their script (S0 about 12 s, S1 about 11 s; one line added to each so the script runs past the old stop). S3 clears 3 (two at 0.5 s, the third on the second kill; hidden cap 120 s). S4 clears 14 (`level` is the 0.8 s cue, the walls grow in, enemies from 3.5 s, the dash line a 5 s cue after the walls; the body lines and "I made you a level. Type play." in the chat before play; cap 90 s). S5 keeps rate 0.8 (owner Q1); the new enemy takes 40 % of the crowd's draws (`spawn_weights {"fork:fork_enemy": 0.4}`) plus two from cues at 3, 8, 13 and 18 s; it clears 10 + 8 or ends 4.5 s after the gravity (`ends [clear, script]`, "Okay. That's the test."; cap 120 s). S6 clears 28, the story re-keyed (gift at 8 s, big enemies at 10 kills, the flip and slow-mo at 18; cap 180 s).

**Deviations:** refs under `ref_m47_6c1` (design: `ref_m47_6c`); S5 per the owner's Q1 instead of the design's rate 2.0 / `enemy_total` 24 (10 measured: every seed spends it before 20 s); only the shooter needed the chase change; two `last_few` reactions (two left, last one) instead of one; the three new reactions stop after S6 so Stage 2's reaction lists and hashes stay; S3's intro capitalised; the `start=` printed is the arena's `player_start` until M47.6c2 carries it.

**Numbers:** GUT on the affected files all green (new `test_endings` 14/14, `test_campaign` 48, `test_events` 38, `test_dash_styles` 21, `test_reactions` 24, `test_s8_day_one` 21, `test_stage2_door` 21; 61 files run, 0 failing). Good enough."); S6 under the flip and its cleared debrief ("That's all of them. Clean up." in the chat). No goal text in any frame.

### 6.22 As built (M47.6c2)
No reload between Stage 1 sessions, per design `.check/stage1_rework/design.md` section 6 (rev. 2) and 2.7, 2.9, 2.10.

**Built:** `Main.next_start` / `next_lead` (static hand-offs like `next_seed`, `NO_START` = INF, consumed and reset in `_ready`; `Main.clear_start()` on the pause menu's Restart, Restart / F5 (`start_new_build`), the title or cooking screen (`_leave_to`) and every retry); `GameHost.carry_ok(from, to, outcome)` (both Stage 1, the same arena, no death, no stage card) and `GameHost.capture_start(main, next)` (the one capture path: the player snapped to 0.01 px, or `NO_START`), called by `Main._end_session` on Next / Move on and by `run_hash --chain`; the genre places the player at the carried spot (`Genre.carried_start`, `TopdownGenre.start_for`) and the camera at the view it had (`CameraRig.carry_lead`, presentation); `GameHost.next_session` swaps a carried session on the next frame with no glitch (`seamless`); `GameHost.clear_field` at the beat of a carried session (`Main._on_report_ready`): the old frozen instance's enemies, shots and pickups pop out at their screen points (`Puffs`, 0.3 s rings; reduce flashing: fading dots) and the player's HP refills; `RunLoop.start_point` and the run record's `start` [x, y], copied to the campaign line (`start`, additive); `[botrun]` prints the real start; `run_hash --start=x,y` (a carried start replayed cold) and `--chain` (S0-S6 in one tool host per seed, the carry on, its own loop, one line per session with its `start`); check.ps1: the invincible chain's S3 must start off `player_start`, and the chain ref compare (`hash_chain`, 14 runs) when `.check/ref_m47_6c2/` exists.

**Deviations:** the chain ref is `ref_m47_6c2/chain_hash.jsonl` (design: `ref_m47_6c/`); a carried spot hugging a wall (closer than half a tile to a block, common at S5's end under the gravity: 2 of 6 carries in the first mortal chain fell back) moves to the nearest free tile centre (`Arena.free_tile_near`, the walls' own push-out) instead of `player_start`, which stays for a spot outside the arena or with no reachable free tile (owner rule "no map reload" over the design's fallback); the facing is not carried (it is sim state, the dash direction at rest, and `--start` replays the position only), so the gun points right at the next gate; the camera carries its whole view offset (lookahead, smoothing and limits), not only the lookahead, else the room shifted by about 170 px (seen in the first capture); `next_start` needs no reset on a `--session` jump (the static is fresh at boot); the tool chain answers and jumps the save per session as a cold run does, so a chained session differs from its cold run by its start only.

**Numbers:** GUT on the affected files all green: 31 files, 640/640, then after the last fixes `test_carry` 10/10 with `test_always_on`, `test_play_gate`, `test_endings`, `test_campaign`, `test_dev_chat` (153/153). `test_carry`: the rule, the 0.01 capture reading back as its printed text, a cold start, a carried start consumed and in the record, a walled spot moved to a free tile and an outside one to `player_start`, the live Next flow (leftovers popped, HP refilled, the host loop's start equals the tool loop's capture, a fresh instance there, no glitch), the glitch only without a carry, a death and a retry start cold, every reset. Bot chains (bot numbers): invincible 1i, the same outcomes as M47.6c1 (S0 12.0, S1 11.0, S2 stopped 45.0, S3 won 12.5 (3), S4 won 32.0 (14/14), S5 stopped 43.5 by script (16 kills), S6 won 41.0 (28/28)), starts carried from S2 on (S2 1936.05,287.07; S3 2447.99,580.15; S4 3027.43,639.69; S5 1329.58,541.87; S6 243.48,923.37), S7-S9 outcomes and times unchanged (152.0 / 120.0 / 150.0, cold starts); mortal 2: S2 died (its way on: S3 starts cold), S3-S6 won, S5 started on a free tile (2040.00,552.00) after a wall-hugging S4 end; every check.ps1 campaign regex matches the new log. Gates (`.check/m47_6c2/gates.sh`, then `gates_chain.sh` after the capture fix): 75 runhash summaries 0 failures and the render sheet exit 0 with no ref changed (v6, gauntlet, `ref_m47_6c1` campaign S0-S6 x 4 and S4 x 4 replayed twice, pixel, `--shell` at both views, `--handoff=120`, S7 vs `ref_m50_1b` / `ref_m50_1a`, S8 x 3, S9 x 3 with skins off and pixel, the pause-determinism bases); new `ref_m47_6c2/chain_hash.jsonl` (S0-S6 x seeds 1, 2) recorded once, replayed twice, with `--look=pixel`, and at 1560x878 with `--handoff=120`, 14 runs 0 failures each; each chained session replayed cold from its printed start: 13/14 equal. The 14th, S5 seed 2, diverges at 30 s, and so does a cold S5 seed 2 from `player_start` run alone against `ref_m47_6c1` (31 s, no carry): S5 under the gravity depends on what the process ran before (seen only on seed 2), which predates M47.6c2 (Still open). Found by that replay and fixed: the capture was `snapped()` in single precision, one ulp off the parsed "%.2f" text, which moved S2's replay (`GameHost.printed_start`).

**Windowed:** `.check/m47_6c2/*.png`: `seam_*` (S4's end with the beat open, the room at the beat, S5's gate after the invisible swap: the same walls and the square at the same screen point; in the game window only the gun's side, the AI's face and the version differ), `s2_*` (S2 stopped at 45 s: its 150-strong crowd popping as rings at the beat, the empty room, S3's gate after the chat upgrade with the square where it stood), `pop_*` (S5 cleared, nothing to pop, S6's gate at the carried spot).

**Review (4 findings, 1 upheld, all fixed):** the beat's pops for off-screen leftovers drew outside the game window (over the dev chat, the title bar, the taskbar), since `Puffs` does not clip its draw and the arena is far wider than the view. `clear_field` now pops only a leftover whose canvas point is inside the 1280x720 view; off-view ones still leave the room. Test: `test_carry.gd` `test_off_screen_leftovers_are_cleared_without_a_pop` (fails without the fix: 2 pops, one at x 2200); the Next-flow test now places its two chasers on screen. Re-gated after the fix (`.check/m47_6c2r/`): 91 runhash summaries 0 failures plus the render sheet exit 0, with only the known cold `--start` replay of S5 seed 2 differing. That replay also differs when it runs twice in one process (30 s, 41 then 40 s against 42), so it depends on the process's history, as in Still open. `check.ps1 -BotRuns` CHECK PASSED with GUT 1156/1156 and the hosted and chain hash steps. Windowed `s2_*` / `seam_*` again: 150 pops are all inside the game window and the room is empty.

You get a revolver. The rest is out there.", "Run dry and you get the revolver back. It just reloads."); `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated; check.ps1's S7 expectations (bot_s7, the chain and both door fixtures: `t=117.1`, `swaps=arsenal@5.5,survival@41.5,auto@77.5,off@111.5 leftovers=0 end=script`).

**Deviations:** the revolver's ranks add traits (knockback at 2, ricochet at 3) beside the design's reload 0.9 / pierce 1, since every weapon rank must add a behaviour (`test_each_rank_changes_exactly_its_keys`); Survival's smaller crowd needed the crowd overlay without a `crowd` basic (the design assumed the multipliers already applied; Survival has no crowd basic); the path titles are data in `PathDef`, not in `tools/path_lines.gd` (a line-budget counter; `survival` counts 291 there with the overhead, and the tool was already over budget on Arsenal, 614, before this milestone); there is no "HUD digits role" in the Pixel look, so the overhead draws with the UI font in Palette colours under every look; the overhead is a child of the player (world space), not a HUD-layer widget; S8 / S9 Survival lines that name the clock stay for M47.6d2; S9 had no Survival line naming the pistol; the full GUT run (pre-commit) found `test_dash_aim` red on the design's S7 times: the 12 s intro could not end before the 4 s Arcade cue and "Saving that one." at 38 / "Saving that too." at 74 ran into the 40 / 76 swap warnings, so the three intro lines moved, word for word, to the front of S7's `before_play` (S7 has no bubble intro, like S4) and the two lines to 36 / 73 s (`test_s7_tryout` TIMES; `lines_stage2.md` regenerated).

### 6.24 As built (M47.6d2)
Stage 2: S8 and S9 endings and the carry, per design `.check/stage1_rework/design.md` section 6 (rev. 2), 2.6, 2.7, 3 (S8, S9) and 4.1.

**Built:** `SessionDef.enemy_total_by_path` (path id -> the fixed set, like `goal_by_path`; linted: known paths, values >= 0, a clear ending, a path, not a tryout; the `kills N` spawn-cue lint reads the smallest set) and `enemy_total_for(path)`; `TopdownGenre.enemy_total_now(session)` (the live path's set) feeds the spawner budget, the run loop's `enemy_total` / goal value and `Reactions._progress()`; `SessionDef.fresh_start` (S9: always `player_start`, in `GameHost.carry_ok` and, as a guard, in `TopdownGenre.start_for`); `GameHost.carry_ok` now carries within one stage for Stage 1 and 2 (`CARRY_STAGES`), so S7 -> S8 is seamless; S8 `clear` (Arcade 100, Auto 180, Survival 55; `enemy_total` 100 for an unanswered path; hidden cap 180 s; `goal_by_path` gone), its two clock lines ("Two minutes" / "Ninety seconds") replaced by one "One wave. I'll build it properly this time."; S9 `clear` (Arcade 120, Auto 220, Survival 70; hidden cap 210 s; `fresh_start`); the `wave_done` / `last_two` / `last_one` reactions run past Stage 1 (no `until`), so the S8 / S9 endings are called; bot_runs `--session-set enemy_total` clears `enemy_total_by_path`; `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated; check.ps1: the chain's S8 (`end=cleared spawned=100/100`, a carried start) and S9 (`spawned=120/120`, `start=1584.00,672.00`), the `--only` S8 / S9 runs per path (their set cleared, S8 after 87 s, S9 after 75 s, the Arcade fusion), the hosted S8 hash against `ref_m47_6d2`.

**Deviations:** refs under `ref_m47_6d2` (design: `ref_m47_6d`); `carry_ok` drops the `stage_card_due(to)` test (the same-stage rule already keeps a stage's first session cold, and it read true for S8 at S7's report, before S7 is done, so S7's leftovers would not have popped); the three clear reactions now also load in S7 (they never fire there: no budget; `tests/gut/stage2_reactions.json` gains them); the design's counts are kept as built (every bot gate met, no tuning); no S8 / S9 debrief line for the hidden cap; `export.ps1` not run (the orchestrator ships).

### 6.25 As built (M48.5a): the version rail (Pixel UI, owner pick 3A)
*Built in the `build/m48_5a` worktree from the owner's pick of mockup 3A ("the version rail", `Pixel_A.dc.html` play screen and `Pixel_A_hud.dc.html`). Presentation only: every hash ref unchanged. Plan: `.check/m48_5a/plan.md`; gates `.check/m48_5a/gates.sh`.*
- **When it shows:** `Skins.is_on(&"ui_panel")` and not chapter mode (the terminal's own `dress()` rule): from S9's `at 55: skin ui` in the campaign, in every session with `--look=pixel` / F10 (Stage 1 shows only the modules whose basic is live). Off (no look, `--skins=off`, the stage card, the v6 loop): the raw terminal, HUD corner and corner mascot exactly as before. The host re-checks on `Skins.hub.skin_changed`, every attach and around chapter mode.
- **Layout** (`ui/rail/rail_layout.gd`, pure): the mockup's 1600x900 canvas scaled by `s = min(W/1600, H/900)`, centred, plum checker in the margins. The game view is 1056x594 design px (16:9: the 1280x720 sim stretches evenly; `size_2d_override` untouched); the rail runs under it into the chat, the module row hangs under the rail, the chat log sits right of the game, the reply box under the rail. A live windowed host takes the 1600x900 base once, the first time the rail shows (kept centred where the window was), and nothing after resizes or moves the window: stage cards, S9 retries and the look turning off letterbox into the size the player has (`GameHost.rail_window`, review fix); tool hosts never resize.
- **The rail** (`rail_frame.gd`): one tick per minor version (`RailModel` folds `CampaignDB.campaign.sessions` bumps), gold pips where a module shipped (KILLS v0.2 enemies, HP v0.3 health, DASH v0.4, the path v0.7 tryout, PACK v0.8 first chosen path), the current version a blinking tick joined to the chat spine, "next ?" after. A live rule-book edit becomes a hotfix `vX.Y.Z+n` and tags the module its text names.
- **Modules** (`rail_modules.gd`, values from `rail_state.gd`, redrawn only when the reading changes): KILLS (`RunState.kills`); HP (hearts, hp/max, "n hits taken" from `session_runner.hits`); DASH (the move's label and accent, READY / RECHARGING, 10 segments, %, "ready in x s", the key chip; the cooldown read from `Dasher._cooldown_left` and its peak, no gameplay edit); the path slot: Arsenal HAND (2 cards: rank badge, glyph, 8 pips then +N, fused frame, misfuse tilt, the fusion strip) + PACK (x/6, segments, kills to the next pack), Auto ITEMS (4 slots + 2 passive wells, LV, XP %), Survival AMMO (the weapon card, n/total, shell grid, crates taken, "+ Pistol inf"). Big numbers are code-drawn 5x7 digits (`RailArt.DIGITS`).
- **The chat:** the same `TerminalPanel` and the same rows, dressed instead of replaced (`TerminalPanel.set_dresser`, `rail_chat.gd`): paper back, Pixelify font, the chat palette; each AI line gets a spine tick and a version chip with the speaker in red; `$ patch apply` and its notes become a patch slip, a hotfix a hotfix slip; choice rows hide and become gold / cream pills in the reply box (`rail_reply.gd`: key chip, click or number key, the same `chosen` signal); the answer shows as a mint "you" line; the input well reads "> press 1 2" / "move to play" / "watching you play" with the terminal's own caret. The HUD corner text and path widget hide (`Hud.set_docked`), the corner body too (`Mascot.set_docked`): the AI sits on the rail at the log's bottom-right (`rail_blob.gd`, the player's own body pick, mirroring expression and talking, Pixel body style once `mascot` is revealed).
- **Art and font:** every colour from `systems/skins/art/rail_art.gd` (kept out of `grids.gd`, so the atlas digest does not move); icons are code-drawn grids (heart, skull, arrow, pack, crate, infinity). The one new file is the OFL font **Pixelify Sans** (`ui/rail/font/`, with `OFL.txt`; imported without antialiasing or hinting) through `UiTheme.pixel_font(bold)`, with the mono font as fallback; bold is wght 500 (600-700 turns C into O at 16 px).
- **Reduce flashing:** the reveal is an instant swap instead of a 0.25 s crossfade; the current tick, the live dot and the caret hold steady.
- **Deviations from the mockup:** (1) modules in ship order (KILLS before HP), so the rail reads as a timeline; (2) tick labels are the campaign's real versions, not the mockup's sample numbers; (3) a hotfix reads `vX.Y.Z+n`; (4) Auto has no "EVOLVE next level" line (no evolution code yet); (5) Auto draws 4 slots (`auto.tres`), pips cap at `Loadout.MAX_RANK` 3; (6) big numbers are pixel digits instead of a second display font (one font; Pixelify's 5 reads as S at display sizes); (7) no slip footer text; (8) DASH has a "pick: classic / grapple / tp" subtitle; (9) the blob is the player's mascot pick, not always the blob.
- **Tests:** new `tests/gut/test_rail_ui.gd` (17): layout at 1600x900 / 1920x1080 / 1760x720 / 1280x720 (the game 16:9 and inside the window, no module, track, log, reply or AI over it or over each other); no look keeps the raw terminal, HUD and corner; the look on and off keeps rows, labels and visibility; S9's manual reveal and the stage card; ship versions; module gating per basic; HP, kills, dash recharging then ready; hand and pack; Auto items; Survival ammo; patch and hotfix slips, choice pills by click; reduce flashing; the tool host's view size; the font covers every glyph of `campaign/lines_stage2.md`.
- **GUT:** full suite 1106/1106 (59 scripts, no script errors) after the review; before it, one file at a time: test_rail_ui 14/14, test_always_on 39/39, test_hud_layout 10/10, test_mascot 11/11, test_pixel_skin 30/30 (colour lint), test_front_door 26/26, test_playtest_build 8/8, test_s9_paint 22/22, test_arsenal 36/36, test_fusion 21/21, test_settings 19/19, test_readability 19/19, test_pause_menu 13/13, test_basics_added 21/21, test_flip_help 9/9, test_parking 9/9; no script errors.
- **Hash gates** (`.check/m48_5a/gates.sh`: m50_merge's gates from this worktree plus hosted `--shell --look=pixel` S8 and S9 x 3 paths at both view sizes): 82 summaries, all 0 failures (v6, gauntlet, campaign and `--look=pixel`, S4, S7 / S8 / S9 replays and `--skins=off`, pause determinism, every hosted row, the 12 new rail-on hosted rows); silhouette sheet exit 0; re-run after the review fixes, again 82 summaries with 0 failures. `check.ps1 -BotRuns`: CHECK PASSED.
- **Captures** (`.check/m48_5a/capture_rail.gd`, windowed, inert save): `s8_arsenal_recharging.png` (RECHARGING 63%, hand Pistol 7 / Shotgun 1, pack 2/6), `s8_auto.png` (ITEMS, LV 4), `s8_survival.png` (AMMO Shotgun II 15/24, crate), `s8_arsenal_1080.png` (scales cleanly at 1920x1080), `s8_choice_reply*.png` (the "again / move on" pills; the steady caret under reduce flashing), `s9_before_reveal.png` (raw), `s9_after_reveal.png` (the rail after `skin reveal ui`, cursor body), `s9_after_mascot.png` (pixel body), `raw_s8_arsenal.png` (raw unchanged, 1760x720).
- **Fixed during the build:** on the first reveal `RailFrame.sync` relaid the host out before `host.rail` was set, so the frame drew over the raw layout until the next resize; the frame is now assigned first.
- **Review (7 findings, 4 upheld, all fixed):** (1) the reply box paired the pills with the last line said, not the question (S8's death asks, says two more lines, then posts "again / move on"): with choices open it now takes the nearest line ending in `?` since the last exchange, else the last line; with none open, the latest line as before (`RailReply.asks`). (2) The body on the rail covered the right end of the newest chat line and the scrollbar: the log's bottom inset now ends above it (`RailLayout.LOG_BOTTOM_PAD`, 70 design px). (3) A live window was resized and re-centred on every rail on / off (the S9 reveal, each retry, every stage card): the base is taken once per host and never flipped back. (4) The Survival ammo test never reached its ammo branch (the loadout starts on the sidearm): it now gives a shotgun, checks left / total / title / rank, a second shot count, then the dry sidearm. New tests, each failing without its fix: `test_the_reply_box_shows_the_question_not_the_lines_after_it`, `test_the_log_rows_end_above_the_body_on_the_rail`, `test_the_window_takes_the_rail_base_once_and_never_flips_back`; the ammo test fails with `RailState._ammo` reading -1. test_rail_ui 17/17. Captures re-taken after the fixes. The full suite also caught the rail's colour key `well` naming the `well` mutation (test_mutations lint): renamed `sunk`.
- **`BUILD = "M48.5a"`.**
- **Still open (defaults taken):** Q1 modules in ship order vs the mockup's HP first (default ship order); Q2 a 1600x900 window once the rail first shows (default yes, windowed only, taken once and kept); Q3 the hotfix label `+n` (default yes); Q4 Auto's EVOLVE line waits for evolution code (default wait); Q5 the rail in Stage 1 under `--look=pixel` / F10 (default yes, modules gated by basics); Q6 Minimal / Splat UI styles later (default: they keep the raw terminal for now).
- **Doc/code notes:** 1.8 names a code-built 5x7 pixel font; the body uses the OFL font and only the big numbers are code-drawn digits. 0.7 / 6.15 say the font, layout and frame stay raw until M48.5 (now done). `TerminalPanel.pixel_colors()` builds colours with `Color("#hex")`, which the colour lint misses. The `HandWidget` comment says 3 slots (the hand is 2). `CampaignDB.jump_to` keeps the inert save's own version (v0.0.8), so the captures set it from the campaign fold.

### 6.26 As built (M48.5b): the version rail on the Stage 1 rework, and the digit fix
*Built in the `build/m48_5a` worktree (draft PR #1): `origin/main` (M47.6a-d2, `6604ccd`) merged into M48.5a, no rebase. Presentation only: every ref main uses is unchanged. Gates `.check/m48_5b/gates.sh` (main's `.check/m47_6d2/gates.sh`, `O=` and `cd` changed).*
- **Conflicts and how each was kept:** `main.gd` (BUILD only: `M48.5b`); `ui/mascot/mascot.gd` (both: the rail's `set_docked` / `docked` and the rework's `bubble_rows` / `clamp_text`); `ui/theme/ui_theme.gd` (both: `pixel_font` and `sans_font`); `ui/always_on/game_host.gd` (both: the desktop's layout and dressing, then `rail.apply_layout`; on attach `terminal.set_style(CampaignDB.chat_style())` first, then `_sync_rail()`); `ui/always_on/terminal.gd` (the rework's RAW / CHAT `style` kept as the one meaning of `style`; the rail's `dresser` beside it, never changing `style`); `PLAN.md` (main's 6.19-6.24 kept as numbered, M48.5a's section renumbered 6.25, its milestone row pointed there). `ui/hud.gd` merged clean.
- **The rail over the rework (`TerminalPanel`):** dressed after the chat upgrade, new rows are the terminal's raw rows again (`_in_chat()` is "chat and not dressed"), so the rail's spine, chips, slips and pills work as in M48.5a; each such row also takes its place in `_order`, so `rows()` and `row_count()` keep every row once, in order, across a dress and an undress. A row keeps the kind it was made as (`_chat_owns`: an open `you>`, a think or a cut started in the chat finish there). Dressing hides the ChatPanel and shows the raw list (the raw rows from before the upgrade, marked `pre_chat`, stay hidden and out of `row_nodes()`: the rail's log starts at its reveal); undressing brings the chat back. `set_style` while dressed builds the chat hidden. The desktop hides under the rail (`GameHost.relayout`), and the rail frame sits just above it in the host's children.
- **The play gate under the rail:** the terminal's input row (`ChatInput`) docks in the reply box's well (`TerminalPanel.dock_input`, `RailReply.layout_input`) in the pixel font, so `play` is typed there as in the chat (Enter once armed, `p`, a moved key pulses the ghost); with no choices open a gate shows one gold `play` pill (`RailReply.pills_of`), which gives play as the chat's pill does. The well's own "move to play" / "watching you play" texts are gone (the input shows its ghost `play` or "Talk to <AI>..."); with choices open it still reads "press 1 2".
- **HUD data after the rework:** KILLS reads `RunState.kills` (no goal counter, as the rework wants); HP, DASH and the Arsenal hand / pack x/6 read as before (checked by test_rail_ui and the S9 captures); Survival's AMMO now reads the revolver (`WeaponDef.magazine`): "n/6", one pip per round, "rounds · then it reloads", and while it reloads a gold fill bar (from `Weapon.reload_left` and the rank's `reload_s`); scavenged guns keep the shell count. HP's big numbers shrink to fit the cell (Auto's "10/10" ran past it).
- **The digit fix (owner nit: the small text's 2 read as an 8):** Pixelify Sans's design pixel is 0.091 em, so at the rail's 12-16 px it lands on 1.1-1.5 screen px and its 2 / 8, 5 / S, 0 / 8 and 1 / 7 blur together (24 px is not clean either). `UiTheme.pixel_font()` is now a two-font chain: first a code-built bitmap font holding only 0-9 (`UiTheme.pixel_digits`, one cache per size 6-96 px), then Pixelify for every other glyph, then the mono fallback. The digits are `RailArt.DIGITS` (the 5x7 grid the big numbers already used: a slashed 0, a flagged 1 with a foot, a flat-topped 5) stretched to Pixelify's digit height in whole pixels (`RailArt.small_digit`: bar rows and outer columns at the font's stroke, the rest shared evenly). Ascent, descent and each digit's advance are Pixelify's, so line heights match and string widths move by at most 1 px. It covers everything in the pixel font: the rail's drawn text, the log's labels, the input.
- **Deviations:** (1) the rail's log starts at the reveal; the chat's earlier history stays in the (hidden) chat and comes back when the rail turns off. (2) Rows made while the rail shows stay raw rows: after the rail turns off (F10, a stage card) they are not in the chat's view, though `rows()` keeps them in order. (3) No "move to play" hint: the rework's gate is typed, so the well shows the input itself. (4) The digits change only the pixel font (the rail); Palette's raw and chat fonts are untouched.
- **Tests (each fails without its change):** test_rail_ui 20 (was 17): `test_rows_made_under_the_rail_keep_their_order_in_the_chat`, `test_the_play_gate_in_the_reply_box`, `test_small_digits_are_whole_pixels_and_distinct` (whole pixels, inside the advance, 2 / 8, 5 / 6, 0 / 8, 1 / 7, 3 / 8, 9 / 8 distinct at 12-32 px regular and bold; the font has 0-9 first, letters fall through, widths within 2 px, the same line height); the raw-UI and on / off tests now expect the dev-chat and the desktop (hidden under the rail, back after) and the input's home; the Survival test reads the revolver (sidearm "Revolver", 6/6, 5 after a shot, the reload fill, full again). The font test finds Pixelify as the variation behind the digits.
- **Numbers:** GUT: test_rail_ui 20/20; one file at a time test_always_on 40/40, test_hud_layout 10/10, test_mascot 11/11, test_pixel_skin 30/30, test_carry 12/12, test_endings 16/16, test_front_door 26/26, test_s9_paint 23/23, test_playtest_build 8/8, test_dev_chat 18/18, test_play_gate 21/21; full suite 1193/1193 (63 scripts, no script errors) inside `check.ps1 -BotRuns` (CHECK PASSED) and again on the final tree. Hash gates (`.check/m48_5b/gates.sh`, the same 82 logs as main's `m47_6d2` run): 81 runhash summaries, all 0 failures (v6, gauntlet, campaign and S4 vs `ref_m47_6c1` replayed twice and with `--look=pixel`, the chain vs `ref_m47_6c2` twice, pixel and 1560x878 with `--handoff=120`, S7 vs `ref_m47_6d1`, S8 and S9 x 3 paths vs `ref_m47_6d2` twice, with `--look=pixel` / `--skins=off`, every `--shell` row at both views and with `--handoff=120`, the S8 x 3 and S4 pause-determinism bases), the silhouette sheet exit 0; no ref recorded or changed (`ref_m47_6d2` byte-equal to main's). The known cold `--start` S5 seed 2 replay is not in this gate set and was not run. Bots (bot numbers, from `check.ps1 -BotRuns`): the same sessions and outcomes as main (S8 / S9 x 3 paths won, 112.6-157.5 s).

### 6.27 As built (M50.4a): Auto depth, rank 6, 3 shared slots, no cap
*Built in the `build/auto` worktree on `build/m48_5a` (main 8a0bc64 + M48.5a) from `.check/auto_depth/design.md` rev. 2, milestone M50.4a; owner decisions `auto-evolution-design` (3 weapons + 2 passives, rank cap 6) and `overnight-2026-11-26`. **The Stage 1 rework (M47.6a-d2) and Arcade (M50.2 / M50.3) are not in this branch**: built against today's goals (S8 survive 120 s, S9 survive 150 s), today's refs, no S10. Not committed.*
- **Data** (`paths/auto.tres`): `rank_curve deep`, `rank_cap 6`, `max_slots 4 -> 3`, `max_passives 2 -> 0`, `max_items 2`, `shared_slots true`, `max_picks 10 -> 0`, `full_line ""`. `PathDef` gains `shared_slots`, `max_items` (M50.4b fills the slots), `threshold_mult` (1.0; `threshold()` rounds when it is set; M50.4b's `human_pace` uses it) and three `problems()` rows (`shared_slots` needs `max_passives 0`; `max_items >= 0`; `threshold_mult > 0`). `max_picks 0` = no cap on a levelups path (`LevelMeter`); a tryout still stops after its taste list.
- **Ranks:** Auto weapons rank 1-6 on Arsenal's deep curve (damage x1 / 1 / 1.5 / 1.5 / 2 / 2; A, B, C at 2, 4, 6). `PickOffer.candidate` tops a deep weapon at `Loadout.rank_cap`; `card_line` prints digits and the gain on the deep curve ("Pistol 3 -> 4: shots seek (60 deg/s, 4 T)"), ROMAN only on the classic curve (the rank-5 index crash is gone). The launcher ranks 1-6 with sticky at 2 and implode at 4 (no `mine_layer`: Arcade's M50.3a is not in this branch); well and orbit keep their classic traits as A and B.
- **Shared slots** (`Loadout.shared_slots`, copied in `apply_path`; `weapons()` = slots + passives, `weapon_count()`): a new weapon of any mode needs one of the 3 slots (`candidate` refuses a 4th), and `_add` enforces it for `give` / F2 / `--loadout` / the bench: the weakest weapon that is not the active slot goes (lowest rank, ties the last in `weapons()` order; a passive-mode weapon's node is freed; a `weapon_dropped` run-log event). Nova / well / orbit take a weapon slot.
- **Fillers** (no candidate at all; Auto only: `growth levelups` + `shared_slots`): two cards, `hp` "+1 max HP, heal 1" and `boost` "+5% damage, every gun (this session)" (`Loadout.boost`, `BOOST_STEP` 0.05). Other paths keep the single "+1 HP" filler. The boost reaches values through `Weapon.mods` (`might` role -> damage; empty mods = the exact old `value()` path, tested on every base weapon x ranks 1-3); node weapons re-read their numbers with nova's and the well's `reconfigure(w)` (the nova's clock kept; the well's `dps` / `life`, live fields keep their bite) or the orbit shards' `damage`. `clear()` / `apply_path()` reset the boost.
- **Nova's C:** `nova.tres` `mechanics [shove, dash_nova, shockwave]` (classic nova unchanged). `weapons/traits/shockwave.gd` + `shockwave_ring.gd`: each nova blast sends a ring from its radius to 4 T over 0.4 s of game time; each enemy it passes is hit once for 0.5x (kind `blast`, `weapon:nova`); node-reference hit list, no RNG, at most 4 rings alive, not a projectile.
- **HUD:** the raw `LevelWidget`: "LV n" + bar, then every held weapon ("Pistol 6*  Nova 2", `*` = the top rank), and the 2 item slots "Passives  + +" dim, right of the bar. The rail's ITEMS (`rail_state._items` under `shared_slots`): weapons from `lo.weapons()` against `max_slots` 3, `passive_slots = max_items` 2 (empty wells until M50.4b), cap lv 6 with 6 pips.
- **S8 lines** (appended at the end of `cues`, `answer:path_auto`): `at 18` "New top: rank six. Every gun learns three tricks on the way." and `at 60` "Three guns, two passives. That's the whole backpack." `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **Telemetry** (additive; a levelups path, not a tryout): `levels`, `level_picks {upgrade, new, item_new, item_up, evolve, filler}` (`PathRunner.level_picks`), `ranks_end`. **Bot:** a separate `[botrun] auto: session= seed= level= picks= items=- ranks= max_rank= weapons= fillers= boost= level_picks=` line per Auto run (the session line is untouched). Picks stay the `pick_card` draw (the `auto_choice` rule is M50.4b).
- **Bug fixed on the way:** `paths/auto/gems.gd` `merge_oldest` filtered `_mine` with a typed lambda; a freed (taken) gem in the list made it fail, `filter()` returned an untyped `[]` and the typed assignment raised a SCRIPT ERROR (no merge at the 60-pickup cap). The uncapped S9 Auto reached it (48 errors per 2-seed run, which `run_hash` does not count as failures); now `assign()` with an untyped lambda (test_auto_depth fails without it). The S9 Auto ref was recorded after the fix.
- **Deviations:** (1) no S10 (T18): that session is Arcade's and not in this branch. (2) The item slots sit on the LV row, not a third row: the band is 60 px high (`test_hud_layout`). (3) The shockwave ring skips the enemies the blast itself reached, even after the shove pushed them out. (4) The two fillers are Auto-only. (5) `threshold_mult` exists but no probe sets it yet. (6) The dressed level-up cards (`Auto_Choices.dc.html`) are M50.4b in the design's milestone list; the panel keeps today's buttons with the new digit text. (7) `check.ps1` unchanged (the summary assertion is M50.4c1).

With Arcade: `pick_offer.gd` (ours: `BOOST`, `candidate`, `card_line(..., deep)`; theirs adds `gift`), `bot_runs.gd` (our `_auto_line`), `nova_pulse.gd` / `nova.tres`.

### 6.28 As built (M50.4b): Auto depth, passives and the dressed pick panel

Built on `build/auto` (M50.4a), without the Stage 1 rework or Arcade. Source: section 11 of `.check/auto_depth/design.md` rev. 2, and the M50.4m mockup (`.check/mockups/cards/Auto_Choices.dc.html`).

- **Items.** `weapons/item_def.gd` (`ItemDef`: `stat`, signed `per_level`, `pairs`, `card_text`, `built_in`) and `weapons/item.gd` (`Item`: def + level). There are 7 rows in `weapons/items/`:
  - Wired: Haste (-6 % cooldown per level, pairs with the Pistol), Area (+10 % blast, field and cone size, Shotgun), Might (+8 % damage, Nova).
  - Data only, with `built_in` set to "not built yet (S10+/S11)": Duration, Amount, Speed, Magnet. They are never offered and never given, and `item_problems()` lists them.

  `WeaponDB` adds `items()`, `item()`, `has_item()` and `item_built()`. `get_weapon` never resolves an item.
- **Stats.** `Weapon.mods` (role -> multiplier) and `WeaponDef.stat_fields` (role -> field). The default fields are haste -> cooldown, might -> damage, area -> blast_radius.
  - An empty `mods` returns exactly the old value. A byte test covers the deep curve, ranks 1-6, every weapon.
  - `Loadout.mods_now()` is the boost's might times each item's multiplier.
  - `_apply_mods` reconfigures nova and the well. The well has `stat_fields {might: damage}`, and its field radius is multiplied by Area.
  - Haste never changes the well's life.
- **Offers.** `PathDef.offer_pool_extra` accepts item ids. `auto.tres` lists nova, well, haste, area, might.
  - `PickOffer.candidate` builds an item card `{id, rank, kind, item, paired, text}`. It does so when a slot is free (2 slots, `max_items`) or when the item is held below the cap.
  - Card text example: "Haste 1: guns fire 6% faster  (new) · your Pistol evolves with this".
  - `PathRunner._on_picked` adds or levels the item and records it in `level_picks` as `item_new` / `item_up`.
  - The F1 `state_text` shows `items=` only when items are held.
- **Dressed panel.** A level-up offer (`PickOffer.level > 0`, not a pack) shows `LevelCard`s (`ui/level_card.gd`, 232 x 356).
  - Each card shows: the kind (WEAPON / PASSIVE / BONUS), a NEW tag, "LV a > b", a glyph window, the name, pips up to the cap (the new one ringed), the effect, the item's pairing, and "[key] choose".
  - Once the UI look is on, the cards use the Pixel style (plum and paper, as in the mockup). Otherwise they are flat, in Palette roles (new role `card_item`).
  - Banner: "LEVEL n · pick 1 of n · game paused".
  - Each card is still a Button, so keys, pad, click and the input delay all work. The card's line is its tooltip and still goes to the logs.
  - The pack and gift panels are unchanged.
  - There are 7 item glyphs plus hp and boost: tier 0 shapes in `ui/weapon_glyph.gd`, Pixel grids in `systems/skins/art/card_art.gd`.
- **HUD.** The raw LV widget has an items row ("Haste 2  Area 1", or "Haste 1  +"). The rail's passive row is filled by `rail_state._items`.
- **Campaign.**
  - S8: a `[haste]` line at 38 s, an `[area]` line at 42 s, and a debrief line.
  - S9: `give haste` at 9 s (Auto only), a `holds haste` line (said once Haste is picked, review fix 3), and a `[might]` line at 94 s. An item give on another path is skipped and logged as `give_skipped`.
  - New `SessionDef` problems: an item give needs a built item and `answer:path_auto`, and a tag that names an unbuilt item is a problem.
  - `lines_stage2.md` regenerated (+5 lines). Telemetry adds `items_end`.
- **Bot.**
  - `bot_runs.auto_choice(panel, lo, probe, drawn)` applies design rules 2-7. It runs after the `pick_card` draw, so S7 and every non-Auto hash stay equal.
  - Probes: `random_picks` (keeps the drawn card) and `no_evolve` (no-op until evolutions exist).
  - `HUMAN_PACE_LEVELS` is 1.5.
  - The `[botrun] auto:` line adds `items=` and `item_t=` (the time each item joined).
  - `tools/run_hash.gd` uses the same rule.
- **Deviations.**
  1. Item glyphs and card colours are in `card_art.gd`, not `grids.gd`, so the atlas digest does not move. This means less friction with Arcade's digest move.
  2. Area scales `blast_radius` (launcher, nova ring), the well's field, the Shotgun's cone reach (its pellets' `lifetime`, `shotgun.tres` `stat_fields`) and the laser's burn width (review fix 1). The knockback push itself is not scaled.
  3. The raw items row is on the LV widget, not on a row of its own.
  4. No S10 here, because this branch has none, so S10 item tags do not apply.
  5. Pixel item glyphs show whenever any look is on.
  6. The gate script was copied from `.check/m50_4a/gates.sh`, which is newer than the brief's `m48_5a`.
  7. New test file `test_auto_items.gd` (25 tests with the review's) instead of growing `test_auto_depth`.
  8. Card height is 356 (the mockup is about 344), so the key row clears the focus ring.
- **Touch points with the Stage 1 rework (for the merge):**
  - `s2_02_day_one.tres`: 2 cues and a debrief line appended.
  - `s2_03_paint.tres`: 3 cues appended.
  - `campaign/session_runner.gd`: item give branch.
  - `campaign/session_def.gd`: item give and tag problems, and the `holds` item check.
  - `campaign/cue.gd` (the `holds <item>` when) and `campaign/lines_render.gd` (renders it).
  - `weapons/shotgun.tres` (`stat_fields`), `weapons/traits/burn_line*.gd` (`width`), `ui/rail/rail_modules.gd` (Auto slot gun).
  - `campaign/campaign_db.gd`: `items_end`.
  - `ui/pick_panel.gd`: banner and `_build_dressed`.
  - `systems/palette.gd`: `card_item`.
  - `lines_stage2.md`: regenerate after the merge.
  - `main.gd`: BUILD.
- **Numbers.**
  - GUT, one file at a time (no full suite): 34 files, 798 tests, 0 failing, 0 script errors (`.check/m50_4b/gut_affected.txt`). The new test_auto_items passes 21/21.
  - Gates (`.check/m50_4b/gates.sh`): 86 summaries, 0 failures, no script error in any log.
  - S8 and S9 Auto were recorded in `.check/ref_m50_4b/` and differ from `ref_m50_4a`: S8 first differs at hash 233 / 265, S9 at hash 92 (Haste given at 9 s).
  - Every other ref is equal: S7 x4, Arsenal vs `ref_m50_1b`, Survival, v6, gauntlet, campaign, S4, pixel / skins-off, shell, pause.
- **Bots (8 seeds; the design asks for 16).**

  | Set | Result | Target | Met? |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Invincible S8 Auto, wins | 8/8 | | |
  | Invincible S8 Auto, level median | 13.5 (13-15) | 13-16 | yes |
  | Invincible S8 Auto, a weapon at rank 6 | 8/8 | | yes |
  | Invincible S8 Auto, 2 items held by 60 s | 1/8 | 14/16 | **no** |
  | Mortal S8 Auto, deaths | 5/8 | <= 10/16 | yes |
  | Mortal S8 Auto, median t | 95.1 s | not below the parent's 116.2 s | **no** |
  | Mortal S9 Auto, deaths | 7/8, median 81.0 s | <= 8/16, within 6 of both other paths | **no** |
  | Mortal S9 Arsenal, deaths | 3/8, 150 s | | |
  | Mortal S9 Survival, deaths | 1/8 | | |
  | Invincible S9 Auto | 8/8 wins, level median 16.5, Haste 3 + Might 1 held | | |

  - Items by 60 s: the first item joins at 38-47 s, the second at 51-105 s (median about 68 s).
  - Mortal S8 comparison: M50.4a on the same set had 6/8 deaths, 95.7 s. The parent (`random_picks`) had 4/8, 116.2 s.
  - Mortal S9 comparison: M50.4a had 6/8 deaths, 126.2 s.
  - With `--probe=random_picks`: S8 mortal 6/8 deaths (85.9 s), S9 mortal 8/8 deaths (60.3 s).
  - The mortal S9 runs that die hold one or two weapons plus Haste 2-3. The item picks take levels that went to the guns in M50.4a.
  - No retune (AGENTS 6). The design's "too weak" fallback is crowd spawn mult 1.6 -> 1.5, which is an owner call.
- **Captures.** Made with `.check/m50_4b/capture_items.gd` and `card_preview.gd`: windowed, inert save, S8 Auto, seed 1. They match the mockup's layout and palettes.
  - `s8_auto_items.png`: raw HUD with 56 s left, "LV 7 / Pistol 6* / Haste 1 +".
  - `pick_item.png`: flat, LEVEL 6. Area NEW passive "pairs with Shotgun", Pistol 5 > 6, Shotgun 2 > 3.
  - `pick_weapon.png`: Pixel. Pistol 3 > 4, Shotgun NEW (its own long-barrel gun since review fix 2), Haste NEW passive.
  - `pick_filler.png`: Pixel, everything at 6, the two BONUS cards.
  - `rail_items.png`: Pixel rail. ITEMS 1+1, Pistol LV 6 MAX, Haste LV 1 in the passive slot.
  - `preview_{flat,pixel}{,_fill}.png`: the panel on its own.
- **Review (4 findings, 3 upheld, all fixed).**
  1. Area was a dead pick in S8: it scaled only blasts and the well's field, but S8 Auto holds only the Pistol and the Shotgun, and its card said "pairs with Shotgun". Fix: `shotgun.tres` `stat_fields {area: lifetime, haste: cooldown, might: damage}` (Area grows the cone's reach, x1.1 per level) and the burn line's width reads `stat_mult(&"area")` (`BurnLineMod.width` -> `BurnLineZone.width`, 1.0 without Area, byte-equal). Card text "+10% blast, field and cone size", the 42 s line "And area: bigger blasts, longer shotgun reach." Test: `test_area_grows_the_shotgun_reach_and_the_burn_width` (fails on the old `shotgun.tres`).
  2. Pixel weapon cards drew the shot sprite, and the Pistol's and the Shotgun's are the same pellet. Fix: `CardArt.GUNS` (Pistol, Shotgun, Laser, Launcher, from the mockup sheet's pixel guns) and `WeaponGlyph.draw_card`, used by the Pixel level cards and Auto's rail slots (other weapons and the flat cards keep their glyphs). Test: `test_each_card_weapon_has_its_own_gun`. Capture: `pick_weapon.png` now shows a pistol and a long-barrel shotgun.
  3. S9's "Haste came back with you" played at 18 s, but `give haste` only puts Haste first in the next offer. Fix: a new cue time `holds <item>` (`Cue.held`, due once the PathRunner's Loadout holds it; `SessionDef` refuses an unbuilt item; `lines_render` prints it), and S9's line is now `answer:path_auto holds haste:`. Tests: `test_a_holds_cue_waits_for_the_item`, `test_the_runner_says_the_haste_line_only_after_the_pick`, the shipped-lines test.
  - After the fixes: S8 Auto re-recorded in `.check/ref_m50_4b/` (first differs from the pre-review ref at hash 429 / 441, seeds 1 / 2; the old one is in `pre_review/`), S9 Auto unchanged (the line moves no hash). Gates again: 86 summaries, 0 failures.
  - Bots after the fixes (8 seeds, `.check/m50_4b/review/`; bot numbers, not a retune): invincible S8 Auto 8/8 wins, level median 14 (12-14), a rank-6 weapon 8/8, 2 items by 60 s 1/8 (second item 51-110 s); mortal S8 Auto 6/8 deaths, median 77.5 s (hits: projectile 35, contact 2); mortal S9 Auto 7/8 deaths, 81.0 s (unchanged); S9 Arsenal 3/8, 150 s (unchanged).
- **`BUILD = "M50.4b"`.**
- **Still open (defaults taken):** see section 9 (items by 60 s, mortal S9 Auto, mortal S8 Auto).

### 6.29 As built (M50.4c1): Auto depth, EVOLVE, the Minigun and the live nerf

Built on `build/auto` (M50.4b), without the Stage 1 rework or Arcade. Source: section 11 of `.check/auto_depth/design.md` rev. 2, and the M50.4m mockup (`.check/mockups/cards/Auto_Choices.dc.html`, the EVOLVE card).

- **Evolutions.** `WeaponDef` gains `base`, `catalyst`, `launch_values` and `is_evolution()`. `problems()` checks the catalyst, the launch fields and "not built yet (<where>)". There are 7 rows in `weapons/evolutions/`:
  - Built: **Minigun** (Pistol + Haste). Aimed, cooldown 0.066 (4 ticks, 15 shots/s), damage 1.5, speed 1100, life 0.8 s. Mechanics: heavy_round, seeker, chain, and the new `sweep` (`weapons/traits/sweep.gd`: a fixed -6/-2/2/6/2/-2 degree pattern by shot count, no RNG). `launch_values {cooldown 0.033 (2 ticks, 30/s), damage 2.0, pierce 1}`.
  - Rows only: Supernova (Nova + Might) and Thunderclap (Shotgun + Area), "M50.4c2"; Sunbeam and Barrage (with Duration / Amount); Halo and Singularity, "S11".
  - `WeaponDB` adds `evolutions()`, `evolution_for(weapon)`, `evolution_built(id)` and `evolution_problems()`. The checks: the base is a base weapon, the catalyst pairs with it, a built row needs a built catalyst, and mechanics = the base's mechanics + a built signature. `get_weapon` resolves evolutions; `all()` / `ids()` stay the base weapons.
- **Stats.** `value()` reads `Weapon.launch` (field -> value) first, then the item mods. `Weapon.label()` is the title in capitals ("MINIGUN"). The deep curve treats an evolution like a fusion: all mechanics at level 1.
- **EVOLVE.** It is offered only when all of these hold: the `evolve` basic is live (it belongs to Auto, `Unlocks.CAMPAIGN_BASICS`), the base is at rank 6 (the cap), its catalyst is held, and the row is built.
  - `PathRunner.evolve_option()` sets `PickOffer.evolve` on a level-up offer. EVOLVE is the choice **after** the cards (key `choose_(n+1)`) and is never a card, so the `pick_rng` draws never move.
  - `_on_picked` with index `cards.size()` calls `_take_evolve`:
    - `Loadout.evolve(from, into)` replaces the base in place and keeps the slot, the shots and the item mods.
    - The level-up is spent (`picks`, `level_picks.evolve`) and an `evolved` event is logged.
    - The first time: `SaveGame.learn_recipe` and a "New recipe: Minigun (pistol + haste)" patch note.
    - Limits: one per weapon, 2 at most (`MAX_EVOLUTIONS`). An evolved base and a held evolution are never offered again.
  - Steering: once `evolve` is live, a held weapon under rank 6 whose catalyst is held is the offer's first card (`steer()`).
  - `evolve_ready_count` / `evolve_ready_t` count each time EVOLVE becomes available.
- **The live nerf.**
  - Action `nerf <s>` (it must sit on an `evolved` cue) applies the launch values to the evolution just made.
  - `<s>` seconds of game time later (the pause stops the clock) they clear, with one glitch pulse (`_pulse`, which respects `reduce_flashing`; it does not count in `wipes`).
  - The patch note is built from the effective numbers: "Balance: Minigun 30 -> 15 shots/s, damage 2 -> 1.5. It was too strong." With Might held, both damages are scaled; with Haste 5-6 it reads "30 -> 20".
  - `stop()` lands a pending nerf; a path swap drops it.
  - Whens: `evolve_ready N` (waits SAY_GAP after the last say, dropped once N evolutions exist), `evolved N`, `nerfed N`. Condition `evolved` (debrief). `SessionDef.problems()`: these need the `evolve` basic and `answer:path_auto`.
  - The debrief changelog files a "Balance: " note under **Changed** ("~ Changed minigun 30 -> 15 shots/s, ...").
- **S9 ("Fresh paint", Auto only).**
  - At 84 s: `add evolve` and "New trick. A gun at rank six plus its passive evolves."
  - `evolve_ready 1`: "It's glowing. Take EVOLVE."
  - `evolved 1`: `nerf 8` and "Oh. Oh no. That came out strong."
  - `nerfed 1`: "Too strong. Patched it down live. Still good, promise."
  - Debrief, `answer:path_auto&evolved`: "You evolved one. I only nerfed it a little."
  - `lines_stage2.md` regenerated (+5 lines). S8 has no `evolve` basic, so S8 Auto never steers or evolves.
- **UI.**
  - The gold EVOLVE card (`LevelCard.KIND_EVOLVE`, 264 x 356, flat and Pixel; Palette role `card_evolve`): the pink EVOLVE ribbon, the "PISTOL 6 MAX" + "HASTE" tiles, the merge arrows, the Minigun's own Pixel gun (`CardArt.GUNS`, from the mockup), the gold name plate, "fire rate x2.5, a sweeping stream", and "[n] evolve".
  - The plain panel gets an "EVOLVE: ..." button.
  - Raw HUD: a dim hint row ("EVOLVE next level: pistol lv 6 + haste -> MINIGUN", or "pistol lv 4/6 + haste -> MINIGUN"), inside the 60 px band.
  - Rail ITEMS: an evolution's slot has a gold ring, a pink "EVO" tag and its name. The bottom line shows the hint (pink when EVOLVE is ready).
  - The pause menu shows "Evolutions: Minigun (pistol + haste)" below the fusion recipes.
  - Pixel shot `shot_minigun_player` (a warm tracer) and a tier-0 `minigun` glyph.
- **Bot.**
  - `auto_choice` rule 1: take EVOLVE when offered, unless `--probe=no_evolve`.
  - The `[botrun] auto:` line adds `evolved= nerf= nerfed_line= launch_dps= pre_dps= launch_live= pre_live= evolve_kills=`. Damage is counted in the tool from `entity_damaged` (no sim change).
  - A new `[botrun] auto_depth:` summary per set.
  - `check.ps1`'s S9 Auto row asserts `evolved=1/1 ... nerf_landed=1/1 nerfed_before_clear=1/1`.
- **Telemetry** (additive): `evolutions` ({id, t, from, catalyst}), `nerf`, `evolve_ready_s`.
- **Deviations.**
  1. The gate script was copied from `.check/m50_4b/gates.sh`, which is newer than the brief's `m48_5a`.
  2. This branch has no S10, so S10's `add evolve` (design T18) is not built.
  3. This branch has no `enemy_total` (S9 is "survive 150 s"). So the `human_pace` row "EVOLVE at <= 50 % of enemy_total killed" is measured as kills at EVOLVE / kills at the end (`evolve_half`). "Said before the clear" is measured as "the `nerfed 1` cue fired before the session ended": the line is queued at that point, and the speech queue runs on the wall clock, which a headless bot outruns.
  4. `check.ps1` runs one seed, so its assertion is on that seed's summary (1/1), not the design's 14/16.
  5. The EVOLVE card is 264 wide (the sheet's 256 + the gold rim). Its window is 88 high so the name plate and the effect line stay inside the card.
  6. (Withdrawn by the review: the banner now counts EVOLVE, "pick 1 of n" with n = cards + 1, as in the mockup.)
  7. `SaveGame.parse` keeps evolution ids in `recipes` (same key, design Q12).
  8. New test file `test_evolve.gd` (26 tests) instead of growing `test_auto_items`.
  9. The atlas digest moves (`shot_minigun_player`): `test_pixel_skin` now pins `95eb8f0b...` (was `d07379b4...`).
- **Touch points with the Stage 1 rework and Arcade (for the merge):**
  - `s2_03_paint.tres`: 6 cues and a debrief line appended. Move them to the rework's S9 timings; S10 gets `add evolve`.
  - `campaign/cue.gd` (3 whens, `nerf`), `campaign/session_runner.gd` (ctx, `_waits`, the `nerf` branch of `_fire`), `campaign/session_def.gd` (evolve problems), `campaign/line.gd` (`evolved`), `campaign/campaign_db.gd` (`line_context.evolved`, telemetry).
  - `core/unlocks.gd` (`evolve` basic), `paths/auto.tres` (basics), `core/save_game.gd` (recipes parse).
  - `ui/pick_panel.gd` (the EVOLVE choice), `ui/level_card.gd`, `ui/debrief_screen.gd` (Balance -> Changed), `ui/pause_menu.gd`, `ui/rail/rail_state.gd` and `rail_modules.gd` (Arcade's rail T7 / T11), `systems/palette.gd` (`card_evolve`).
  - `systems/skins/art/grids.gd` and `skins.gd`. The atlas digest moves, so Arcade's digest must be re-pinned after the merge.
  - `tools/bot_runs.gd` (rule 1, `auto_depth`), `tools/check.ps1` (the S9 Auto assertion), `lines_stage2.md` (regenerate), `main.gd` (BUILD).
- **Numbers.**
  - GUT, one file at a time (no full suite): 41 files, 0 failing, 0 script errors (`.check/m50_4c1/gut_affected.txt`, `gut_affected2.txt`, `gut_affected3.txt`, `gut_evolve.log`). test_evolve passes 26/26.
  - Gates (`.check/m50_4c1/gates.sh`): 88 summaries, 0 failures, sheet exit 0, no script error in any log.
  - S9 Auto was recorded in `.check/ref_m50_4c1/`. Against `ref_m50_4b` it first differs at hash 716 / 512 (seeds 1 / 2). It replays equal twice, with skins off, in Pixel and in the shell (0 failures).
  - S8 Auto is equal to `ref_m50_4b` and Arsenal to `ref_m50_1b`. S7, Survival, v6, gauntlet, campaign, S4, pixel, shell and pause are all equal.
- **Bots (8 seeds; the design asks for 16; `.check/m50_4c1/bots/`).**

  | Set | Result | Target (scaled to 8) | Met? |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | Invincible S9 Auto, evolved | 8/8, median 91.8 s (85.2-96.8) | >= 14/16 (7/8), median <= 100 s | yes |
  | Invincible S9 Auto, nerf landed | 8/8 | n/n | yes |
  | Invincible S9 Auto, `nerfed 1` before the end | 8/8 | >= 14/16 (7/8) | yes |
  | Invincible S9 Auto, wins / level | 8/8, max level 17 | | |

The design's fallback (`add evolve` at 0) was written for the rework's clear S9 and would move the beat, so it was not applied (AGENTS 6). See section 9.
