### 6.30 As built (M50.4c2): Supernova, Thunderclap and the stress bench

Built on `build/auto` (M50.4c1), without the Stage 1 rework or Arcade. Source: section 11 of `.check/auto_depth/design.md` rev. 2. For M50.4c2 it asks for the two defs built, the Supernova node (keeps the clock rule), `clap`, their glyphs and the 3-evolution caps bench.

- **Supernova** (`weapons/evolutions/supernova.tres`, built). It is Nova + Might, a node weapon on `nova_pulse.gd`.
  - Steady: a blast every 2.0 s, radius 2.5 T, 8 damage. The shockwave ring goes out to 6 T at 0.75x the damage, and the shove pushes 1.5 T.
  - Launch values: every 1.2 s, 12 damage.
  - Mechanics: shove, dash_nova, shockwave, and the signature `supernova` (`weapons/traits/supernova.gd`).
    - The signature sets the node's new `ring_to`, `ring_mult` and `shove_impulse`.
    - A plain nova keeps 4 T, 0.5x and 610 (`test_a_plain_nova_keeps_its_numbers`).
  - **Clock rule.** `Loadout.evolve` carries nova's two clocks into the Supernova node: the time to the next blast and the dash wait.
    - The blast clock is clamped to the new period (`nova_pulse.clocks()` / `keep_clocks()`).
    - An item pick still calls `reconfigure`, which keeps the clock.
- **Thunderclap** (`weapons/evolutions/thunderclap.tres`, built). It is Shotgun + Area, aimed.
  - Steady:
    - 7 pellets over 34 degrees, cooldown 0.5, 2 damage per pellet, speed 900, life 0.35 s.
    - `mechanic_values` is empty, so point blank's 18 degrees do not apply.
  - Launch values: blast radius 1.5 T (3 T across), 4 damage.
  - `stat_fields`: Area scales `blast_radius`, Haste scales `cooldown`, Might scales `damage`.
  - Mechanics: knockback_cone, point_blank, shrapnel, and the signature `clap` (`weapons/traits/clap.gd`).
    - Once per volley (on pellet 0), `clap` pops one `Explosion.blast` 2.5 T ahead along the aim, or short of a wall in the way.
    - The blast uses the weapon's `blast_radius` (1.0 T) and damage, with knockback 420. Its kind is `blast` and it never pushes the player.
    - The pellets get `blast_radius` 0, so they never blast.
    - The blast is not a projectile, and it uses no RNG.
- **The nerf on a node weapon.** `arm_nerf`, `_land_nerf` and `_drop_nerf` now call `Loadout.reread(w)`.
  - On a passive-mode weapon, its node runs `reconfigure` and keeps its clock. Aimed weapons are unchanged.
  - The patch note for a node weapon reads "Balance: Supernova every 1.2 s -> every 2 s, damage 12.96 -> 8.64. It was too strong." The Minigun's note is unchanged. After the review the note names only the launch keys (see Review).
- **The EVOLVE card.** The line "fire rate xN, a sweeping stream" now shows only for an evolution with `sweep` (the Minigun). The other two show their pitch:
  - Supernova: "A bigger nova, a wider ring." (shortened after the Pixel capture: the longer line ran past the card's edge).
  - Thunderclap: "Every volley claps a blast ahead."
- **Pictures.**
  - Tier 0 shapes in `ui/weapon_glyph.gd`: `supernova` (a star in a ring) and `thunderclap` (a fan and a blast).
  - Pixel pictures `GUN_SUPERNOVA` and `GUN_THUNDERCLAP` in `CardArt.GUNS`, in the sheet's colours. The M50.4m sheet has neither picture.
  - The two keys are not in `WeaponGlyph.KEYS` (they have no shot sprite), so the atlas digest does not move.
- **A full hand keeps its evolutions.** `Loadout._drop_weakest` runs when a give, F2, `--loadout` or the bench adds a weapon to 3 full slots.
  - It now counts an evolution's rank as past the cap (`_keep_rank`).
  - Before, an evolution held at rank 1 was the first weapon to go.
  - Play never reaches this, because offers never show a NEW weapon on a full hand.
- **Stress bench.**
  - `test_three_evolutions_at_launch_stay_in_the_budgets`: Minigun, Thunderclap and Supernova at launch with Haste 6 and Might 6, against 57 enemies for 10 s.
    - Player shots peak at 36 (cap 140). Enemy shots stay at or under 60.
    - Two shooters keep firing (14 windups), and a 16-shot ring spawns whole.
    - `enemy_shots_refused` stays 0.
  - `tools/bench.gd --auto --evolved[=ids]` is the windowed perf bench.
- **Deviations.**
  1. The gate script was copied from `.check/m50_4c1/gates.sh`, the newest one (the brief named `m48_5a`). It adds `g9_auto_vs_m50_4c1.log`, S9 Auto checked against the c1 ref.
  2. This branch has no S10, so the Thunderclap can only be reached through F2, the bench or tests (design: S10).
     - The Supernova needs nova 6 + Might in S9. No bot seed got there (nova 5 at most), so S9 shows no Supernova.
     - The design expected this: "rare, reported".
  3. `.check/ref_m50_4c2/s9_auto_hash.jsonl` was recorded, and it is byte-identical to `ref_m50_4c1`. S9 Auto is "equal", not a new ref (the design's cell: "new only if the bots reach Supernova").
  4. The pictures live in `card_art.gd`, not `grids.gd` (as in M50.4b, deviation 1). So the atlas digest does not move, although the design's T17 expected it to.
  5. The two pictures are not on the M50.4m sheet. They are drawn here in its colours.
  6. `_drop_weakest` keeping evolutions is not in the design. It is a fix found while building the capture.
- **Touch points for the merge (rework, Arcade).**
  - `components/loadout.gd`: `evolve` clocks, `reread`, `_keep_rank`.
  - `paths/path_runner.gd`: the nerf's `reread` and the note.
  - `weapons/nova_pulse.gd`.
  - `ui/level_card.gd` (one line), `ui/weapon_glyph.gd`, `systems/skins/art/card_art.gd`, `tools/bench.gd`.
  - `main.gd` (BUILD).
  - No session or line changes: `lines_stage2.md` is unchanged.
- **Numbers.**
  - **GUT**, one file at a time (no full suite): 21 files, 486/486 passing, 0 script errors (`.check/m50_4c2/gut_affected.txt`).
    - The new `test_supernova_thunderclap.gd` passes 11/11.
    - In `test_evolve.gd`, three pins were rewritten: 4 unbuilt rows; nova 6 + Might offers the Supernova; a held Shotgun + Area may now steer.
  - **Gates** (`.check/m50_4c2/gates.sh`), run twice. Run 2 is on the final tree (`gates_run2.txt`; run 1 is in `run1/`).
    - 86 summaries, 0 failures, sheet exit 0, and no script error in any log.
  - **Refs: every one is equal.**
    - S9 Auto against `ref_m50_4c1`.
    - S8 Auto against `ref_m50_4b`.
    - Arsenal against `ref_m50_1b`.
    - S7, Survival, v6, gauntlet, campaign, S4, pixel and skins-off, shell, handoff and pause.
  - **Perf**, windowed (`bench --auto --evolved`): 55 enemies, Minigun + Thunderclap + Supernova at launch, Haste 6, Might 6.
    - Physics average 5.31 ms, p95 6.16 ms, max 6.16 ms (target: p95 <= 8 ms).
    - 113 player shots (the bench's synthetic fill included), 0 refused on either side.
    - For comparison: the Minigun alone, p95 4.38 ms; M45b's default loadout, p95 5.85 ms.
- **Bots** (8 seeds; the design asks for 16; `.check/m50_4c2/bots/`). These are bot numbers; nothing was retuned.

  | Set | Result | Against M50.4c1 |
  |---|---|---|
  | Invincible S9 Auto | 8/8 wins; evolved 8/8, median 91.8 s; nerf landed 8/8; `nerfed 1` before the end 8/8; max level 17 | 3 seeds pick differently (nova is steered once Might is held: nova up to 5); no Supernova |
  | `human_pace` S9 Auto | evolved 8/8, median 90.4 s; EVOLVE in the first half of the kills 5/8 | 3 seeds differ; still 5/8 |

  | Mortal S9 Arsenal / Survival | 3/8 / 1/8 deaths | equal |

- **Captures.** Windowed, inert save.
  - The scripts:
    - `.check/m50_4c2/capture_c2.gd`: S9 Auto, seed 1, the Pixel look.
    - `card_preview.gd`: the panel on its own.
  - `preview_{supernova,thunderclap}_{flat,pixel}.png`: the gold EVOLVE card.
    - "NOVA 6 MAX + MIGHT -> SUPERNOVA", with a star in its ring.
    - "SHOTGUN 6 MAX + AREA -> THUNDERCLAP", with the shotgun and its blast.
    - The banners read "pick 1 of 3" and "pick 1 of 4".
  - `evolve_supernova.png`: in play at 95 s, after the Minigun's nerf, with nova 6 + Might set (F2-style).
    - LEVEL 10 with three cards plus the EVOLVE Supernova.

  - `thunderclap_clap.png` and `rail_three_evo.png`: the Supernova's blast and a clap blast on screen, and three EVO slots in the rail. The name "THUNDERCLAP" fills its rail slot from edge to edge.

The Thunderclap now reads "Balance: Thunderclap damage 4 -> 2, blast 1.65 T -> 1.1 T. It was too strong." (Area 1). The Minigun and Supernova notes are unchanged. Test `test_the_thunderclap_nerf_note_names_the_blast_not_the_rate` (test_supernova_thunderclap) fails without the fix. Not upheld: 1 finding.

### 6.31 As built (M50.4f): Auto cards match the mockup

Built on `build/auto` (M50.4c2). Source: the approved mockup `.check/mockups/cards/Auto_Choices.dc.html` (owner: "I like mockup let's build it") and `Arcade_Cards.dc.html` for the mechanic vocabulary. Presentation only: no gameplay, RNG, save or telemetry change.

- **Pixel card window** (`ui/level_card.gd`, `CardArt.draw_card_art` / `card_px`). The card art is drawn at the window's own scale (cell size from the window, 2-7 px, about 90 % fill) instead of the rail-slot size. The window has the sheet's backdrop: the lower floor band, two faint stars and a gold one; a passive's window has the faint centre column. The laser's pink beam leaves the muzzle (`CardArt.MUZZLE_ROW`), and Haste gets its four speed lines.
- **MAX variant.** `data_for` sets `max` when a rank-up reaches the path's cap. Pixel: a gold MAX tag where NEW goes, the gold badge and a gold ring around the card when it is not focused. Flat: a gold MAX pill and a gold edge (`card_evolve`). The fresh-pip glow is skipped on a MAX card (all pips lit).
- **Pips.** Tall pips (Pixel) and diamonds (flat) mark the mechanic ranks of a weapon card (`mechanic_rank`: the deep curve's even ranks, the classic curve's 2-3). Passives have none. The fresh pip keeps its ring plus a steady soft glow. The focused weapon card carries the sheet's "tall pips = new mechanic" note.
- **Effect line.** A rank that brings a mechanic (`LevelCard.gained_mechanic`) draws that trait's 8 x 8 icon (`CardArt.MECHS`: overcharge, heavy round, seeker, chain, burn line, ricochet, knockback cone, point blank, shrapnel, sweep; a gold spark for the rest), then the trait's name in bold with a colon, then the text (a describe() that repeats the name drops it). Pixel 16 px, flat 15 px; the paragraph steps down until it fits its lines and its height.
- **Pair row.** The paired gun (Pixel grid / flat gun), "pairs with Pistol" in ink and a pink READY tag when the offer's EVOLVE uses this item as its catalyst.
- **Key row.** The focused card's chip is gold (flat: cyan) and its word is "focused"; the others "choose"; EVOLVE keeps "evolve".
- **Flat guns** (`WeaponGlyph.draw_card_flat` / `draw_gun_flat`, `FLAT_GUNS`): pistol, shotgun, laser, launcher and minigun in rounded blocks and polygons (Palette roles `dust`, `wall`, `hit`, `card_evolve`, `card_item`), on the flat cards, the flat EVOLVE card's parent tile and window, and the flat slot bar. Haste draws a filled bolt with speed dashes. Other icons keep their glyphs.
- **EVOLVE Pixel card.** The window is 104 px tall with the floor band and stars (the placeholder boxes are gone); the minigun is drawn at 4 px cells (fills the window's height). The line wraps to at most 2 lines at 14 px inside the card, above the gold studs. The focus ring is a 4 px pink ring outside the gold rim (ink-edged), so the ribbon cannot hide it. Flat EVOLVE: a pink ring outside the gold rim when focused.
- **LEVEL banner.** Ink-outlined pink ribbon tabs with a dark lip and a plum notch, a small ink stem under the plate, a gold highlight on the plate.
- **The rail's ITEMS module** (`RailState._preview`, `PickPanel.preview` / `focused_choice`). While the dressed level-up is open: the bar is full with "UP!" in gold, and the bottom line is the per-key legend ("[1] laser new . [2] haste 3 > 4 . [3] pistol -> minigun"). The focused choice lifts its slot (3 px, gold ring) and its next pip glows (steady); a NEW card lifts the free well (gold "+", pink NEW tag); a focused EVOLVE rings the base weapon in pink and ticks its catalyst.
- **Flat slot bar** (`PickPanel.SlotBar`, `RailState.items_for`). In the flat look the dressed panel shows "Items n + m" under the cards: one card per slot (LV, the flat gun or glyph, the name, the dots; MAX pill), the same focus lift (cyan ring, next dot glowing), EVOLVE's pink ring, then "LV n", the full bar, "Level up" and the legend. The space under the panel shrinks from 60 to 12 px so the LEVEL banner stays clear of the timer. `main.gd` sets `pick_panel.runner`.
- **Deviations.**
  1. READY shows only when the offer's EVOLVE uses this item (the item is evolve-ready), not whenever the paired weapon is held. The two findings disagreed; the mockup's READY sits beside an EVOLVE choice. Holding the weapon still shows the gun and the ink text (dim when not held).
  2. The title keeps the Pixelify font (no VT323-like font in the repo).
  3. The EVOLVE card stays 316 px tall: the minigun is drawn at 4 px cells (the window's height), not 5.
  4. The mechanic icons are 8 x 8 grids in `card_art.gd` drawn for this branch (the Arcade module's glyphs are not in `build/auto`).
  5. Card size stays 232 x 356 (M50.4b deviation 8).
- **Numbers.** Hash gates `.check/m50_4f/gates.sh` (copied from M50.4c2): every summary 0 failures and `g0` exit 0, so no ref changed (S9 Auto still equal to `ref_m50_4c1`, S8 Auto to `ref_m50_4b`). GUT 1194/1194 (suite 278.6 s). `check.ps1 -BotRuns`: CHECK PASSED.
- **Tests** (`test_auto_items.gd`): `test_m50_4f_max_mechanic_and_ready_card_data` (MAX, the mechanic, tall-pip ranks, READY, the pair icon, mechanic grid colours) and `test_m50_4f_focus_lifts_the_slot_and_the_rail_reads_the_pick` (each focus reads "focused" on its card only; the rail reading has `picking`, one legend entry per choice, one lifted slot with its next pip for an upgrade, the free well for a NEW card; nothing after the pick; the flat slot bar exists). Both fail without the change (the fields and functions are new).
- **Captures** (`.check/m50_4f/`, windowed, inert save; the shots before the change are in `before/`): `preview_{flat,pixel}.png`, `preview_evolve_{flat,pixel}_{2,3}.png`, `pick_weapon.png`, `pick_item{,_pixel}.png`, `pick_filler.png`, `evolve_choice{,_flat}.png`, `audit_rail/focus_{1,2,3}.png`, `rf_focus_*.png` (reduce flashing: identical, nothing animates).
- **`BUILD = "M50.4f"`.**
- **Review (17 found, 16 upheld, all fixed):** the 16 upheld mockup mismatches above (card art scale and backdrop; MAX variant; tall pips and diamonds; mechanic icon and bold name; pair gun and READY; "focused" key, reported twice; flat guns; EVOLVE text, studs and backdrop; banner tabs; rail slot lift; rail pink EVOLVE ring; rail XP strip; flat slot bar; EVOLVE focus ring; MAX and READY, reported again). Not upheld: 1.
### 6.32 As built (M50.2a): packs, tuned (Arcade through S10, design 1)
*Built in the `build/arcade` worktree on M48.5a (main 8a0bc64 + the rail), from `.check/arcade_s10/design.md` rev. 2 section 1 and the owner's overnight answer ("tune down: rare 0.20 -> 0.15, no rare duplicate in every pack, then x6 -> x5 if still too strong; max rank about 12-16 by the end of S9"). The Stage 1 rework (M47.6a-d2) is **not** in this tree: measured on today's S8 / S9 (fixed length, 2:00 / 2:30). Not committed.*
- **Rarity:** `paths/arsenal.tres` `rare_chance` 0.20 -> 0.15. No pity, no epic.
- **G2' (the fallback roll, `BoosterPack._take`):** a card slot with no unshown id left adds **no card**. When that slot's own rarity roll says rare (`roll < rare_chance`), the first shown common card (card order) becomes rare in place (`BoosterPack.card(..., true)`: +2 for a held card, rank 3 for a NEW one); a common roll, or an all-rare pack, changes nothing. A pack never shows an id twice (the old "common next to its own rare" is gone). Still exactly 6 `pack_rng` draws per pack in the same order. Packs: pool of one = 1 card (rare 1 - 0.85^3 ~ 39 %), a 2-weapon pool on a full hand = 2 cards (~0.45 rares), 3+ weapons = 3 cards. G3 / G4 unchanged.
- **The 1-card pack is a toast (D3):** `PathRunner.toast_pack()` (one card, no FUSE, an upgrade or a NEW card with room) skips the panel and the `pick` pause: the card applies at once through the same `_pick()` the panel's choice uses (taste nudge, `pick` log, `pick_made`, `picks_on_path` for `picked n`, the `PACK_GAP` wait for an owed pack), `pick_offered` still fires, and `PickPanel.toast()` shows "Pack: Pistol +1" ("+2  RARE" in the `card_rare` gold, "Laser 1 joins") at the game's bottom right, above the rail's PACK module, for 1.6 s real time with a 0.4 s fade (no shine or flash in any mode). A plain label until M50.2c dresses it as a card face. No `require_fresh_press` (no key picked it).
- **Telemetry (additive):** the path record gains `packs_short` (packs under 3 cards), `packs_toast` (1-card toasts) and `rares_shown` (rare cards shown). Schemas unchanged.
- **Bot:** `human_pace` reads `pack_every + 3` (`HUMAN_PACE_EXTRA`, was the literal 9). The per-run line adds `packs_short= packs_toast= rares_shown=` at its end; a new `[botrun] packs:` line gives rares per pack and the short / toast counts per seed.
- **Tuning steps 2-3 (design 1.3) not applied:** `pack_every` 7 needs an S9 invincible median max rank above 17 (got 15). The x6 -> x5 trigger is a pace band on S9 **clear** times, which this tree does not have (S9 is a fixed 2:30 until the rework's `clear` ends); the stand-in, S9 invincible kills in 150 s, reads Arsenal 138 (median) vs Survival 134 and Auto 222 (Auto kills a bigger, gem-fed crowd), so nothing points at "too strong by pace". `WeaponCurve.MULT` is untouched. Re-measure the band on the merged rework tree (its sets were sized for equal clears).
- **Deviations:** (1) built on today's S8 / S9, not on the rework (refs from `ref_m50_1b` / `ref_m48b` / `ref_m47*`, not `ref_m47_6d`); (2) bots on 8 seeds, not 16; (3) the pace band is reported through kills, not clear times (above); (4) the toast sits at the game view's bottom right (inside the game), not drawn inside the rail's PACK cell; (5) no PLAYTEST.md entry (the next sheet carries P3).
- **Touch points with the Stage 1 rework (for the merge):** `tools/check.ps1` (the hosted-S8 block's `Test-Path` and `--compare` now read `ref_m50_2a`; after the rework they and the S7 / S8 / S9 Arsenal refs must be re-recorded on the merged tree); `tools/bot_runs.gd` (the `human_pace` setter, the arsenal per-run line and the new `packs:` line: merge by hunk with the rework's `end= spawned= start=`); `paths/path_runner.gd` `_open_offer()` / `_pick()` (the rework does not touch them). No session `.tres` changed.
- **Tests:** test_arsenal_depth (34/34): new `test_fallback_upgrades_first_common_on_a_rare_roll`, `test_pool_of_one_is_one_card`, `test_rare_chance_is_0_15`, `test_one_card_pack_is_a_toast_not_a_pause`; G1/G2 test renamed for G2' (1 card / 2 cards); the packs-queue and rare-frame tests announce the shotgun (a pool of one is a toast now) and force rares by `rare_chance` 0.99. test_arsenal (36/36): `_pick` takes the runner's last offer on a toast; the fresh-press test announces the shotgun; `rare_chance` 0.15. Each new test fails without the change (M48.5a adds a duplicate card, opens the panel on a pool of one, reads 0.2). Also run: test_fusion 22/22 (with the review test), test_s9_paint 22/22, test_s8_day_one 21/21, test_s7_tryout 29/29, test_paths 41/41, test_rail_ui 17/17, test_always_on 39/39, test_answers 19/19, test_auto_scale 21/21, test_dev_session 41/41, test_front_door 26/26, test_hud_layout 10/10, test_pause_menu 13/13, test_pixel_skin 30/30, test_settings 19/19, test_stage2_door 21/21, test_survival 23/23, test_playtest_build 8/8, test_campaign 48/48 (full suite not run).
- **Hash gates** (`.check/m50_2a/gates.sh`, the M48.5a matrix): 102 logs, all 0 failures (g0 sheet exit 0). Equal: v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign 16/16 (ref_m43.3, also `--look=pixel` and hosted at both view sizes), S4 (ref_m42.2), S8 / S9 Auto and Survival (ref_m47, ref_m47_2, ref_m48b; replayed, `--skins=off`, `--look=pixel`, hosted), pause determinism S4 and S8 x 3 paths (8 seeds, both patterns, bases on this tree), handoff 120. **New refs `.check/ref_m50_2a/`** `s7_hash.jsonl` (4 seeds), `s8_arsenal_hash.jsonl`, `s9_arsenal_hash.jsonl` (2 seeds), each replayed twice plus `--look=pixel`, `--skins=off` (S9), hosted `--shell` at both sizes and `--look=pixel` hosted; against ref_m50_1b they differ as intended (S7 3/4, S8 Arsenal 2/2, S9 Arsenal 2/2: `.check/m50_2a/x_*.log`).
- **Bots (bot numbers, seeds 1-8; logs `.check/m50_2a/bot_*.log`):** S9 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won; max rank 15,16,15,18,13,22,14,11 (**median 15**, M50.1b 11-23 with a 20 on seed 1); packs 22-23, gap median 5.9 s; rares per pack 0.49; packs under 3 cards 18-20 per seed; fused 8/8, median 106.5 s (98.5-109.5); misfuse restored 8/8. `human` and `human_pace` (identical lines): max rank 9-12 (median 10), fused 8/8, median 110.5 s. S9 mortal deaths: **Arsenal 4/8** (hits projectile 31, contact 3, explosion 1; M50.1b 3/8), Auto 5/8, Survival 1/8 (guard: Arsenal <= Survival + 3 and <= Auto + 2 holds). S8 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won, packs 18-20, max rank 19-29 (M50.1b 31-39), rares per pack 0.45, toasts 2-3 per seed (the pool-of-one packs before the 30 s shotgun); mortal 2/8 deaths (as M50.1b). S7 4/4 swaps and picks as before. Pack band (median gap 4-12 s) in band 8/8 everywhere.
- **Captures** (`.check/m50_2a/capture.gd`, windowed 1600x900, inert save, seed 1, invincible bot): `s8_toast.png` (no look: "Pack: Pistol +2  RARE" in gold at the game's bottom right, the panel shut, the hand Pistol 3) and `s8_toast_fading.png`; `s8_toast_pixel.png` / `s8_toast_fading_pixel.png` (the rail: the toast sits just above the PACK module, PACK 1/6 then 2/6); `s8_pack2_pixel.png` (40 s: a 2-card pack, Pistol 6 -> 7 and Shotgun 1 -> 2, no repeated pistol card; M48.5a's capture at 57 s showed Pistol 11 -> 12 next to Pistol 11 -> 13 RARE); `s9_pack_pixel.png` (59 s, after `skin ui`: Pistol 9 -> 10 and Shotgun 3 -> 4).
- **`BUILD = "M50.2a"`.**
- **Review (1 finding, 1 upheld, all fixed):** after FUSE, `BoosterPack.recompute()` deduped by (id, rarity), so a common parent card next to a rare one (the usual full-hand 2-card pack on a recipe pair) came back as "Prism r -> r+1" next to "Prism r -> r+2 RARE", breaking G2'. Fixed: recompute dedupes by the folded id alone; a second card for a shown id is never added, and the shown card turns rare in place when either was rare (G2''s own rule). New `test_fusion.test_recompute_folds_a_common_and_a_rare_parent_into_one_rare_card` (both orders, plus two commons -> 1 card), and the fuse-reopen test now expects `pistol/common, prism/rare` (it read the old three-card pack); both fail without the fix. S8 / S9 Arsenal hashes stay equal to `ref_m50_2a` (the hash bot's seeds never hit that pack).
- **Still open (defaults taken):** P3 2-card packs are now the norm in S8 / S9 (the owner said 3 cards) and a 1-card pack is a toast (D3); P1 `pack_every` 7 was not needed. Both in 9.

### 6.33 As built (M50.2b): the S9 laser give step (Arcade through S10, design 2)
*Built in the `build/arcade` worktree on M50.2a, from `.check/arcade_s10/design.md` rev. 2 section 2 (2.1-2.4) and the owner's overnight answer ("It opens the 'replace which?' step, or the player keeps their hand"). The Stage 1 rework (M47.6a-d2) and Auto depth are **not** in this tree: S9 is still a fixed 2:30. Not committed.*
- **The give step** (`paths/path_runner.gd` `give()`, `_open_give()`, `_on_give_done()`; `paths/pick_offer.gd` `give_step`; `ui/pick_panel.gd` `open_give()`, `give_text()`, signal `give_done`): a `give` on a full hand while the packs are live is logged `give_offered` and opens, on the first tick where `can_open_now()` holds and never on the misfuse's last tick (`_misfuse_end_tick`), before any owed pack, the replace step on the given card alone: "A gold card: Laser 3." over the card's face (gold frame for rank 3+, NEW / RARE tags, its have line), then "[1] Replace Pistol 12", "[2] Replace Shotgun 9 (it links with the Laser)", "[3] Keep your hand". No slot is locked; the card that makes a built recipe with the given one has the hint in a lit frame, still a choice; the recipe's name (": Prism") shows only once the `fusion` basic is live. Replace uses `Loadout.replace_slot` (slot and active slot kept); Keep leaves the weapon in the live pool (a later pack shows it as NEW). It draws nothing from `pack_rng`, emits no `pick_offered` / `picked`, leaves `_want` and the PackMeter alone, pauses with `pick`, has the 0.4 s input delay and `require_fresh_press()`; an owed pack waits `PACK_GAP` after it. If the hand changed before the step opens (room again, the weapon held, or a fusion it feeds) the give applies at once as `give()` would. Before the `cards` basic the old replace stays (`give_replaced`). **G4 is retired from the runner** (`_give_wait`, `GIVE_PACKS`, `give_waiting()` gone; `give_pending()` / `give_steps()` / `paired()` added); `BoosterPack.draw()` keeps its `give` argument, marked test-only. New `BoosterPack.give_card()`.
- **Grammar:** cue when `give_choice n` (the n-th give step closed, any choice; held `SAY_GAP` after the previous said line, like `fused`); line conditions `give_kept`, `give_taken` (this attempt's `give_choice` log events), `paired` (a live hand fact, `BoosterPack.recipe_pair` on the hand, independent of the `fusion` basic; added to a say cue's context by `SessionRunner.cue_context()`, never in the debrief's) and `made:<fusion id>` (from `fused` events; a non-fusion id is a parse problem). `SessionDef.problems()`: `give_choice` needs the `cards` basic and an Arsenal-only cue; a say line reading give_kept / give_taken / paired / made: must be Arsenal-only; none of them in an intro (`reads_fusion` grows, `reads_hand`); `paired` never in a debrief.
- **S9 (Arsenal only, appended; no cue reordered):** the 86 s line now reads "New gun: the laser. Gold, rank three. Take it, or keep your hand."; `give_choice 1` lines on `give_kept` ("Keeping your hand? Fair. The laser's in the packs now."), `give_taken&paired` ("Laser and shotgun. Those two go together."), `give_taken&!paired` ("Laser and pistol. No recipe for those two. Yet."); the 94 s fusion line is now `paired`, with a new `!paired` line ("...The laser and the shotgun make something. Hold both."); debrief `answer:path_arsenal&!fused` ("No fusion today. The recipe's still there."). `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **Fix found by the tests:** `SessionRunner._fire` set `_last_say` (the `SAY_GAP` clock) for every say cue, said or not; with three `give_choice 1` lines (one per branch) the first, silent one held the other two back 4 s. Only a line actually said starts the gap now (only `fused` / `fuse_ready` / `give_choice` read it).
- **Bot** (`tools/bot_runs.gd`): `BotRuns.give_choice(panel, lo, probe)` (replace the card that is not the given card's recipe partner, the lowest rank when nothing links; `human` follows the hint the same way), called from `choose_pack()` so `tools/run_hash.gd` shares it; probes `give_keep`, `give_swap`; the per-run line ends with `give=<choice>@t`, the `fusion:` line adds `give=`.
- **Telemetry (additive, schemas unchanged):** run-log events `give_offered`, `give_choice` `{weapon, rank, choice, t}`; the path record gains `gives` and `pack_picks.give_kept`.
- **Deviations:** (1) built on today's S8 / S9 (fixed 2:30, no `clear`): the "median fuse <= median clear - 10 s" gate reads the session's 150 s end, and SR's "S9 median clear >= 110 s" cannot be measured here; (2) the hint reads "(it links with the Laser)" on its own line (the design's "links with the Laser"; "it" says which card links); (3) S9 give-step tests live in the new `test_give_step.gd` (plus a data test in `test_s9_paint.gd`), and the old `_give_wait` test in `test_fusion.gd` became a pack replace-step test; (4) the give card's face is a plain gold-framed panel until M50.2c dresses it; (5) no PLAYTEST.md entry (the next sheet carries P2).
- **Touch points with the Stage 1 rework (for the merge):** `campaign/sessions/s2_03_paint.tres` (appended cues and one debrief line, two line texts changed at 86 / 94 s: re-append after SR's M47.6d2 version and regenerate `lines_stage2.md`); `campaign/session_runner.gd` (`_physics_process` ctx key `give_choices`, `_waits`, the say branch of `_fire` and `cue_context()`); `tools/bot_runs.gd` (probes, `choose_pack`, the per-run and `fusion:` lines: merge by hunk with SR's `end= spawned= start=`); `tests/gut/test_s9_paint.gd` (one appended test). Touch (Auto depth): `path_runner.gd` `give()` only changed its pack-path branch (the `_first` branch is byte-identical).
- **Tests:** new `tests/gut/test_give_step.gd` (12): the step opens instead of G4 (no `pack_rng` draw, `_want` / packs / `pick_offered` / `picked` untouched, `give_done` emitted); Replace 1 / Replace 2 / Keep (slot, rank, active slot, pool, next pack can show it NEW); no lock, the hint with and without the recipe name; a pending give before an owed pack, which waits `PACK_GAP`; never over the misfuse, opening the tick after its undo; a give that finds room joins; the bot's choice and probes; the grammar and conditions from the run log; `SessionDef.problems()` cases; `draw()`'s test-only give; S9 at 86 s on each branch with its line (Replace Pistol: the `give_taken&paired` line before the `fusion` basic, then the `paired` 94 s line and a forced FUSE; Replace Shotgun: the `!paired` lines and the shotgun back as NEW; Keep: its line). Edited: test_arsenal (G4 assert -> `give_pending`), test_fusion (the replace-step keep test opens a forced pack), test_s9_paint (+1 data test). Each new test fails on M50.2a (no give step, no grammar); the S9 line tests also failed before the `_last_say` fix. GUT (one file at a time): test_give_step 12/12, test_arsenal 36/36, test_fusion 22/22, test_arsenal_depth 34/34, test_s9_paint 23/23, test_s8_day_one 21/21, test_campaign 48/48, test_reactions 24/24, test_stage2_door 21/21, test_paths 41/41, test_playtest_build 8/8, test_rail_ui 17/17, test_always_on 39/39 (full suite not run).
- **Hash gates** (`.check/m50_2b/gates.sh`, the M50.2a matrix): 80 logs, all 0 failures, g0 sheet exit 0. Equal: v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign (ref_m43.3, also `--look=pixel` and hosted at both view sizes), S4 (ref_m42.2), S7 and S8 Arsenal (ref_m50_2a; replayed twice, `--look=pixel`, hosted), S8 / S9 Auto and Survival (ref_m47, ref_m47_2, ref_m48b), pause determinism S4 and S8 x 3 paths, handoff 120. **New ref `.check/ref_m50_2b/s9_arsenal_hash.jsonl`** (2 seeds), replayed twice plus `--skins=off`, `--look=pixel`, hosted `--shell` (both sizes, also `--skins=off` and `--look=pixel`); against ref_m50_2a it differs as intended (2/2, `.check/m50_2b/x_s9_arsenal_vs_m50_2a.log`).
- **Bots (bot numbers; logs `.check/m50_2b/bot_*.log`), S9 Arsenal invincible, 16 seeds each, per give branch (gate: default >= 14/16, human >= 12/16, give_swap / give_keep >= 12/16, median fuse <= 140 s):** default (replace pistol) fused **16/16**, median **99.1 s** (94.3-104.1; M50.2a 106.5 on 8 seeds); `human` (human_pace + the hint) 16/16, 103.3 s; `give_swap` (replace shotgun) 16/16, 115.0 s (107.3-126.2); `give_keep` 16/16, 110.4 s (98.5-120.6). G1b not needed. Max rank at the end (invincible) median **17.5** (11-22; M50.2a 15 on seeds 1-8, the same seeds now 17.5), human 14, give_swap 13, give_keep 15. Packs 22-23 per seed, gap median 5.3 s, rares per pack 0.46. **Mortal S9, 16 seeds: Arsenal 12/16 deaths** (hits projectile 61, contact 7; fused 10/16), Survival 2/16, Auto 10/16 (8 seeds: Arsenal 6/8 vs M50.2a 4/8; the same seed 1 dies at 34.8 s, before the give). The design's reported guard "Arcade <= Survival + 3" misses (12 vs 5), "<= Auto + 2" holds (12 vs 12). S8 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won, packs 18-20, max rank 19-29, no give step (no `give=`), as M50.2a.
- **Captures** (`.check/m50_2b/capture.gd`, windowed 1600x900, inert save, S9 Arsenal seed 1, invincible bot): `s9_give.png` / `s9_give_keep.png` (86.0 s, the rail on: "A gold card: Laser 3." over the gold-framed card "GOLD CARD / Laser 3 NEW RARE / replaces a card: burning line 1 s", then "[1] Replace Pistol 15" (focused), "[2] Replace Shotgun 5 (it links with the Laser)" in a lit frame, "[3] Keep your hand"; no recipe name before 94 s); `s9_give_after.png` (Replace Pistol: at ~106 s a pack with FUSE "Laser 5 + Shotgun 6 -> Prism 6", the HAND module's PRISM READY strip); `s9_give_keep_after.png` (Keep: at ~106 s the laser is back as "Laser 1 NEW" in a pack next to Pistol 20 and Shotgun 5 -> 7 RARE). The rail chat runs well behind the game in both (it shows the 55 s line at ~106 s), so the give lines are not in the shots yet (the queue, not this change).
- **`BUILD = "M50.2b"`.**
- **Review (2 findings, 0 upheld, all fixed):** no finding was upheld, so no code changed after the review; the full GUT suite, every gate and `check.ps1 -BotRuns` were re-run on the final tree.
- **Still open (defaults taken):** P2 the gold card's "take it or keep your hand" and the unlocked hint (default: no lock); the S9 max rank (17.5, above 12-16: the design's `pack_every` 7 trigger, "above 17", now fires on 16 seeds; not applied here, as it moves the S7 / S8 refs that M50.2b keeps equal); the mortal Arsenal deaths. Both in 9.

### 6.34 As built (M50.2c): pack opening and card art (Arcade through S10, design 2.5)
*Built in the `build/arcade` worktree on M50.2b, from `.check/arcade_s10/design.md` rev. 2 section 2.5 and the owner's overnight ask ("a mockup of the booster pack opening, and real card assets instead of plain rectangles"), following the mockups `.check/mockups/cards/Arcade_Pack.dc.html` and `Arcade_Cards.dc.html`. The Stage 1 rework (M47.6a-d2) and Auto depth are **not** in this tree. Presentation only. Not committed.*
- **Card faces** (`ui/card_face.gd`, new `CardFace`): a pack card is drawn as a card: frame by rarity (common; rare in gold foil with a pink ring and a "RARE +2" / "RARE" ribbon), the top band in the weapon's colour (by icon key), the art box with `WeaponGlyph.draw` (the look's shot sprite in Pixel), the rank badge in the rail's pixel digits, the tag ("NEW", "6 > 7"), the name bar, rank pips (held ranks gold, this card's ranks pink, then "+N"), two lines of gain text, the key ("[1]") and a rarity gem. FUSE is its own face (`card_fuse`: stepped prism bands, the band split in the two parents' colours, both parents' pictures, "[4]  Laser 5 + Shotgun 5" at the foot, the kept mechanics and "loses:" as its text, no "+N"). The give step shows the gold card (`card_give`, "GOLD CARD" ribbon) over its three choices; the replace step now shows the waiting card's face over "Replace x / Back". The hand under a pack and the 1-card toast use small faces (the toast: the rail's `pack` icon from `RailArt.PACK`, read not copied, then a small card with its rank, inside the toast so it fades with the line).
- **Input unchanged:** each face is the visible part of a frameless `Button` (same `_buttons` order, keys `choose_1..4`, focus, click; the face draws focus as a lift). A level-up offer that is not a pack (Auto, S7) keeps today's plain buttons.
- **Tier 0 vs Pixel (D16):** before S9's `skin ui` (`Skins.is_on(&"ui_panel")` false) the face is a rounded flat card in `CardArt.FLAT` colours (the mockup's flat half), with the `card_rare` role for the rare border; once the UI panel is on (S9 55 s, `--look=pixel`) the Pixel frames and colours.
- **Card art** (`systems/skins/art/card_art.gd`, new `CardArt`): a separate art script baked by `ArtSkin.bake` (6 grids, 16x16 and 8x8: `card_common`, `card_rare`, `card_fuse`, `card_give`, `card_back`, `ribbon`), drawn as 9-slices at 3x, plus the PIXEL / FLAT / BAND colour tables (systems/skins/ is the colour lint's home). Its digest is pinned in `test_card_art.gd` (`e4f73d12...`); the arena atlas (`grids.gd`, `d07379b4...`) does not move and holds no card id. `card_rare` is still read from Palette at draw time, so M50.3a's ROLES entry needs no rebake.
- **The deal:** the cards are dealt face-down (the card back: the blob's gold smile) and flip left to right, `REVEAL_STEP` 0.08 s apart, each in `FLIP_TIME` 0.1 s, with a 0.12 s white landing flash; the last of 4 is face-up at 0.34 s, inside the unchanged 0.4 s `INPUT_DELAY`, so no choice unlocks earlier or later. A rare or gold card's shine crosses it every 1.8 s while the panel waits. Reduce flashing: no flip, no flash, no shine, a fade-in on the same schedule. After FUSE the new pack is dealt again; Back from the replace step is not.
- **Deviations:** (1) the mockup's five beats (drop 0.30 s, tear 0.25 s, fan + flip 0.40 s, the pick, slot-in 0.35 s) are cut to the flip beat inside the 0.4 s input delay, as design 2.5 asks (no input or bot timing change): no sealed pack drops or tears and no slot-in after the pick (the game resumes at once); (2) the art box shows `WeaponGlyph` (in Pixel the weapon's shot sprite, as the rail's HAND module does) instead of the mockup's gun sprites, which would be new atlas grids (the design keeps the atlas digest equal until M50.3a); (3) the mockup's per-mechanic icons on the gain strip are text; (4) faces are 240x316 game px (the mockup's 176x232 at 1.36), because the game view is drawn scaled down beside the rail; (5) no PLAYTEST.md entry (the next sheet carries P8).
- **Touch points with the Stage 1 rework (for the merge):** `ui/pick_panel.gd` only (the rework does not touch it); no session data, runner or gate change. Touch (Auto depth): the non-pack branch of `pick_panel.gd` `_build()` still makes plain buttons, so Auto's level-up panel is unchanged; an Auto card face (`Auto_Choices.dc.html`) can reuse `CardFace` later.
- **Tests:** new `tests/gut/test_card_art.gd` (7, one from the review): every kind has a face (styles, tags, ribbons, FUSE parents) and draws on tier 0 (code glyphs) and Pixel (sprite glyphs); CardArt bakes clean, twice equal, digest pinned, the atlas digest unchanged with no card id; the deal ends inside the input delay, the lock is still exactly the input delay, every face is up at unlock; reduce flashing deals with a fade only (no flip, flash or shine, also on the panel); choices, keys, FUSE as `choose_4`, the replace step (face + Replace x2 + Back) and a non-pack offer's plain buttons are unchanged; the give step's gold card and the toast's small card. They fail on M50.2b (no CardFace / CardArt). Edited: `test_arsenal_depth.test_pack_panel_text_and_the_rare_frame` reads the rare face's style and ribbon (it read a PanelContainer's border). GUT (one file at a time): test_card_art 6/6, test_arsenal_depth 34/34, test_give_step 12/12, test_fusion 22/22, test_arsenal 36/36, test_paths 41/41, test_pixel_skin 30/30 (colour, image and RNG lints), test_playtest_build 8/8, test_settings 19/19, test_pause_menu 13/13, test_s9_paint 23/23, test_s8_day_one 21/21, test_always_on 39/39, test_rail_ui 17/17, test_auto_scale 21/21, test_mutations 19/19, test_big_slot 20/20 (full suite not run).
- **Hash gates** (`.check/m50_2c/gates.sh`, the M50.2b matrix): 81 summaries, all 0 failures, g0 sheet exit 0. Equal: v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign (ref_m43.3, also `--look=pixel` and hosted at both view sizes), S4 (ref_m42.2), S7 and S8 Arsenal (ref_m50_2a), **S9 Arsenal (ref_m50_2b)** replayed twice plus `--skins=off`, `--look=pixel` and hosted, S8 / S9 Auto and Survival (ref_m47, ref_m47_2, ref_m48b), pause determinism S4 and S8 x 3 paths, handoff 120. No new ref.
- **Bots (bot numbers, seeds 1-8; logs `.check/m50_2c/bot_*.log`), the same numbers as M50.2b (presentation only):** S9 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won, fused 8/8, median 99.1 s, give replace:pistol @86.0, packs 22-23, gap median 5.3 s, rares per pack 0.45, max rank 11-22 (median 17); mortal 6/8 deaths (hits projectile 30, contact 3), fused 6/8. S8 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won, toasts 2-3 per seed, rares per pack 0.45; mortal 2/8 deaths (projectile 23, contact 1).
- **Captures** (`.check/m50_2c/capture.gd`, windowed 1600x900, inert save, seed 1, invincible bot; the deal shots wait on the deal's own clock, 0.13 s, because `--fixed-fps 60` runs about 4x real time): `s8_pack_deal.png` / `s8_pack.png` (tier 0, 40 s: Pistol 6 > 7 lands with its flash while Shotgun 1 > 2 is mid-flip; then both flat cards with gold rank circles, pips, gain lines, "[1]" / "[2]", the hand as two small cards); `s8_toast.png` (tier 0) and `s8_toast_pixel.png` (the pack icon, a small rare card with rank 3, "Pack: Pistol +2  RARE"); `s9_pack_deal.png` / `s9_pack.png` and `s9_pack_deal_reduce.png` / `s9_pack_reduce.png` (Pixel, 64 s; reduced: the Shotgun card half faded in, no flip, no flash); `s9_give.png` (86 s: the gold card LASER rank 3 with the GOLD CARD ribbon over Replace Pistol 15, Replace Shotgun 5 (it links with the Laser), Keep your hand); `s9_fuse_deal.png` / `s9_fuse.png` (99.6 s: Shotgun 5 > 6, Pistol 3 NEW RARE with its shine, Laser 5 > 6, FUSE Prism 5 in the prism frame with "[4] Laser 5 + Shotgun 5"; the deal shot shows card 1 landing, card 2 mid-flip, cards 3-4 face-down); `s9_replace.png` (95 s: "Your hand is full. Pistol 1 replaces:" over the Pistol NEW face, Replace Laser 3 / Replace Shotgun 5 / Back to the pack).
- **Review (1 findings, 1 upheld, all fixed):** (1) the FUSE face cut its text to two 12 px lines, so only the first kept mechanic showed and the "loses:" list never did (the irreversible choice hid its cost). Fixed: on `card_fuse` the art box is shorter (50 instead of 96 card units), the pips are dropped (the rank is the badge), and the kept mechanics, one per line, then "loses: ..." on its own line in red, fill a box from under the name bar to the foot, the font shrinking (`CardFace.gain_fit`, down to `MIN_FS` 6) until the whole text fits; the FUSE button's tooltip is the full `fuse_text`. New `test_card_art.test_the_fuse_face_shows_every_kept_mechanic_and_the_losses` (4 keeps + 3 losses on tier 0 and Pixel: every line drawn, the wrapped height fits its box above the foot, the tooltip); it fails without the fix (no `fuse_drawn`, and the old face joined the keeps with ", " into two lines). `s9_fuse.png` re-taken: the Prism card reads all four kept mechanics (knockback cone, centre beam burns, point blank x2, ricochet 1 wall; no losses on that seed). After the fix: gates `.check/m50_2c_review/gates.sh` 81 summaries all 0 failures, g0 exit 0, no ref changed; full GUT 61 scripts 1131/1131; `check.ps1 -BotRuns` CHECK PASSED with no expectation changed.
- **`BUILD = "M50.2c"`.**
- **Still open (defaults taken):** P8 the card faces before S9's `skin ui` are flat code-drawn cards and the Pixel frames come with `skin ui` (D16); the opening is the flip beat only (no drop / tear / slot-in, so the pick pause does not grow). Both in 9.

### 6.35 As built (M50.3a): the deep launcher and mines (Arcade through S10, design 3)
*Built in the `build/arcade` worktree on M50.2c, from `.check/arcade_s10/design.md` rev. 2 section 3 (3.1-3.4). The Stage 1 rework (M47.6a-d2) and Auto depth are **not** in this tree. No S7-S9 pool holds the launcher, so no session's play moves. Not committed.*
- **Data (3.1):** `weapons/launcher.tres` gains `mechanics = [sticky, implode, mine_layer]`, `mechanic_values = [{}, {}, {}]` and `icon = &"bomb"` (was `ball`); classic `ranks` and `card_text` unchanged (Survival / Auto still play it classic). Deep table: damage 2 / 2 / 3 / 3 / 4 / 4 / 6 / 6 / 8 / 8 / 12 / 12 at ranks 1-12; sticky at 2, implode at 4, mine layer at 6, then sticky 2 at 8, implode 2 at 10, mine layer 2 at 12 (rank 20: sticky 4, implode 3, mine layer 3).
- **Trait levels:** `sticky.gd` `steps()` `radius_mult` 1.2 / 1.3 / 1.4 / 1.5 (overflow `radius_mult`), `implode.gd` `pull` 455 / 606 / 758 / 910 (overflow `pull`), both with `describe()` ("sticky: shells stick, then blow bigger (x1.2)", "implode: the blast pulls enemies in", "(pull xN)" from level 2). `StickyMod.radius_mult` / `.pull` are instance fields defaulting to the old consts, set by the traits; level 1 (the classic curve) is the old consts exactly.
- **Mines (3.2):** new `weapons/traits/mine_layer.gd` (every 3 / 3 / 2 / 2 shots, max 4 / 5 / 5 / 6, overflow `damage_mult` +10 %; "mine layer: every 3rd shot drops a mine (4 max)") and `weapons/traits/mine.gd` (the caltrop pattern: a `Node2D` in group `mine`, not an entity or projectile; dropped at the shell's muzzle `origin`; arms after 0.5 s; the first enemy body within 0.5 T sets off `Explosion.blast(pos, 1.6 T, damage, 0.0, &"blast", source, false)`, credited to the shell's `source_patch` `weapon:launcher`; fizzles after 12 s; `make_room(max)` frees the oldest in tree order; no RNG). The count rides `Weapon.shots + 1` in the trait's `spawned()` hook, which runs only for a shell that spawned; Loadout counts a shot only when `fire()` spawned it, so a refused shell drops no mine and does not advance the count. One mine per shot whatever the pellets (`_laid_at`). Damage = the launcher's `value(&"damage")` at its rank x `damage_mult` (no quick-draw factor).
- **The blast hook (3.3):** `ProjectileMod.blasted(p, pos)` (no-op); `Projectile.explode(pos, radius := -1.0, target := null, normal, wall := false)` replaces the two inline blasts: `Explosion.blast(pos, radius or blast_radius, damage, blast_knockback, ...)`, then `projectile_hit` (entity) / `projectile_hit_wall` (wall) / no event (sticky's timed blast), then each mod's `blasted()`, then the despawn. New `Projectile.blast_knockback := 0.0` (spawn param). `StickyMod` blows through `p.explode(pos, p.blast_radius * radius_mult)` (no event, as before).
- **Pixel art and `card_rare` (3.4):** new grids `shot_bomb_player` 9x7, `shot_cluster_player` 9x9 (Cluster's shell, for M50.3b), `mine` / `mine_armed` 7x7 (drawn at 3x); `Grids.ROLES["card_rare"] = "#ffd84a"`. `Skins.GROUPS[&"shots"]` gains the four ids; `WeaponGlyph.KEYS` gains `bomb`, `cluster` (with tier-0 shapes: a squat bomb with a fuse; a shell ringed by five bomblets). The atlas digest moves once: `d07379b4...` -> `5ccf2497...` (pinned in `test_pixel_skin.gd` and `test_card_art.gd`); the `card_art` digest is unchanged (it reads `card_rare` at draw time). g0 silhouette sheet exit 0.
- **Telemetry (additive):** campaign record `mines {laid, blown}` (pack paths; from the run log's new `mine_laid` / `mine_blown` events, which no hash reads). Bot run line: `mines=<laid>/<blown>` after `give=`.
- **Deviations:** (1) the bomb sprite is the player shots' cyan with a warm gold fuse spark, flat-sided, not "a squat warm-orange bomb": the grids' faction rule (only the player and its shots are cool; enemy shots are warm and round) wins over the design's colour word; the mines are cyan-rimmed for the same reason; (2) the mine is drawn at 3x (21 px), not the bodies' 2x: at 2x it vanished on the dark floor in the first capture; tier 0 is a dark disc with a player_shot ring and light; (3) the armed light blinks every 0.4 s of game time (steady under reduce flashing), drawn only, never in play; (4) the design's "player-only id list" in `test_pixel_skin.gd` does not exist in this tree; a new test checks the four ids are player-side (no `_enemy` twin; an enemy launcher shot falls back to `shot_dot_enemy`) instead; (5) "mines <= cap at rank 20" is a GUT test (`test_rank_20_keeps_mines_under_the_cap`: 40 shells, mines full at 5 and never over), not a bench row; (6) no PLAYTEST.md entry (no session reaches the launcher until S10, M50.3c; P4 is asked there).
- **Touch points with the Stage 1 rework (for the merge):** none in session data, runners or gates; `campaign_db.gd` (the debrief record) gains the `mines` field next to M50.2a's pack fields; `tools/bot_runs.gd` gains `mines=` after `give=`. Touch (Auto depth): `launcher.tres` (merge by field: this build adds `mechanics`, `mechanic_values`, `icon`); on Auto's deep curve (cap 6) the launcher's rank 6 is `mine_layer` once both builds are in.
- **Tests:** new `tests/gut/test_launcher_deep.gd` (13): the deep table 1-12 and rank 20; classic = today (no mines); sticky / implode / mine_layer steps, level 1 = the old consts, overflow, card lines; sticky 2 blows at 1.3x and implode 2 pulls 606 in play, classic shells keep the consts; a mine every 3rd spawned shell, none from a refused shell (not counted); none on classic or below rank 6; arming 0.5 s, trigger, damage, credit `weapon:launcher`, one blast then gone, the player not pushed; out of reach, fizzle at 12 s; caps 4 / 6 and the oldest goes; rank 20 never over its cap; `explode` order (blast, hit / wall event, `blasted`, despawn), its radius argument, default knockback 0 and `blast_knockback` pushing. They fail on M50.2c (no `mine_layer`, no `explode`, no steps). Edited: `test_pixel_skin` (launcher -> `shot_bomb_player`, cluster -> `shot_cluster_player`, the digest, new `test_the_launcher_sprites_are_player_shots`), `test_card_art` (`ATLAS_DIGEST`), `test_arsenal_depth.test_card_rare_is_a_role_in_every_look` (Pixel names it, `#ffd84a`). GUT (one file at a time): test_launcher_deep 13/13, test_pixel_skin 31/31, test_arsenal_depth 34/34, test_card_art 7/7, test_weapons 36/36, test_fusion 22/22, test_arsenal 36/36, test_paths 41/41, test_give_step 12/12, test_auto_scale 21/21, test_hud_layout 10/10, test_playtest_build 8/8, test_mutations 19/19, test_big_slot 20/20, test_readability 19/19, test_s9_paint 23/23 (full suite not run).
- **Hash gates** (`.check/m50_3a/gates.sh`, the M50.2c matrix): 81 summaries, all 0 failures, g0 sheet exit 0. Equal: v6 4/4, gauntlet 4/4, campaign (ref_m43.3, also `--look=pixel` and hosted at both view sizes), S4 (ref_m42.2), S7 and S8 Arsenal (ref_m50_2a), S9 Arsenal (ref_m50_2b) replayed twice plus `--skins=off`, `--look=pixel` and hosted, S8 / S9 Auto and Survival (ref_m47, ref_m47_2, ref_m48b), pause determinism S4 and S8 x 3 paths, handoff 120. No new ref.
- **Bots (bot numbers, seeds 1-8; logs `.check/m50_3a/bot_*.log`), identical to M50.2c:** S9 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won, fused 8/8 median 99.1 s, give replace:pistol @86.0, packs 22-23, rares per pack 0.45, max rank 11-22 (median 17), `mines=0/0`; mortal 6/8 deaths (projectile 30, contact 3), fused 6/8. S8 Arsenal invincible 8/8 won, packs 18-20, max rank 19-29; mortal 2/8 deaths (projectile 23, contact 1). The launcher's own numbers wait for S10 (M50.3c).
- **Captures** (`.check/m50_3a/capture.gd`, windowed 1600x900, inert save, seed 1, invincible bot; the hand is set to a deep launcher at 20 s, capture only): `launcher_mines_s8.png` (tier 0, S8: Launcher 10 card with the bomb glyph, two armed mines as ringed discs, shells in flight), `launcher_mines_pixel.png` / `launcher_mines_b_pixel.png` (S9 `--look=pixel`, Launcher 11: cyan-rimmed mine plates, lit when armed; a bomb shell in flight with its gold spark; the HAND card's art is the bomb sprite), `launcher_mines_pixel_reduce.png` (the same with reduce flashing: armed lights steady), `launcher_mines.png` (S9 story look at 32 s, taken before the mine sprite was enlarged).
- **Review (1 findings, 1 upheld, all fixed):** (1) the icon rename `ball` -> `bomb` left `CardArt.BAND` without a `bomb` key, so every launcher card face (pack, hand, toast, the Cluster / Satellite recipe bands) fell back to the misfuse lilac: `BAND` gains `bomb` (the launcher's old `ball` pair `#ff9a6b` / `#e0664f`) and `cluster` (`#ff8a4a` / `#c95a2e`, a default: no mockup shows Cluster's card, for M50.3b). ### 6.36 As built (M50.3b): Cluster (Arcade through S10, design 4.1)
*Built in the `build/arcade` worktree on M50.3a, from `.check/arcade_s10/design.md` rev. 2 section 4.1. The Stage 1 rework (M47.6a-d2) and Auto depth are **not** in this tree. No S7-S9 pool holds the launcher, so no session can make Cluster yet (S10, M50.3c): no session's play moves. Not committed.*
- **Data:** `weapons/fusions/cluster.tres` `built_in = ""`, `burst = 5` (new `WeaponDef.burst := 0`), mechanics `knockback_cone_cluster`, `sticky`, `point_blank_cluster`, `implode` (the `*_cluster` names pass `WeaponDef.covers()`); inherits unchanged (shotgun A/B, launcher A/B: `fusion_problems()` has no Cluster line, 4 unbuilt rows left). Shell: aimed, 600 px/s, cd 0.9, blast 1.2 T, **damage 3.9** (design 2; see the bench). Fused levels from the rank (5 = [1,1,1,1], 7 = [2,1,1,1]).
- **The burst** (`weapons/cluster_mod.gd`): on each blast of the shell (`ProjectileMod.blasted`, after the blast and its contact event, before the despawn) 5 bomblets leave the blast point at the shell's flight angle (kept while stuck) + k x 72 deg, no RNG: 1.5 T in 0.2 s, `pop_at_end` (new `Projectile` field / spawn param, default false: a shot whose life ends blows instead of vanishing), blast 0.6 T, **damage 0.2 x the shell's** (`BOMBLET_MULT`; design 0.5), drawn as `shot_ball_player` at 0.5 size, `weapon:cluster`, no mods. Spawned through `RunState.spawn` as player shots, so the player budget (140) refuses them, never the enemies' 60. Attached by **`Weapon.attach(shots, faction)`**, called by the Loadout after `Shooter.fire` (try_fire, not on the misfuse; auto_tick), only for a player weapon with `burst > 0`; `Shooter.fire` (shared with enemies) never attaches it. A bomblet that starts inside the body the shell stuck to blows on it at once (the ray's hit-from-inside), so on a lone target the burst lands there and in a pack it spreads.
- **Mechanics:** `knockback_cone_cluster` puts the shotgun's push (420 x 1 / 1.3 / 1.6 / 1.8) on the shell's blast (`blast_knockback`); `sticky` (the burst comes from the stuck point); `point_blank_cluster` (D10 "a burst near you": a blast within 2-2.5 T of the player x2-x3 on the shell and, through its damage, the bomblets; applied in the new `ProjectileMod.blasting(p, pos)` hook, which `Projectile.explode` calls first: no-op by default); `implode` (pull before the blast). FUSE: "FUSE: Shotgun 4 + Launcher 5 -> Cluster 5", loses shrapnel / mine layer when held; a parent's card feeds it; the recipe is learned (generic M50.1b code).
- **Bot:** `BotInput._choose_card`: Cluster answers for the shotgun and the launcher; when it is held and a group of >= 3 sits in its blast, the launcher's group rule wins (aimed at the group), then the shotgun / laser rules as before (`_group_for`). A plain launcher behaves exactly as before.
- **Telemetry (additive):** run-log event `cluster_burst {bomblets, refused}`; campaign record `bomblets`, `bomblets_refused` (pack paths); bot line `bomblets=<thrown>/<refused>` after `mines=`.
- **Bench (gated in GUT, `test_cluster.test_cluster_vs_prism_power_bench`):** one tank, or a pack of 8 chasers 0.75 T apart, near face 3.3 T from the player (all 5 Prism beams land on the tank; neither point blank reaches), AI off, bodies movable (knockback and implode act), damage of one shot over its cooldown. With the design's values (bomblet 0.5, shell 2) Cluster read 0.67-0.73 x Prism on the tank and 0.85-0.87 x on the pack at a 3 T face (Prism's pierce 2 + burn on a tight pack is strong). The design's levers in order: `bomblet_mult` alone cannot lift the pack ratio without the tank ratio (both scale), so 0.5 -> 0.2, then the shell's base damage 2 -> 3.9. **After: rank 5 tank 17.3/23.3 = 0.74, pack 69.3/51.3 = 1.35; rank 9 0.77 / 1.44; rank 13 0.81 / 1.26** (bands 0.7-0.9 and 1.2-1.5).
- **Deviations:** (1) shell damage 3.9 and bomblet 0.2 x instead of 2 and 0.5 (the bench above; the design names both as levers); (2) the bench is a GUT test (deterministic one-shot trials), not a `tools/bench.gd` row (bench.gd is a windowed perf tool); (3) the cap check is a GUT test (`test_cluster_20_in_a_crowd_stays_under_the_budgets`: Cluster 20 vs 60 enemies, half shooters, 30 s: max 5 player shots, 330 bomblets, enemy shots keep firing, `enemy_shots_refused` unchanged, no mines) plus a budget test with the player budget pre-filled to 138 (2 bomblets spawn, 3 refused, the enemy budget untouched); (4) a second ProjectileMod hook `blasting()` (before the blast) is added next to `blasted()`, because point blank must change the damage before the blast lands; (5) the bomblets do not push (only the shell's blast); (6) no PLAYTEST.md entry (no session reaches Cluster until S10; P5 is asked there).
- **Noticed, not changed:** implode pulls the shell's own stuck target too (the pull points at the shell on its surface), so a stuck enemy moves ~1 T toward the player before the blast (launcher and Cluster alike; classic launcher unchanged on purpose).
- **Touch points with the Stage 1 rework (for the merge):** none in session data, runners or gates; `campaign_db.gd` (the debrief record) gains `bomblets` / `bomblets_refused` next to `mines`; `tools/bot_runs.gd` gains `bomblets=` after `mines=`; `core/bot_input.gd` `_choose_card` (merge by function). Touch (Auto depth): `Loadout.auto_tick` calls `Weapon.attach` (no Auto weapon has a burst).

### 6.38 As built (M50.5a): card art, small-font letters

Built on `build/integration` after M50.5 (50de90c). `BUILD = "M50.5a"`. Presentation only: no ref moved.

- **Card art (the `Arcade_Cards` / `Arcade_Pack` sheets).** Arcade's card faces drew the weapon's shot glyph (a pellet: the Pistol and the Shotgun looked the same). `CardFace._gun()` now draws Auto's M50.4f art: on Pixel `CardArt.draw_card_art` (its `GUNS` grids, filled to the art box), on tier 0 `WeaponGlyph.draw_card_flat` (the flat guns); a weapon without a gun keeps its glyph. Every kind: common, RARE, NEW, the give card, FUSE (the fused gun in the middle, both parents' small guns below it), the hand under a pack, the 1-card toast. `CardArt` gains `GUN_PRISM` (converted from the sheet's `ac-px-prism`, its plum outline dropped since CardArt draws its own) and `GUN_CLUSTER` (no sheet picture: the launcher's tube, the shell ahead, five bomblets round it); `draw_card_art` takes a `least` cell size (1 px for the minis). `WeaponGlyph.FLAT_GUNS` gains `prism` (after the sheet's `ac-fl-prism`) and `cluster`, in Palette roles only. The rail's Arsenal HAND card draws `WeaponGlyph.draw_card` (the gun, as Auto's rail slots do). `CardFace.art_drawn` records what each draw used (tests).
- **Small-font letters.** The owner's misreads ("Fifty" -> "Aifty", "Fire rate" -> "Are rate", "Five pellets" -> "Awe pellets") are Pixelify's `liga` ligature: "fi" (also fl, ff, ffi, ffl; `dlig` st) becomes one joined glyph that reads as an A at 10-16 px. The M50.5 S8 capture shows it in the chat ("Secret: the Arst shot"). `UiTheme.pixel_font` turns `liga`, `clig`, `dlig` and `hlig` off on both its variations (`UiTheme.no_ligatures()`), so every letter is its own Pixelify glyph. Close-ups of A-Z, a-z, 0-9 and the owner's words at 9-16 px, regular and bold: `.check/m50_5a/cap/font_before.png` / `font_after.png`.
- **Deviations:** the task also asked to redraw the confusable capitals the way M48.5b redrew the digits. Tried and reverted: B, F and Z as code-drawn glyphs in the digit font (F read as P, B as G or 8, Z as 2 at 10-12 px) read clearly at 1:1, but the game's window is scaled (1600x900 on a 1280x720 base: 1.25), Pixelify oversamples and the bitmap font cannot (M50.5's whole-size caches), so a drawn F stood 2 px taller than its word ("FUSE" read "Fuse"; measured 12 vs 10 px at 1.25, 13 vs 11 at 1.33). The ligature fix alone covers the three reported words. Still open.
- **Part C, S9 Arsenal max rank (bot numbers, 8 seeds, `.check/m50_5a/bots/`):** invincible 14, 15, 16, 17, 14, 21, 11, 10 (median 14.5, won 8/8); mortal 5, 15, 4, 15, 14, 11, 11, 5 (median 11, won 2/8). Inside the owner's ~12-16, so no lever was applied (the next would be "no rare duplicate in every pack"). Both logs are byte-equal in these numbers to M50.5's.
- **Numbers.** Tests (each fails without its fix): `test_card_art.gd` `test_every_arsenal_card_draws_its_gun_on_both_looks` (new) and `test_every_card_kind_has_a_face_on_tier_0_and_pixel` (now asserts guns, not the look's shot sprites); `test_rail_ui.gd` `test_the_pixel_font_never_joins_letters` ("fi", "first", "fire rate", ... shape one glyph per letter, both weights, 10-18 px); `test_auto_items.gd` lets a fusion's icon have a flat gun. GUT 1336/1336 inside `check.ps1 -BotRuns` (71 scripts, no script errors). Gates `.check/m50_5a/gates.sh` (M50.5's without its phase 0): 97 runhash summaries 0 failures (v6, gauntlet, `ref_m47_6c1` campaign and S4 replayed twice, the chain vs `ref_m47_6c2`, pixel, `--shell` at both views, `--handoff=120`, S7 / S8 x 3 / S9 x 3 vs `ref_m50_5` with their skins-off / pixel / 1560 rows, the S4 / S8 pause bases), render sheet exit 0 (ERRORS: 0), GATES_DONE; `main.gd`'s BUILD string and the docs changed after the gates started. `check.ps1 -BotRuns`: CHECK PASSED, exit 0.
- **Captures** (windowed, inert save; all opened): `.check/m50_5a/cap/cards_after_flat.png` / `cards_after_pixel.png` (every card kind, the hand minis and two toasts: guns on both looks, Prism and Cluster on FUSE), `s8_pack.png` (tier 0: Pistol 6 > 7 and Shotgun 1 > 2 with flat guns, the hand's guns), `s8_pack_pixel.png` (the same pack in Pixel; the chat now reads "the first shot"), `s9_fuse.png` (Shotgun / RARE Pistol / Laser guns, the FUSE card's Prism with Laser and Shotgun below), `s9_give.png` (the gold card's Laser gun), `s9_fuse_rail.png` (the rail's HAND cards with guns); before: `.check/m50_5/cap/` (same names).

### 6.39 As built (M50.6): stacked packs and weapon rarity

Built in the `build/packs` worktree on `build/integration` after M50.5a (3e2b0d5). `BUILD = "M50.6"`. Owner asks of 2026-11-26. Arsenal play moves on purpose; nothing else does.

- **The stack** (`paths/path_runner.gd`): on a pack path `want_pick()` adds to the stack (`pack_stack()`, cap `Tuning.pack_stack_max` 99) instead of asking for a panel. A pack opens only on a tick where it is asked: the player's `open_pack_pressed` (new field on `PlayerInput`; `HumanInput` reads the new `open_pack` action, F / pad X, never while anything pauses), `PathRunner.open_pack()`, or the bot. One press, one pack: the panel, the pause, the deal, the replace step, Back and the 1-card toast are M50.1a-M50.2c's. The give step still opens by itself, before any pack. Level-up paths are untouched (they open by themselves). `PACK_GAP` no longer delays anything (the press is the gap); the constant stays for the tests that wait that long.
- **HUD:** the rail's PACK module (`ui/rail/rail_state.gd`, `rail_modules.gd`) shows "x3" in its tab and, with packs stacked, a key chip and "open - 3 stacked" in place of "pick 1 of 3". The legacy hand (`paths/arsenal/hand_widget.gd`) shows "3 packs" and "[F] open" over its meter. The options screen's Controls tab lists "Open a pack".
- **The AI:** `PathDef.pack_first_line` ("A pack! F opens it. No rush.", at the session's first pack) and `pack_stack_line` ("Three packs waiting. F opens one.", once a session at `Tuning.pack_nudge_at`); both go through the session's say queue with the key named for the keyboard. S7's and S8's pack cue lines name the key; S8's "Gold ones count twice" became "Gold cards are rarer"; S9's shotgun line dropped "Gold card" (the shotgun is common).
- **Carry:** `SaveGame.packs_banked()` / `bank_packs(n)` on a sparse campaign key `packs` (0-99; a save without it has none, as `recipes`). `Main._end_session` banks the stack when the player moves on (not on Retry, not in a replay, never from the tryout); `_build_cards` starts the path's next session with them. `stop()` keeps the stack so the end of a session cannot lose it.
- **Rarity** (`weapons/weapon_def.gd`): `rarity` in `RARITIES` (common / rare / epic); `problems()` refuses an unknown tier and any mismatch between "epic" and "is a fusion". Set on laser, nova, well, orbit (rare) and the six fusion rows (epic). `BoosterPack.card()` is always +1 and carries the weapon's tier; `weighted()` multiplies a held weapon by `Tuning.pack_held_weight` (2.0) and a rare one by `Tuning.pack_rare_weight` (0.5). `PathDef.rare_chance` and the in-place rare fallback of M50.2a are gone. A pack still draws exactly 6 values from `pack_rng` (two of them now decide the epic slots), so the stream's length per pack is unchanged.
- **Epic cards** (`BoosterPack.epic_ids`, `epic_card`, `is_epic`; `Loadout.absorb`): as 1.17. The card's kind is `absorb` (with `absorbs`: the 1 or 2 held parents) or `new`. Taking one counts as a fusion made: `fusions` gets a row with `epic: true`, the `fused` event is logged, the recipe is learned. `Loadout.absorb(parent, fused_id, rank)` swaps the parent for the fusion in its slot and keeps its shots and the active card.
- **Faces** (`ui/card_face.gd`, `systems/skins/art/card_face_art.gd`, `systems/palette.gd`): the frame follows the tier: `card_common`, `card_rare` (gold) and the new `card_epic` (a lilac face with a mint ring on Pixel; a double violet border on tier 0; `Palette.CARD_EPIC`). The ribbon is the tier (COMMON / RARE / EPIC); NEW stays the tag in the art box. The toast reads "Pack: Laser +1  RARE".
- **Bot** (`core/bot_input.gd`, `tools/bot_runs.gd`): the bot asks every tick, so each pack opens on its first free tick (a seed replays); `--probe=hoard` (and `afk`) never open one. `pack_choice` rule 2 is now "an EPIC card that melts a held parent, or finds room" (never with `no_fuse` / `skip_new`); the old "rare card for a held weapon" rule is gone (rarity gives no bonus). The `--only` line adds `earned= opened= left= stack_peak= epics=`; a `[botrun] stack:` summary follows the `fusion:` line.
- **Telemetry (additive):** the path record gains `packs_opened`, `packs_left`, `packs_carried`, `stack_peak`, `epics_shown`; `pack_picks` gains `epic`. `pack_picks.rare` now counts rare-tier cards taken.
- **Deviations:** (1) epic cards wait for the `fusion` basic (S9 at 94 s), so the AI still introduces fusion before a fused gun can show up. (2) A fusion is not offered as an epic while one of its parents is inside another held fusion. (3) The bot never takes an epic NEW card on a full hand (it would have to let a card go). (4) No first-pack auto-open: the key is taught by the HUD, the cue line and the first-pack line. (5) The HUD's count includes a pack that is open (it leaves the stack when a card is taken). (6) Rarity on Auto's passives-only weapons (nova, well, orbit) is data only: they never show in a pack. (7) The give card's frame follows the weapon's tier, so S9's rank-3 laser is still gold and the F2 shotgun gift is not.
- **Numbers.** Tests (each fails without the change: they call the new API): new `tests/gut/test_pack_stack.gd` (22 tests): the stack never opens by itself and draws nothing; the open action opens one pack with the pause and no other follows; the `open_pack` action through `HumanInput`; a closed gate and an open panel refuse; the cap; the HUD's count and key (hand and rail); the first-pack line and the one nudge at 3; packs carried S8 -> S9 through the save, the tryout and the arena bank nothing, an old save without `packs` loads, a wrong type or a negative count is refused; rarity per weapon and `problems()`; every card +1 over 300 packs; the ribbons and frames; the 2 : 1 : 0.5 weights and card 3 favouring the held weapon over 2000 packs; `epic_ids` (parents held or in the pool, no held fusion, built only); one epic a pack, never card 1, 300-480 packs of 4000 with one, exactly 6 draws; rank 4 or the parent's; the absorb of one parent in place, of both, the replace step on a full hand; `Loadout.absorb`; the bot's epic rule; the bot opens 5 of 5 packs and the same seed twice gives the same packs, picks, hand and `pack_rng` state (`hoard` opens 0). ### 6.40 As built (M50.6a): readable ribbons, the tryout's pack

Built in the `build/packs` worktree on M50.6 (ab26e9e). `BUILD = "M50.6a"`. Three small Arcade fixes; no play moved outside S7's taste, and S7's replay is byte-equal (the bot already opened each pack on its first free tick).

- **The ribbon** (`ui/card_face.gd`, `systems/skins/art/rail_art.gd`): the fault was Pixelify Sans itself. Its design pixel is 1/11 em, so at 9, 10 and 13 px a capital C closes on the right and reads as an O or a D (measured on a windowed sheet of both weights, 8-20 px: clean at 11, 12 and 14-20). The card is drawn inside the game's scaled view, so no font size is safe there. On Pixel the ribbon is now drawn from `RailArt.DIGITS`, which gains block capitals A-Z in the 5x7 grid its digits use (C is open on the right; O, D, G and Q differ by at least 3 cells), at `CardFace.RIBBON_CELL` (2 px a cell at card scale 1). `CardFace.ribbon_drawn` records the text, the box and the cell. Tier 0's pill is unchanged (the sans font).
- **The rail's small text** (`ui/theme/ui_theme.gd`, `ui/rail/rail_layout.gd`): `UiTheme.pixel_size(px)` steps 9 and 10 to 11 and 13 to 12 (`SMEAR_SIZES`); `RailLayout.text_px` returns through it. On a 1280 px window the rail's 16 px labels landed on 13: "PACK" read "PAOK", "CURSOR" "OURSOR".
- **The tryout's pack** (`paths/path_def.gd`, `paths/arsenal.tres`, `paths/path_runner.gd`): `PathDef.taste_auto_open` (Arcade: true). `PathRunner.auto_opens()` is true only on a pack path in a tryout session with that flag: a pack then opens on its first free tick, with no first-pack line and no nudge. Data on the path, read through `_tryout()`; nothing branches on a mode. S8 and later stack as M50.6. S7's `at 8` line dropped "{key:open_pack} opens it." (it now ends "Six kills, I'll deal you a pack."); F is taught by S8's `at 10` line and the path's first-pack line. `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated (one line).
- **Small things:** three comments in `path_runner.gd` still described `PACK_GAP` as a delay between packs; `pick_panel.gd` still said "RARE +2".
- **Deviations:** (1) no gap between two auto-opened taste packs (the constant delays nothing since M50.6; the taste earns 2-3 packs). (2) Text drawn in Pixelify inside the scaled game view (card names, rule lines) keeps the font: only the ribbon was misread; the same closing C can show there at some window sizes. (3) The block capitals are the rail's digit font, not a redraw of Pixelify.
- **Numbers.** Tests (each fails without its change): `test_pack_stack.gd` `test_the_tryout_taste_opens_a_pack_by_itself_and_s8_still_stacks` (new, 23 in the file); `test_card_art.gd` `test_the_pixel_ribbon_is_block_capitals_that_tell_c_from_d` (new: every ribbon letter has a glyph, C differs from D, O, G and Q, the ribbon fits its box at 3 scales); `test_rail_ui.gd` `test_the_rail_never_draws_text_at_a_size_that_closes_the_c` (new). GUT 1361/1361 inside `check.ps1 -BotRuns` (72 scripts, no script errors, `CHECK PASSED`; bot chain 10 sessions, play 559.0 s: a bot number). Gates `.check/m50_6a/gates.sh` (M50.6's matrix without its recording phases; every row against an existing ref): 97 runhash summaries, 0 failures; render sheet exit 0 (ERRORS: 0); GATES_DONE. No ref was recorded: S7, S8 and S9 on Arcade equal `ref_m50_6`, Auto and Survival `ref_m50_5`, Stage 1 `ref_m47_6c1` / `ref_m47_6c2`, the v6 loop `runhash_m41*`.
- **Captures** (windowed 1280x720, inert save, all opened): `.check/m50_6a/cap/font_before.png` and `font_sizes.png` (the sheet: C closes at 9, 10 and 13 px in both weights), `rail_pack.png` (the pack on the rail: ribbons read COMMON / RARE / EPIC; the rail's tabs read CURSOR, KILLS, HP, DASH, HAND, PACK x3), `rail_stack.png` (PACK x3 with the F chip and "open - 3 stacked"), `rail_replace.png` (the EPIC ribbon over Prism, "Your hand is full. Prism 4 replaces:"), `rail_absorbed.png` (the hand reads Pistol / Prism), `flat_pack.png` and the other three tier 0 shots (unchanged: pills in the sans font).
