### 6.41 As built (M50.6b): pixel projectiles

Built in the `build/packs` worktree on M50.6a. `BUILD = "M50.6b"`. The owner approved the projectile sheet (the mockup's Shots sheet and its in-play page); the Pixel look's shots are redrawn from it. Presentation only: no ref moved.

- **The shots** (`systems/skins/art/grids.gd`): the pistol's bolt (pointed, white-hot tip, three exhaust pixels), the shotgun's shard, the laser's lance (a comb of exhaust), the launcher's finned shell with a gold and orange flame, Prism in three bands, the thin Minigun tracer, Cluster with gold bomblets; the enemy orb gains a second, bigger pulse frame, the enemy spread pellet is a teardrop, the enemy chain is three orbs of falling size. The file's header states the rules again: player shots angular and cool, enemy shots round and warm, a 1 px outline on the body; the shape rule is the one that never bends (the tracer and the revolver's slug are warm bars).
- **Tails** (`systems/skins/art_skin.gd`, `entities/rect_visual.gd`, `ui/weapon_glyph.gd`): a grid points along +x and turns with the shot's heading. Loose pixels behind the body carry no outline, and the grid's `"tail"` key counts their columns: `ArtSkin.tail_of(id)`. `RectVisual` draws a shot shifted back by half its tail, so the outlined body is centred on the shot and the tail trails on every heading; `WeaponGlyph` sizes a picture by the body and lets the tail hang out.
- **Sizes**: every sheet shot is drawn at 2 px a cell. The launcher's shell (shot size 1.6) was at 3x; `ArtSkin.NATIVE` now names it, so its body is 24x14 px over a 22x10 flat rect. The other bodies: bolt 16x10 (rect 14x6), shard 12x10, lance 26x10 (rect 21x9), Prism 20x6, tracer 14x6, Cluster 18x18.
- **The muzzle flash and the hit spark** (`systems/shot_fx.gd`, new; `systems/feedback.gd`): two new sprites, `muzzle_flash` (a warm wedge, its flat side on the muzzle) and `hit_spark` (a cyan eight-point star, no outline), revealed with the `shots` group. `ShotFx` holds one for a few frames (0.08 s and 0.1 s). Feedback makes the flash in `_on_projectile_fired` (one per physics frame, turned to the pellets' mean heading; in place of the look preset's particles) and the spark from `EventBus.projectile_hit`, a hook Feedback did not listen to before (player shots only). Under reduce flashing there is no drawn flash and the spark is at half alpha. At most 48 live.
- **Deviations from the sheet:** (1) the bolt's body is 8 cells long, not 9, so the HUD picture keeps its 2x scale. (2) Prism is one cell thin (the sheet: three), because the weapon fires five beams 4.5 degrees apart and the thick ones merged into a block for the first frames. (3) The sheet's letters T, F, C and V are mapped onto the look's four ramp letters; its pure white is the look's warm white. (4) The enemy teardrop and chain are drawn, but no enemy fires a weapon with those icons today: every enemy shot is the orb. (5) The orb's first frame is padded to 10x10 so both frames match.
- **Numbers.** Tests in `test_pixel_skin.gd` (each fails without the change): `test_m50_6b_shot_sprites_and_their_tails` (sizes, tails, no outline in a tail, the orb's two frames, the shell at 2x, gold bomblets, the two effects in the `shots` group) and `test_m50_6b_muzzle_flash_and_hit_spark` (none without the look, one flash per frame on the mean heading, a spark at the hit, none for enemy shots, reduce flashing). Re-pinned on purpose: the atlas digest (`db2c8451...`, was `30a94910...`) and the launcher shell's size (13x7). GUT 1363/1363 inside `check.ps1 -BotRuns` (72 scripts, no script errors, `CHECK PASSED`; bot chain 10 sessions, play 559.0 s: a bot number). Gates `.check/m50_6b/gates.sh` (M50.6a's matrix, unchanged): 97 runhash summaries, 0 failures; render sheet exit 0 (ERRORS: 0); GATES_DONE. No ref was recorded.
- **Captures** (windowed 1280x720, inert save, Pixel look, all opened; `.check/m50_6b/cap/`): `lineup.png` (every shot on eight headings at 3x with the flat rect's frame over it: tails trail on every heading, bodies sit on the rect; the flash, the spark, both orb frames), `<weapon>_fire_zoom.png` for pistol, shotgun, laser, launcher, prism, minigun and cluster (S9 on Arcade, the frame after a shot: the flash at the muzzle, the shot leaving it; the shotgun's five shards and Prism's five beams still overlap into a dark knot on those first frames, as the old bars did), `shotgun_hit_zoom.png` and `pistol_hit_zoom.png` (cyan sparks on the hit, beside the kill pop), `enemy_orbs_zoom.png` and `enemy_orbs_b_zoom.png` (orbs in both pulse frames), `minigun_fire.png` (the full hosted window with the rail). M50.6's captures show packs and cards, no shot in flight, so there is no like-for-like picture; the comparison is the lineup's flat frames.
- **Review (self-review, one build agent):** (1) the flash was drawn under the shot that made it and could not be seen on its first frame: it is on z 5 now. (2) 0.06 s was shorter than two frames on a slow machine: 0.08 s. (3) The teardrop's point is outlined, so it is body, not tail. (4) The revolver's slug (shot size 1.3) still rounds to 3x and reads bigger than its rect; it is not on the sheet and was left alone.
- **Still open (defaults taken):** see section 9.

### 6.42 As built (M51a): creature mascots

Built in the `build/mascots` worktree on M50.6b (`build/packs` ea37666). `BUILD = "M51a"`. The owner approved the "Mascot creatures" mockup (`.check/mockups/mascot_creatures.html` in the main checkout): "they should look like a little creature so it has more life. I truly want them to feel cute and appreciable." Presentation only: no ref moved.

- **`CreatureBody`** (`ui/mascot/creature_body.gd`, new, extends `MascotBody`): the three pickable bodies (`cursor`, `window`, `blob`) now extend it; the caret is unchanged. Everything sits on the mockup's 26 x 26 cell canvas centred on the body's origin, feet on row 25. A body gives only its grid, its feet's rows, its ramp, where its face sits and its smooth shapes (`bodies/*.gd`). The mockup's grids and ramps are copied cell for cell.
- **Two looks.** Flat (the look off): smooth shapes over the same cells (soft polygons, Chaikin corners; the shade is the shape minus itself moved toward the light, the blob's rim the lit part minus itself moved away), a dark outline, outlined feet and a soft floor shadow; faces drawn from primitives (`flat_face()`). Pixel (`PixelBodyView` sets `body.pixel`): the grids drawn in runs of whole viewport pixels, a cell the largest whole number of pixels not above the flat cell, so the corner creature is 3 screen px a cell (the mockup's "at game size"); offsets snap to whole pixels and nothing rotates. `PixelBodyView` gets a finer `pixel` when a cell would be under one viewport pixel (the chat avatar: 2x, the debrief's small avatar: 1x; the corner, the debrief and the cards stay 3x).
- **Faces.** `CreatureBody.FACES`: a 10 x 6 grid per mood the game uses (`Line.EXPRESSIONS`). The mockup's five as drawn: happy, proud (shut eyes, open grin), thinking (eyes up-left, a side mouth), worried (brows, sweat, frown), surprised (big eyes, O mouth). Added in its style: idle (happy's eyes and cheeks, a small mouth), guilty (lidded eyes looking down, sweat on the brow, blush, a flat mouth), panic (white eyes with small pupils, brows, sweat, a wide open mouth). A blink on idle, happy and thinking; talking opens an O mouth (pixel: every other 1/7 s; flat: as before). The window's cap turns red in panic and grey in guilt, as its title bar did.
- **Motion.** The feet never move with a mood; the body and face move over them in the mockup's 0.42 s frames (pixel: whole cells; flat: smooth): happy hops, proud puffs, idle, thinking and guilty breathe, worried shivers, surprised jumps, panic shakes and hops. Reduce flashing drops the shivers and shakes. `point_at` leans one cell and grows an outlined arm toward the target; the glitch is unchanged. `outline()` reports both looks' motion in flat cells, so `keep_out` and `worst_bounds` are the same in both looks.
- **Palette**: 19 new tier-0 roles (`creature_*`, `cursor_hood*`, `cursor_feet`, `window_cap*`, `window_button`, `window_face*`, `window_feet`, `blob_body`, `blob_shade`, `blob_light`, `blob_shine`, `blob_leaf`, `blob_feet`), the mockup's hex values. The old body roles stay in the table (unused by the bodies now). `MascotBody` lost `draw_eye`/`draw_mouth`/`look`/`blinking` (no other caller) and gained `pixel`, `grid_unit()` and `_snap()`.
- **Deviations from the mockup:** (1) Names stay Cursor, Window and Blob (the mockup's Pip, Tab and Mochi were placeholders; asked in 9). (2) Idle, guilty and panic have no mockup face; they are drawn in its style. (3) The flat look is a smooth redraw of the grids, not the grids. (4) The window's cap buttons are three white squares, as the mockup (the old `_ □ ×` glyphs and the "ai.exe" / "(Not Responding)" text are gone: no room on a 3.5-cell cap). (5) The blob's "squashes a little on every step" is the shared bob only, no squash. (6) The mockup's blink is a whole 0.42 s frame every 7 frames; here it is 0.17 s every 2.9 s. (7) Guilty (no mockup move) breathes like idle, sagging a little lower in flat; a one-cell slump hid the cursor's feet in Pixel. (8) In Pixel the corner creature is smaller than the flat one (about 70 % of its size: one cell is one 3x pixel, as the mockup's game-size scene).
- **Numbers.** Tests in `test_mascot.gd` (each fails without the change): `test_each_creature_draws_every_mood_in_both_looks`, `test_the_faces_differ` (eight distinct grids and eight distinct flat faces; a blink and the talking mouth change the face), `test_the_feet_stay_planted_and_gentle_drops_the_shakes`, `test_the_pixel_look_draws_whole_cells`. GUT 1367/1367 inside `check.ps1 -BotRuns` (72 scripts, `CHECK PASSED`; the first run had one failure in `test_pack_stack.gd` (`an absorb epic is taken`, 2 vs 3), gameplay code this milestone does not touch; it passed alone twice and in the full re-run, so it is listed as a flake). Gates `.check/m51a/gates.sh` (M50.6b's matrix, unchanged): 98 runhash summaries, 0 failures; render sheet exit 0 (ERRORS: 0); GATES_DONE. No ref was recorded or moved.
- **Captures** (windowed 1280x720, inert save, all opened; `.check/`): `mascot_<body>.png` (flat: 8 moods, the same talking, pointing two ways, glitch), `mascot_<body>_pixel.png` (the same through `PixelBodyView`, plus a row at the corner's size), `mascot_chat_<body>[_pixel].png` (the dev-chat with the creature in its header and the corner creature beside it). The pixel grids match the mockup cell for cell (compared with a render of the mockup's own grids).
- **Review (self-review, one build agent):** (1) the first pixel `keep_out` moved (pixel motion is whole cells, flat motion smooth): `outline()` now spans both. (2) A guilty slump of one cell hid the cursor's feet in Pixel: guilty breathes instead. (3) `tools/mascot_capture.gd` named classes at parse time, which compiled the game's scripts before the autoloads and left `Skins.hub` null; it now loads them at run time, as `campaign_shots.gd` does.

### 6.43 As built (M50.3c): S10 "Level one", part one

Built in the `build/packs` worktree on M50.6b (ea37666), finishing a previous builder's uncommitted work. `BUILD = "M50.3c"`. Design `.check/arcade_s10/design.md` 4.2-4.4, adapted to M50.6's stacked packs and weapon rarity, with the session grown toward PLAN's S10 row (3.2, M50) where the earlier work had already built it.

- **The session** (`campaign/sessions/s2_04_levels.tres`, `campaign/campaign.tres`): index 10, "Level one", `bump patch` (v0.8.3), the last playable session (S9 now offers Next; S10 ends "to be continued"). Weapons pistol / shotgun / laser / launcher; enemies chaser, shooter and the Stage 1 `fork_enemy` answer at 1.3 / 1.3; `goal clear` with `enemy_total_by_path` Arcade 170, Auto 300, Survival 100 and the hidden `time_limit 300`; skin pixel; no fork. Each path's basics at 0 s (Arcade `cards` / `quick_draw` / `fusion`, Auto `levelups` / `crowd` / `evolve`, Survival `pickups` / `kill_heal`), `give launcher` at 4 s for all three (Arcade: a common card, the hand is the pistol alone; Auto: first in the next level-up; Survival: a crate), the recipe line at 20 s, `fuse_ready 1` and `fused 1` lines with `made:cluster` / `made:prism`, Auto's passives lines at 12 / 20 / 36 s. Debrief: won / died / the three enemies stay / the Arcade recipe lines; notes "Levels, in three parts", "The launcher", "Three new enemies (all of them stay)". `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **Levels in parts** (`campaign/session_def.gd`, `systems/run_loop.gd`, `genres/topdown/topdown_genre.gd`, `campaign/session_runner.gd`, `campaign/cue.gd`, `campaign/campaign_db.gd`): `SessionDef.stages = "fork:fork_layout"` plays the answer's StageSet (an unanswered fork: its first option). Under `goal clear` with a set of more than one stage, `RunLoop.in_parts()`: the total splits evenly (`part_budget`, cumulative); a part closes when its budget is spent and its own `room N` spawn cues have fired; a dead part moves on through the stage-clear beat, or opens the rooms layout's door and waits for the player; the beat is off the session clock; the last part ends as any clear session. The session's spawn multipliers ride on each stage's preset (`rate_mult`, `cap_mult`). `room N [at s]` cues are built (`SessionRunner.room()` / `room_clock()`). The HUD says "Part n/3"; the rail's caption "part n/3" and "door open".
- **`fork_layout`** (`devsession/forks/fork_layout.tres`, `devsession/changes/layout_*.tres`, kind `layout`): campaign data on the v6 sets, asked in S9's debrief (one new debrief line, "It's one room. Games have levels."). The v6 `stages` fork is untouched.
- **The three enemies** (`enemies/variants/{shielder,sniper,swarm}.tres`, `entities/enemy_walker.gd`, `entities/enemy_shooter.gd`, `entities/shield_arc.gd`, `entities/sniper_line.gd`, `components/health.gd`, `core/damage_info.gd`, `entities/projectile.gd`): the shielder (tank base, x0.75 HP, x0.8 speed) blocks player shots inside a 100 degree front arc that turns 90 degrees a second (`Health.guard`; blasts, beams, orbit and piercing shots pass; `RunState.shield_blocks` counts); the sniper (shooter base) keeps 9-12 T away, locks a red line for 1 s, stands, then fires one 1400 px/s shot along it; the swarm (chaser base, x0.6, x1.6 speed, 1 HP) comes in packs of 5 around one marker (the pack's offsets are `gameplay_rng` draws, S10 only). All three are drawn as their base under a look (`EnemyDB.PLAIN`, no sprites of their own yet) plus the drawn arc or line.
- **`edit_hot_edges`** (`rules/edit_hot_edges/`, `levels/hot_edges.gd`, `WorldRules.hot_edge_tiles`): the outer two tiles inside the border hurt 1 HP a second; never within 2 tiles of an exit gate; drawn in the `danger` role, pulsing unless reduce flashing. S10 adds it at part 3 + 20 s.
- **The known pool** (`SessionDef.known`, `PathRunner._apply`): shotgun, laser and nova (announced in S8-S9) are in the path's pool from S10's start without a new line.
- **Bots** (`core/bot_input.gd`): the path policies hunt the last of a part along the tile field only in a level in parts (`_level_hunting`), and inside `keep_distance` only once the survivors chase; S8 and S9 keep today's orbit (their refs). A live shielder now answers the M46 flank rule (`EnemyWalker.shield_arc_deg`), and the flank step only fires within `BotInput.FLANK_REACH` (5 T): farther out the shield turns faster than a step around it can travel, and Survival held its fire into the hidden cap on 6 of 8 seeds.
- **Deviations from the design:** (1) design 4.2's minimal default (one arena, no new enemies, no layout fork) was replaced by PLAN's S10 (3 parts in the player's layout, shielder / sniper / swarm, `edit_hot_edges`): the earlier builder had built it and it is the session M50 grows to. M50's own gates (200-260 s per layout, every exit reachable with 3 dash styles x 2 aims, Minimal / Splat shapes for the new enemies) are not run here. (2) The launcher cue is at 4 s, not 3 s; Auto's and Survival's launcher lines at 7 s, not 6 s. (3) Auto also gets the `evolve` basic and its passives lines in S10 (M50.4c1's S9 basics carried). (4) `tools/export.ps1` gains an S10 boot block but was not run (this worktree never exports). (5) No `--probe=human` or `give_*` S10 runs: S10 has no give step (the hand has room at 4 s). (6) The design's 16-seed Arcade target was measured on 8 seeds x 3 layouts.
- **Numbers (bot numbers, seeds 1-8 per cell; logs `.check/m50_3c/bots2/`).** Invincible: 72/72 cleared, no `end=limit`. Median clear per layout (rooms / gauntlet / shifting): Arcade 215.9 / 199.8 / 200.0 s, Auto 193.7 / 181.1 / 178.8 s, Survival 233.5 / 207.8 / 204.7 s (max/min of the nine medians 1.31, of the per-path medians about 1.15; Auto shifting 1.2 s under the 180 s floor, not retuned). Arcade fused 24/24, median 61.5-67.5 s (Cluster 15, Prism 9); max rank 29-35 (the bot opens all ~34 packs). Mortal (time to death; hit kinds): Arcade 24/24 deaths, medians 54-63 s (projectile 84, contact 34, hot edge 2); Auto 22/24, medians 64-98 s (projectile 105, contact 11, explosion 2); Survival 23/24, medians 71-142 s (contact 160, projectile 123, hot edge 15). The design's mortal guards hold: Arcade <= Survival + 3 (24 vs 23), Arcade <= Auto + 2 (24 vs 22). Not retuned from bot numbers (AGENTS 6).
- **Hashes.** Gates `.check/m50_3c/gates.sh` (M50.6b's matrix plus S10 rows): 142 runhash summaries, 0 failures; render sheet exit 0 (ERRORS: 0); GATES_DONE. Every older ref equal: S7 and S8 / S9 Arcade `ref_m50_6`, S8 / S9 Auto and Survival `ref_m50_5`, Stage 1 `ref_m47_6c1` / `ref_m47_6c2`, the v6 loop `runhash_m41*`; S8 / S9 pause and handoff bases. **New `.check/ref_m50_3c/`**: `s10_<path>_<layout>_hash.jsonl` for 3 paths x 3 layouts (seeds 1 2), each replayed twice with 0 failures, plus (rooms) `--look=pixel`, `--skins=off`, `--shell` at both views, `--shell --look=pixel` and `--shell --handoff=120`. `tools/check.ps1`: S9's chain line now expects `choice=next_session`, a new S10 chain line (v0.8.2->v0.8.3, `to_be_continued`), and a `bot_s10_<path>` loop with no invincible `end=limit`. GUT 1382/1382 inside `check.ps1 -BotRuns` (73 scripts, no script errors, `CHECK PASSED`; bot chain 11 sessions, play 760.6 s: a bot number; S10 on Arcade won in 201.6 s, 170/170, v0.8.2->v0.8.3, `to_be_continued`).
- **Captures** (windowed 1280x720, inert save, hosted, Pixel; `.check/m50_3c/cap/`, script `.check/m50_3c/capture.gd`): `arsenal_rooms_{10s,shielder,part2,part3}.png`, `auto_gauntlet_*.png`, `survival_shifting_*.png`: the launcher card / level-up / crate lines, the purple shielder with its arc, part 2's arena swap with the sniper line, part 3 with the hot band drawn on the arena's edges.
- **Review (self-review, one build agent):** the invincible stalls (Auto gauntlet seed 3: a chaser behind a lane wall; Survival: a shielder's front, then a sniper across a wall) were bot problems, fixed in the bot only. A live shielder never exposed `shield_arc_deg`, so the M46 flank rule never fired: new test `test_the_live_shielder_answers_the_flank_rule` (fails without the property). The first flank fix (no reach limit) made Survival worse (6/8 capped): the reach limit came from that run.

### 6.44 As built (M50.7): polish, packs, fuses and Auto cards

Built in the `build/polish` worktree on M50.6a/b (ea37666). `BUILD = "M50.7"`. The plan file (`.check/polish_m51c/plan.md`) names it M51c; renamed M50.7 (the test file `test_polish_m50_7.gd`, `.check/m50_7/`). Items 1-19 and the three line edits; the owner questions Q1-Q13 were not built. Presentation and bot_runs only: no hash ref moved.

- **Pack beats** (`ui/pick_panel.gd`, items 4-5): with `beats` on (any windowed run; off headless, so run_hash and GUT keep the old timing) a pack drops (DROP_TIME 0.30 s), tears (TEAR_TIME 0.25 s) and then deals; the old 0.4 s lock follows. A pick slots into the hand (SLOT_TIME 0.35 s) before the panel closes. All in real time while the tree is paused (paused time is not game time, so no hash moves). Reduce flashing cuts the drop and the tear to 0.3 s in all (2 x REDUCE_BEAT, split in the drop : tear ratio) and the slot to 0.225 s, fades only. A fuse shows its two parents for the first half of `fuse_time()`, then the result (review: FUSE_BEAT 0.9 s in a window so the fused card reads, FUSE_TIME 0.4 s headless or with reduce flashing); while the parents show, the rail's HAND shows them too (`PickPanel.fuse_hand`). The pack art (review) prints BOOSTER and 3 CARDS in the rail's block capitals, sits lower (clear of the title), and on the tear its top strip peels off with RRRIP, three light shafts and six sparks (none under reduce flashing). The chosen card flies to the real hand (`PickPanel.hand_target`, set by `Main.pick_hand_target`: the rail's HAND module, below the game view, or the flat hand widget). New sounds (`systems/sound.gd`, appended after the dashes so the old streams keep their bytes): `pack_tear`, `card_flip`, `card_slot`, `fuse_chime`, on the UI bus.
- **The give and replace steps** (items 1-2): each hand card is drawn as a choice face (`CardFace` mini `&"choice"`): the focused one has the gold ring, the card that would make a recipe has a mint edge (`link`, apart from the focus), a locked card is dim with a lock; the link's note ("links with the Laser") sits under its card, and the Keep / Back chip is as wide as its text (on Pixel "Back to the pack" was cut short at 200 px). Hovering a card focuses it (review: pack cards too; a card lights on the focus only, so arrow keys and a resting mouse never light two; the lock's end focuses the hovered card); dressed cards lose their tooltip. Review: the linked card's mint REPLACE sits on an ink chip.
- **Hand and rail** (items 3, 6, 10, 11): `HandWidget` bursts a ranked-up card ("RANK 5!", RANK_TIME 0.6 s) and pulses the stack once per landed pack (STACK_PULSE 0.5 s; both steady under reduce flashing); "[F] open" reads larger on tier 0. The hand preview shows a socket between two cards of a recipe. The rail's PACK module counts the next pack after the opened and the carried ones (the pack on screen once: `PathRunner.pack_on_screen`, else "pack N" read one higher while its panel was open), says "pick 1 of N" with the next pack's real card count (S8's pool gives 2; review: "pick 1 of 2 or 3" when an epic card may join, its roll unknown), "open - N waiting", and the PRISM READY strip says "[F] open a pack to fuse" ("FUSE is in this pack" while one is open). Review: the header's xN and the flat HUD's "N packs [F] open" count only the waiting packs (`HandWidget.waiting_count`), the flat stack does not pulse for a session's carried packs, a rail hand card flashes only on a rank rise, and the "+N" pip chip stays inside its card. Rail hand cards flash on a rank-up. The level-up legend never shrinks under the module's small size.
- **Card text** (items 7, 12, 13): an odd (numbers-only) rank's gain ends "next: <mechanic>" (`WeaponCurve.gain_text`); an Arcade face draws it as a NEXT tag on the art box (`CardFace.face_of` splits it off, so the two-line gain keeps its breakpoints: in the gain line the teaser fell past the second line; review: on Pixel in the rail's block capitals, wrapped to a second row, never cut; on tier 0 its size steps down to fit); faces carry the mechanic icon. Review: a gain longer than two lines drops its last whole sentences ("joins your hand: The gun you started with."), and the Pixel socket's "fuse ready" is READY in block capitals. Auto breakpoints read the path's enemy HP (`enemy_hp_mult`, 0.7 on Auto) and Might (review: `LevelCard.live_damage_gain` swaps the whole damage part, so a rank whose 0.7-HP text has no breakpoint keeps "damage a -> b"; before, Pistol 9, Shotgun 9 and Laser 5 read only "next: ..."); a multi-pellet damage rank says "(per pellet)" after the damage, not after the teaser. The Haste card shows the pistol's real shots a second after tick rounding (`LevelCard.haste_rate_text`: "5.5/s -> 5.5/s" when a rank does not change the tick count; review: it reads the Minigun once the Pistol evolved, `LevelCard.paired_now`, and a rate never wraps inside "/s"). Review: on Pixel the header's GIFT / NEW / MAX tag, the "LV 3>4" badge and the banner's "PICK 1 OF 3 · GAME PAUSED" are block capitals (Pixelify read "BIFT", "LV !"). The EVOLVE card names the launch window when the session has a nerf cue. A card a session `give` put first wears GIFT (`PickOffer.given`).
- **The live nerf** (item 8): `PathRunner.nerf_landed(text, weapon)` after the two log events; `Main._on_nerf_row` writes `$ hotfix minigun ... ok` in the terminal and, with the rail on, a hotfix slip carrying the patch note (`RailFrame.mark_nerf`, `RailChat.mark_hotfix(prefix, label, body)`); on the flat HUD the note is a toast (`PickPanel.note`). Review: the slip's note never shrinks under the strip's size (at 8 px "1.5" read "1 5"): "Balance: " goes and whole ", " parts drop from the end until it fits (`RailChat.slip_body`; at the capture's width "Minigun 30 -> 15 shots/s"). `nerf_window` reads a cue's path condition term by term (`PathRunner.path_cond_fits`: a `!answer:path_x` cue no longer counts for path x). The run log is unchanged.
- **F2** (item 9): an ITEMS row (built items: Haste, Area, Might; a press adds at level 1, then +1) and an EVOLUTIONS row (Minigun, Supernova, Thunderclap; unbuilt ones shown disabled).
- **Speech and the bubble** (items 14-15): the level-up's pick line is said on picks 1, 2, then every 5th (`PathRunner.pick_line_due`: ten picks say three). The mascot's bubble parks while a pick panel is open (`Mascot.park_bubble`) and comes back if the line is still up.
- **Caption and plates** (item 16): the caption names the path title ("stage 2 · fresh paint · arcade"); Auto card plates use the rail's block capitals on Pixel.
- **Bot** (item 18): `BotInput.hunt_last` (off by default) adds the default policy's "go and get the last enemies" branch to the Arsenal, Auto and Survival policies; `tools/bot_runs.gd` turns it on (`--probe=nohunt` keeps it off) and adds `--probe=human_open` (a stacked pack opened every 480 ticks, measure only). `tools/run_hash.gd` never sets it, so its refs keep the old bot: **bot_runs and run_hash now play slightly differently; bot numbers are bot numbers.**
- **Lines** (text only, same cues and moods): S8 at 16 drops "Gold cards are rarer."; S9 at 94 "Nova evolves with it, one day."; S9 evolve_ready "It's ready. Take EVOLVE." `lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **Deviations:** (1) the plan's base was `build/mascots` (with M51a); this tree is M50.6a/b, where `Mascot.keep_out` / `bubble_rect` already exist, so nothing was missing. (2) The pack beats are off headless (`beats := DisplayServer.get_name() != "headless"`) rather than gated on a setting, so no headless run (hash, bot, GUT) waits on them; GUT sets `beats` to test them. (3) The beat lock keeps the old `<= 0` compare plus a 0.0005 s epsilon for the new countdowns.

The review pass added 10 tests (32 in all), each failing without its fix (the Auto damage-line test was run against the old swap: Pistol 9, Shotgun 9 and Laser 5 read only "next: ..."). `check.ps1 -BotRuns`: CHECK PASSED, GUT 1395/1395. `.check/m50_7/gates.sh` (the M50.6b matrix): every summary 0 failures, `g0_sheet_iou` exit 0: **no ref moved** (refs ref_m47_6c1/6c2/6d1/6d2, ref_m50_5, ref_m50_6, runhash_m41). ### 6.45 As built (M51b): the version odometer title

Built in the `build/title` worktree on M50.6b. `BUILD = "M51b"`. The owner picked concept B of the title mockup v2 (`.check/mockups/title_v2/title_upgrade.html`: "I like concept B"). Presentation only: no ref moved.

- **The odometer** (`ui/title/odometer.gd`, new, `TitleOdometer`): the title's hero, a plum housing with gold rivets, a gold block "v" and dots, and three paper wheels (major, minor, patch) with 5x7 block digits that roll in their windows (the drum shaded top and bottom). No game name. Its history is the campaign's own data: v0.0.0, then one entry per done session in campaign order folded over `SessionDef.bump` (as `RailModel` folds them), each naming the module that session shipped (its first patch note, else its title), never past the save's version, plus a last entry for the save's version when the walk does not end on it.
- **The boot:** the wheels rewind to v0.0.0 (0.5 s), tick through the history (0.34 s a tick, all ticks within 3 s), the readout naming each ("v0.3.0 / shipped: Getting hurt (sorry)"), and land on the save's version with a 5 px thunk and 16 gold sparks (0.7 s). The mascot drops onto the housing and says "Landed. Every number there is something we shipped."; 1.4 s later the focused card takes the wheels and speaks. Moving the focus (keys, pad, mouse) skips the rest of the boot. After the unveil there is no boot: the wheels stand on the version and the mascot keeps its owning-up line until the focus moves.
- **The menu row** (`ui/title/title_card.gd`, new, `TitleCard`): five cards on the plum grid (Continue, New game, Replay once S8 is done, Options, Quit). Each is a Button (focus, mouse, pad, its text for StartScreen and the tests, drawn transparent) wearing a TitleCard drawn behind it: paper at rest, lifted and lit when focused (gold; New game red, Replay teal), the name, an ink slot saying where it leads ("> v0.8.?", "> v0.0.0", "> v0.8.0", "v0.8.1 tune", "keep v0.8.1") and an up arrow. Focus wraps left / right and up / down; hovering a card focuses it. The root `MenuPanel` stays (its look's panel too), with `self_modulate` 0.
- **Focus targets:** Continue spins the patch wheel (7 digits a second) with a "?" badge ("next: v0.8.?", "play Stage 2 · Session 9 to decide what ships"); New game rolls every wheel back to v0.0.0 in red ("roll back to v0.0.0"); Replay rolls back to the version the chosen replay session started on, in teal, and follows the Replay menu's session list ("check out v0.8.0"); Options shows a gear and Quit a lock over the housing, both keeping the version ("tune this build", "saved at v0.8.1"). The mascot (the player's body, `Mascot` through its public API: `setup`, `say`, `scale`, `offset`) walks along the housing's top to the wheel that changes (patch wheel, minor wheel, the centre, the "v") and says one line per focus (the mockup's; Continue's is concept A's "The last 1% needs a player.").
- **Kept:** Continue, New game with its confirm ("Start over? Your progress goes; your settings stay.", now under a red "Roll back to v0.0.0?"), Replay (session, path, seed) under a teal "Roll to an old build", Options, Quit, the campaign line (top left, pixel font), the first-launch flash question over the row, Esc / pad B back, the pause chip and build label, the unveil. The confirm and Replay are a modal in the middle with the odometer dimmed (0.62 ink), as the mockup's sub dialogs.
- **Backdrop:** `TitleBackdrop.plum` draws the mockup's plum with a 2 px grid every 48 px, scrolling as before; the door beat keeps the arena grid.
- **Reduce flashing:** no boot roll, no sparks, no thunk, and the wheels snap to each target (no roll); Continue's patch wheel shows a still "?".
- **Colours:** 18 `Palette.ODO_*` constants (the mockup's plum / paper / gold, red and teal).
- **Lines file:** `lines_render.gd` lists the landing line and the five focus lines instead of the four launch lines; `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **Deviations from the mockup:** (1) the module names and versions are the campaign's (S8 lands on "shipped: Day one"; the mockup's were placeholders). (2) Replay rolls to the chosen session's start version (v0.8.0 for Day one), not v0.7.0. (3) The confirm and Replay keep the game's MenuPanel / TitleButton (and the look's panel), not the mockup's paper dialog, so the existing look tests hold. (4) The mascot's bubble is the game's (white, left of the body), not the mockup's paper bubble at the top right. (5) The hint line is plain text ("left / right choose · Enter ok · Esc back"), not key chips. (6) The door beat (S7 and the unveil) still builds M47.4's column; its last frame cuts to the odometer.
- **Numbers.** New `test_title_odometer.gd` (6 tests, each fails without the change: the classes do not exist): the history is the campaign's real versions and modules (S0 "Movement", S7 "The shooter, three ways", a patch-stepped save lands on its own version, S8 started on v0.8.0); the boot names every module and lands on v0.8.0, then Continue speaks; landing sparks and the mascot dropping in; each card's target (Continue "v0.8.?" spinning, New game "v0.0.0", Replay "v0.8.0", Options and Quit "v0.8.1", the mascot at the minor wheel, the cards' slots); the confirm keeps the red roll and dims the readout; reduce flashing (no boot, no spin, a snap, no sparks, no wheel roll). Changed: `test_front_door.gd` `test_the_owner_save_shows_the_unveil_exactly_once` (the second launch now boots the odometer and says the landing line, instead of a launch line). GUT 1369/1369 inside `check.ps1 -BotRuns` (73 scripts, no script errors, `CHECK PASSED`; bot chain 10 sessions, play 559.0 s: a bot number). Gates `.check/m51b/gates.sh` (M50.6b's matrix, unchanged; the newest script in the packs tree is M50.3c's S10 matrix, whose session is not in this tree): 97 runhash summaries, 0 failures; render sheet exit 0 (ERRORS: 0); GATES_DONE. No ref was recorded.
- **Captures** (windowed 1280x720, a scratch save in this tree's own user:// folder, S0..S8 done, v0.8.1; `.check/m51b/shots/`): `01_boot_mid` (v0.4.0, "shipped: Bullets that hit things now", no card lit, no mascot), `02_boot_landed` (v0.8.1, sparks over the housing, the mascot dropping in typing "Landed."), `03_focus_continue` (patch wheel mid-spin, "?" badge, gold card and arrow, "The last 1% needs a player."), `04_focus_new` (v0.0.0, minor and patch digits red, red card), `05_focus_replay` (v0.8.0, patch digit teal), `06_focus_options` (gear, v0.8.1), `07_focus_quit` (lock, the mascot on the "v"), `08_new_game_confirm` and `09_replay_menu` (the modal over the dimmed odometer), `10_reduce_flashing_continue` (a still "?" in the patch window), `11_reduce_flashing_new` (snapped to v0.0.0 in red). They match concept B's board; the deviations above are what differs.

### 6.46 As built (M51): S11 "Depth", without the event

Built in the `build/s11` worktree on M50.3c (7f65bc0). `BUILD = "M51"`. Design `.check/m51/design.md`. Owner decisions of 2026-11-27: S11 is its own PR, Auto's difficulty is not retuned, Arcade's pack rules are unchanged, S7-S10 refs do not move.

- **The session** (`campaign/sessions/s2_05_depth.tres`, `campaign/campaign.tres` entry `s11`): index 11, "Depth", `bump patch` (v0.8.4), the last playable session (S10 now offers Next). S10's basics, `goal clear` in the player's layout (3 parts), the hidden `time_limit 300`, skin pixel, `fresh_start`. Enemies chaser, shooter, `fork:fork_enemy`, **shielder, sniper, swarm** with `spawn_weights` 0.15 each (PLAN 1.9); no scripted variant spawns. Weapons pistol / shotgun / laser / launcher / **orbit**; `known` shotgun, laser, launcher, nova, haste, area, might. `enemy_total_by_path` Arcade 150, Auto 270, Survival **80** (started at 90, tuned once from the invincible bots). Cues: each path's basics at 0 s; `room 1 at 15: give orbit` + "Little shields that spin" (Arcade: the passive slot; Auto: first in the next level-up, and its line waits for `answer:path_auto holds orbit`; Survival: a crate); Survival's battery line at room 1 + 16 s and three `drop battery` (room 1 + 36 s, room 2 + 30 s, room 3 + 12 s); Auto's `[well]` say at room 1 + 30 s; `room 2 at 20: event gravity_through_screen` (a placeholder, below); `room 3 at 6: say thinking: You've earned a break.` then `room 3 at 8: edit edit_enemies_flee` (its own warn line "What if they were scared of you?" follows); S10's `fuse_ready 1` / `fused 1` lines on Arcade. Intro and the `died_in:s2_04_levels` memory line as 3.2; before_play "Depth. Three parts again. Type play." Debrief: "All three at once. You handled it." / "That's on me." / the pitch "You keep looking for your health. Let me fix the screen." then `fork_hud`. Notes "Three new regulars", "Spinning shields". `campaign/lines_stage2.md` regenerated.
- **`fork_hud`** (`devsession/forks/fork_hud.tres`, `devsession/changes/hud_{clean,arcade,onyou}.tres`): campaign data, kind `hud` (new in `Change.KINDS`; `Change.HUDS` = clean / arcade / onyou; `ChangeDB` reports a hud change whose take names no style), 1.12's pitch and take lines. Text-only cards (no HudStyle exists to preview). Nothing reads the answer until M53; S11's `hud` stays "".
- **Weighted variants** (`systems/spawner.gd`, `genres/topdown/topdown_genre.gd`): `spawn_weights_for` keeps a variant id; `Spawner.spawn_one` spawns a weighted variant's base with its params and pack through `Spawner.spawn_variant`, which the spawn cue (`spawn_archetype`) now shares (same draws in the same order). A weighted swarm is one budget unit and brings its pack of 5. A base pick draws exactly as before.
- **`SessionDef.part_rate`** (`campaign/session_def.gd`, `systems/run_loop.gd`): one spawn-rate factor per part on top of `spawn_rate` (empty = 1.0); S11 sets `[1, 1, 0.8]` (3.1's "spawns x0.8" in part 3). Validated: a stage set, values > 0, one per part of every set the session can play.
- **`edit_enemies_flee`** (`rules/edit_enemies_flee/`, `WorldRules.enemies_flee`, `entities/enemy_walker.gd`): an additive counter; while > 0 and gravity is off, walkers steer away from the player around walls (`_steer_around_walls(-to_player)`), with the stuck detector off while they flee (no wander, no `gameplay_rng` draw; it starts fresh after, so "Brave again" is immediate). Shooters and snipers are untouched. 10 s, 1.9's warn / done lines, `set enemies.fear on`. In `test_rule_book`'s `ALL` (the reversibility test).
- **`drop battery`** (`Cue.DROP_KINDS`): Survival's `Drops.drop_now` lands a battery pickup on the free tile nearest the player; Scavenge refills the passive's battery. No new `loot_rng` draw.
- **The event placeholder** (`Cue.PLACEHOLDER_EVENTS = [gravity_through_screen]`): the cue parses and validates without an events folder; `SessionRunner` only logs `event_placeholder` (no EventPlayer call, nothing marked seen, no clock hold, no pause); `tools/bot_runs.gd` skips its event report. M52 builds the event and empties the list.
- **Lint:** `test_mutations`' id lint skips only the `weapons = ` and `known = ` lines of `campaign/sessions/*.tres` (S11's weapons name `orbit`, PLAN v9 1.4's weapon id); the cues and the rest of a session stay linted.
- **Review fixes (M51 review):** `holds <id>:` cues also read held weapon ids (`SessionRunner._held_weapons`, a new `weapons` context key; `SessionDef.problems()` accepts a `WeaponDB` id), so Auto's orbit line waits for its pick. The `sniper_seen` reaction (PLAN 3.3, "Red line! Move!", S11 only): `Reactions` now records a variant under its own id as well as its base's (base keys unchanged). Survival's AMMO module shows the orbit's battery ("orbit · 17 s", then "orbit · battery out"; `RailState._ammo` `battery_title` / `battery`). The debrief's terminal card row drops the name prefix when the pitch already starts with it (`DebriefScreen.card_row`: "Clean. Small numbers in a corner." instead of "clean  Clean. ..."; fork_layout's rows read the same way now). `tools/bot_runs.gd` loads the placeholder list once, above the cue loop.
- **Checks:** `tools/check.ps1`: S10's chain line expects `choice=next_session`; a new S11 chain line (v0.8.3->v0.8.4, `to_be_continued`, `end=cleared`); a `bot_s11_<path>` loop (3i, no invincible `end=limit`). `tools/export.ps1`: an S11 boot block (written, not run: this worktree never exports). Tests that pinned S10 as the last session updated (`test_s10_levels`, `test_campaign`, `test_front_door`, `test_cooking`, `test_s7_tryout`, `test_stage2_door`), plus `test_dev_session`'s change / fork lists and the Stage 1 rules fixture (`rules_stage1_m43_3.json` gains `enemies_flee: 0`, a new field joining on purpose).
- **Tests** (`tests/gut/test_s11_depth.gd`, 16, each failing on the base; the review added Auto's orbit line on `holds orbit`, the flee edit at part 3 + 8 s, the sniper seen under its own id, card rows without the repeated name, Survival's battery on the rail, and in the flee test "no wander while fleeing", which fails without the walker fix): S11 follows S10 and is last (v0.8.4, S10 offers Next, no scripted spawns); fork_hud's data and its bad-take check; the debrief's pitch then the question; weighted shielder (arc) / sniper (line) / swarm (pack of 5, one budget unit); a base weight still spawns one plain base (guard; S5's hash is the gate); part 3 at x0.8 and its validation; the flee edit turns a chaser away and back, a shooter still winds up; the event placeholder logs and does nothing else; the orbit on every path and a dropped battery refilling it; the well only after its tagged say; the 3.5 S11 rows.

They're scared of you now." became the say at part 3 + 6 s ("You've earned a break." only; the edit's warn line says the rest) and the edit at + 8 s, so enemies are on screen to run away and the chat does not say it twice. ### 6.47 As built (M47.7a): Stage 1 chains

Built in the `build/m47-7a` worktree on PLAN v9.5 (4f192e8). `BUILD = "M47.7a"`. Spec 0.9.1 (the slide is M47.7b, not built here). Presentation and flow only: no gameplay code, no RNG, no save or telemetry key.

- **`SessionDef.auto_start`** (`campaign/session_def.gd`, default false): true on `s1_00_nothing`, `s1_01_move`, `s1_02_enemies`, `s1_03_shooting`. Nothing reads a stage number.
- **The host** (`ui/always_on/game_host.gd`): `hold(boot := false)` still sets the HANDOFF pause at tick 0. It decides once per hold (`auto_start_due(session, boot, after_death)` and `gate_due()`) between the play gate (`_open_gate`, unchanged) and the new auto hold (`_open_auto`): the session's own `before_play` lines type (never the campaign default, which asks for play), `ChatInput.open_gate` is not called (so no ghost, no focus, no `(type play)` hint, and no play pill in `terminal.gd` or `rail_reply.gd`, which both read `chat_input.gate`), and `_auto_step` releases `AUTO_START_DELAY` (1.0 s, real time) after `terminal.is_busy()` turns false. No `you> play` row on that release; `play_wait_s` and `gate_typed` are 0 for it. Enter while the lines type shows them at once and the delay runs from there; no other key does anything (the input is closed, so keys reach the frozen game: in S0 the square strains).
- **Where the gate stays:** S4 and later (`auto_start` false); `_ready`'s hold is `hold(true)`, so a boot or Continue gates once unless the session is the campaign's first (index 0); `Main._end_session(&"move_on")` sets `host.gate_next`, consumed by the next hold; a restart from a gate sets it too, so a gate that was up comes back. "again" and the pause menu's restart after play keep the carry (no hold). The installer's question still goes first (`answer_first_run` and a stage card's close open the hold through the same `_open_hold`).
- **Off switches:** the auto hold needs `gate_due()` (a live host, `auto_beats`, not `auto_play`). A tool host (`run_hash --handoff=`, `bot_runs`) holds exactly as before and never runs the real-time release; a bot chain and `--autostart` do not hold at all.
- **Forks and the death ask** are the debrief's, before `next_session`: nothing changed, now pinned by tests.
- **Lines** (drafts, the owner's to edit): S0 "hi. i'm the ai making this game." / "that square is you. it's the whole game so far." / "hang on. wiring the keys."; S1 "ok. the keys are in. try now."; S2 "they're in. you can't shoot yet. run."; S3 "Gun's in. Go get them." `before_play_default_for` is not used for these four (each has its own lines, and the auto hold passes `fallback = false`). `campaign/lines_stage2.md` holds Stage 2 only: not regenerated.
- **Deviations:** (1) the pause menu opens during an auto hold (as at a gate) and stops the 1.0 s count while it is up; 0.9.1 does not say. (2) A restart from a gate gates again even on S0-S3 (0.9.1 only names "again" after play). (3) Under a hold that is not `gate_due()` (tool hosts) an `auto_start` session keeps the old gate state, so `--handoff=` runs are byte-for-byte the old path.
- **Tests** (`tests/gut/test_play_gate.gd`, 21 -> 31; the ten new ones do not parse or fail on the base): only S0-S3 have `auto_start` and none of their lines says "play", plus the `auto_start_due` table; an auto session holds at tick 0, types its lines, ignores keys and typed `play`, Enter skips the typing, it is still held at 0.8 s and released at 1.0 s with no gate, ghost, hint, `you> play` row or rail pill; no chat pill on S3; the pause menu holds the count; a boot into S1 gates and the next session chains, a boot into S0 does not gate; the installer goes first, then S0 starts alone; S4 gates in the chain and a tool host on S1 never arms the timer; `gate_next` gates once, "again" carries, a restart from that gate gates again; the death ask waits (no swap behind it) and Main sets `gate_next` on move on; S2's body pick waits (90 frames, no swap), then its answer lets the live chain reach S3, which starts on its own. Moved, not deleted: the tick-0 gate test, the strain-off test and the move-keys test now boot into S1; the idle hint boots into S2; the S0 strain test runs under the auto hold.
- **Numbers:** GUT 1449/1450 in the full run (76 scripts, 291 s); the one failure is `test_pack_stack`'s bot-epic test (`test_the_bot_takes_an_epic_that_melts_a_card_and_never_one_that_costs_a_card`), which draws a random run seed (`_arena()` with no seed) and fails when the random pack holds an epic of its own: alone it passed 2 of 3 runs and 23/23 on the base, so it is not this change; `test_play_gate` 31/31 (all files, no script errors in the log). Hash gates, `check.ps1 -BotRuns` and `export.ps1`: run by the orchestrator, see PR. Expected: no ref changed (the hold is hash-neutral; tool and bot hosts take the old path).
- **Captures:** none taken by the builder (the windowed checks belong to the orchestrator's pass; the change shows no new drawing, only the absence of the ghost and the pill).
- **Review:** the builder's own pass only (small patch). Found and fixed while building: the campaign default line ("type play.") would have typed for an `auto_start` session without lines (`gate_lines(..., fallback)`); a stale `play_wait_s` would have been logged for a session with no gate.
- **Still open (defaults taken):** see section 9.

### 6.48 As built (M47.7b): the handover slide

Built in the `build/m47-7b` worktree on M47.7a (ab09898). `BUILD = "M47.7b"`. Spec 0.9.1, "The slide". Gameplay code: the slide is real movement. No RNG draw, no save or telemetry key.

- **The tried direction:** `SessionRunner.last_tried` (a unit vector on 8 ways, `ZERO` until a try). `_count_tried` sets it on every tick the locked player's input points somewhere (the same rounding as the `tried` count); a release keeps it. A session that can move never sets it.
- **The start point's field:** `Main.next_tried` beside `next_start` and `next_lead`: set in `Main._end_session` by `GameHost.capture_tried` (the runner's `last_tried` when `capture_start` carries, else `ZERO`), consumed in `Main._ready` into `Genre.carried_tried`, cleared by `Main.clear_start()` (a retry, Restart / F5, the pause menu's restart, the title). `RunLoop.start_tried` keeps what the start carried, for the tools. The run record and telemetry do not have it.
- **The slide:** `TopdownGenre.apply_basics()` calls `Player.start_slide(dir)` once, when `movement` is granted and `carried_tried` is not zero (at build for S1, or later if a cue adds `movement`). While it runs, `Player._physics_process` sets `mover.speed` to `Tuning.handover_slide_mult` (3.0) times the normal speed and `mover.wish` to the direction, and reads no move, jump or dash input (the weapon code below is untouched; S1 has no gun). It ends when a slide collision of the last tick faces the direction (normal dot direction under -0.1) or after `Tuning.handover_slide_cap` (2.0 s of game time, a safety: see Numbers). A wall stops the body dead (`Mover.stop()`: a floating `move_and_slide` keeps the speed against a wall); the cap drops the speed to the normal one at once. Then `Player.slid` is true. No rule, no mutation and no "mode": the state is three fields on the player, and a zero direction never reaches `start_slide`.
- **The AI's line:** a new bare cue time `slid` (`Cue.WHENS`, `BARE_WHENS`; the runner's context reads `Player.slid`). S1's data has `"slid: say guilty: ...too fast. fixing."` as its last cue, so the older cues keep their index. It goes through the usual script voice (`SessionRunner.say`).
- **The lean:** `StrainFX.wanted()` returns the slide's direction while it is pending (S1's hold, the game frozen) or running: the square still pulls the way it tried while the AI types "ok. the keys are in. try now.", then shoots off. Presentation only (it reads `Player.slide_dir()`, writes the Visual).
- **Tools:** `run_hash.gd --start=<x>,<y>,<dx>,<dy>` (dx, dy in -1, 0, 1) starts a session with a direction; `--chain` carries it with `GameHost.capture_tried` and prints it in `"start"` when it is not zero, so the old cold-replay gate line (`--start=$st`) still works. `bot_runs.gd` prints the same `start=<x>,<y>,<dx>,<dy>` on a session whose start had one (S1 in a chain); every other line is as before.
- **Deviations:** (1) 0.9.1 said bots never try a key in S0. They do (see 0.9.1 "Determinism", corrected): the builder stopped and reported, and the owner chose to accept the chain ref change (2026-11-28). (2) The speed ramps with the body's own acceleration (0 to 990 px/s in 0.31 s) instead of jumping to 3x on the first tick. (3) At the end the speed left over is dropped (a wall: to zero; the cap: to the normal speed), else the square kept 990 px/s into the wall and took 0.4 s to answer the keys. (4) The cap is 2.0 s, not 0.9.1's first 1.2 s: at 1.2 s a left or right slide from S1's start stopped in the open after about 21 tiles, and the owner's rule is "until it hits a wall" (2026-11-28). (5) Tries are counted only while the sim runs: keys pressed during S0's opening lines (frozen) strain the square but are not the "last try".
- **Tests** (`tests/gut/test_handover_slide.gd`, 14 new; none parses on the base): the runner's last try (left, kept on release, a diagonal and a near-axis stick snapped to 8 ways, a dead-zone nudge ignored, never in a session that can move); `capture_tried` carries with the start and not after a death, to another stage or from no game; Restart, F5, the title, a debrief retry and a boot carry none; **a "left" try in S0, Next, and S1 slides left at 3x (peak 990 px/s) with the right key held and ignored, stops at the left wall before the cap, says the line once, then moves right at 330 px/s**; **from S1's real start each of the 8 directions ends on its wall, stopped dead, at least 0.2 s before the cap** (it fails at the old 1.2 s); with no wall in reach (a start at the far side of the room) the cap ends it after 2.0 s with the normal speed at once; a diagonal stops at the first wall; `movement` added later starts the slide then, once; **a zero direction: no slide state, the normal speed every tick, no line, and the same positions tick by tick as a second zero run** (a run with a direction differs); the `slid` cue parses bare and is S1's last cue; the lean follows the slide; the bot line prints the direction only when there is one.
- **Numbers:** time to the wall from S1's start (tile 33, 14 of 66 x 22; measured by the test): left 1.70 s, right 1.67 s, up 0.77 s, down 0.47 s, the two upward diagonals 1.00 s, the two downward ones 0.60 s. The cap is 2.0 s, so the longest slide keeps 0.3 s of margin; the cap alone would let the square cover about 1830 px (38 tiles). GUT and the hash gates: see below ("Gates").
- **Gates:** GUT 1464/1464 in the full run (77 scripts, 306 s, no error line in the log); `test_handover_slide` 14/14. **Hash gates (two phases):** the build was committed before any hash run, because other worktrees were being gated on the shared `user://` folder. Expected, to be confirmed when the refs are recorded: every cold ref equal (`ref_m47_6c1` campaign and S4, and everything in Stage 2: a cold start carries no direction, and S1's new cue is its last and never fires without a slide); **the chain ref moves from S1 on, on purpose** (the bot's S0 tries make it slide in S1, which moves where S1 ends and so where S2 to S6 start) and is re-recorded as `.check/ref_m47_7b/chain_hash.jsonl` (owner OK, 2026-11-28). `tools/check.ps1 -BotRuns` now compares the chain with `ref_m47_7b` (it skips the step while that folder is missing). `check.ps1 -BotRuns`, the gate script and `export.ps1`: run by the orchestrator.
- **Captures:** none taken by the builder (the windowed checks belong to the orchestrator's pass).
- **Review:** the builder's own pass only (small patch). Found and fixed while building: a slide that ended exactly on the cap never set `slid` (the line was never said); the body kept its 990 px/s against the wall after the slide.
- **Still open (defaults taken):** see section 9.

## 7. What v9 must answer (M43 report, G14a, G14b, G14)
**Asked in the M43 report (one line each; none blocks M43–M50; unanswered = the default in 9):**
1. **Length:** Stage 1 is ~7 min against ~20, Stage 2 is planned at 30–40 against 45, and the campaign target is ~2 h 30. Grow now (a session in a stage's middle) or later?
2. **Versions:** Stage 2 as patch bumps (v0.8.1–v0.8.5, the boss v0.9.0), or one minor per session as Stage 1 did?
3. **Forks:** 13 across the campaign (14 with dash aim) against "about 10–12". Drop `fork_hud` (the AI picks the style, announced)? Needed before M51. **Answered v9.1: keep it** (owner-polish-vision).
4. **G13's open items** (PLAYTEST 7a–7j): the gift's length, the event seen at start, S5's difficulty, S6's length, my lines.

**G14a (the build-changing questions first):**
5. **Are the in-run 1-of-3 picks** (Arsenal's cards, Auto's level-ups) fine, or do they feel like the old patch prompts you didn't want?
6. **Is "power stays in the session" fun** (back to the pistol every session), or does it feel like lost progress?
7. (3, if still open.)
8. **Do the three paths feel like three games?** Which did you keep after S7, and did S7's tastes help you choose? (again at G14 after a whole stage)
9. **The look moved** from the end of Stage 1 (ROADMAP 2 / 6.5) to S8's debrief, after a live try-on. Is that the right moment?
10. **The lines file:** any tone edits? (I draft, you edit; ROADMAP 6.7)

**G14b and G14:**
11. **Do the three looks feel worth choosing?** (v9.1: Pixel / Minimal / Splat, code-drawn: too programmer-y? Splat or voxel?) Did the S8 try-on help, and did your look feel like a reward when it landed in S9?
12. **Is the boss a fair checkpoint**, not a damage sponge? How many tries? Did the hint and the help feel kind or patronising? (G14b on the slice; G14 on your pick)
13. Does **gravity through the screen** read as a funny accident, like the gravity flip did? Too long, too short? Did the crack gag land?
14. Meeting the three enemies in S10 and keeping all three: right, or would you rather have picked one?
15. Is Stage 2 **quick enough, with something new every session**, and does S8 feel new after S7's tastes?
16. Is the AI still **the main force** once the path is in, and does it seem to **remember** you (your dash, your Stage 1 enemy, the drawer)?
17. (v9.1) **The front door:** do the title, options and pause feel like the game growing up? Anything missing from the menus? (G14b, else G14)
18. (v9.2; replaces 9 and 11) **The transformation:** does the first asset feel like a reward, and are the visual bugs funny or confusing? Is warm Pixel the look you wanted? (async on S9's clip, then G14b)
19. (v9.2; extends 12) **The switch:** does it land as "what is happening?!" and not as a real bug? Is the RPG half readable with only the AI guessing along, and the right length? Does Retry at the switch feel right? (G14b)
20. (v9.2) **Per-path boss tuning:** is the Big Square fair on Survival and Arsenal, and still a fight on Auto? (G14b, G14)
21. (v9.2) **Path depth:** after the depth pieces, does each path have a build you want to chase? (the one-page design before M50.1, then G14)

## 8. What happens to old pieces
| Piece | In v9 |
|---|---|
| `Shooter` + `Tuning.shoot_cooldown` / `projectile_speed` | the pistol `WeaponDef` holds the numbers in path sessions; with `path = ""` the pistol still reads `Tuning` (so `--set=` and every hash hold) |
| `AiDirector` meter, `PromptPanel`, `Offer`, `taste.gd` | `PromptPanel`'s look is reused by **`PickPanel`** (a subclass taking a **`PickOffer`**); `taste.gd`'s weights lean the offers. `Offer`, `AiDirector.on_choice` and the PATCH gauge stay legacy-only (they apply Mutations to the stack) |
| `PLAN.md` (v8) | archived as `PLAN.v8.md` ("Phase 5, done") in M43's step 0 |
| `systems/look.gd` (`Look.use`, `path_for`), `systems/looks/look_1_*.tres` | kept; new `look_1_pixel/minimal/splat` and `look_2_*` presets (v9.1); `Look.try_on` added; colours go through the new `systems/palette.gd`, bodies through `systems/skins/` |
| `test_readability.gd` `LOOK_READERS` | + `systems/palette.gd` and `systems/skins/skins.gd` only (v9.1) |
| `ui/start_screen.gd` (the raw column), the save's `settings` block, the `pause` action (v9.1) | `StartScreen` stays the main scene and builds `TitleScreen` from S7 on (the raw column before, and always in the legacy loop); `Settings` wraps the save's settings additively; `pause` opens `PauseMenu` (it still closes the v6 build log) |
| v9's Pixel / Neon / Paper shader looks, `LookLayer`, the "no per-entity art" rule (v9.1) | dropped for the code-drawn looks (1.7); a full-screen pass exists only if a look has a post shader (none at tier 1) |
| `RunState._under_cap` (combined 200) | split into player 140 / enemy 60 in path sessions; unchanged in Stage 1 and the legacy loop |
| `campaign.index` in the save | derived from the new `campaign.next` id |
| `Fork.answer()`, `Fork.shelf()` | the base of `Unlocks.answer/take` and `CampaignDB.shelf` |
| v6 mutations `echo`, `bouncing_projectiles`, `explode_on_hit`, `orbit`, `well`, `enemies_split`, `elite`, `horde_mode`, `auto_attack` | their code is reused for weapon fields (bounce, blast), the orbit weapon (`mutations/orbit/orbit_shards.gd`), Auto's `well` (`mutations/well/well_field.gd`), the splitter, the shielder's shield (`mutations/elite/elite_mark.gd`), Auto's density and Auto's targeting. **No evolution reskins them** (1.4). The patches themselves stay in the legacy loop |
| `enemy_walker.gd` (3 inline state machines) | split into per-archetype behaviour scripts (**M43.1**), hashes identical |
| `Unlocks.ARCHETYPES`, `cue.gd:29`, `spawner.gd:7` | `EnemyDB.BASES` (`Unlocks.ARCHETYPES` an alias) |
| `mutations/hunter/hunter_body.gd` | the boss entity's pattern |
| v6 stage sets (breakout, gauntlet, shifting), `exit_gate.gd`, the stage swap | the layout fork's answers (new campaign `.tres` files, 1.8); `StageDef.prompts` forced to 0 in sessions; the v6 `stages` fork (`genres/topdown/devsession/…`) stays legacy-only |
| `look_1` fork and its 3 takes (punchy / arcade / clean) | legacy-only; their juice numbers feed the 3 new looks' juice |
| `LookPreset.palette` (dead data) | becomes the palette roles |
| `fork_enemy` (Tank · Bomber · Lancer, Stage 1) | unchanged; S10's memory lines read its answer. There is no second enemy fork |
| `min_session` / `max_session` on reactions | `after` / `until` by session id (converted 1:1), then audited for Stage 2 (1.13) |
| `wait` (E / pad Y) | stays for B2's turn mode; E is also `weapon_next` in sessions |
| Mutation `boss_only` / `boss_weight`, `ai_patch_added(owner &"boss")` (v4) | stay parked; the boss uses the rule book like everything else |
| Per-fork flags (`--dash=`, `--dash-aim=`) | aliases of `--answer=` |
| `check.ps1`'s hard-coded campaign end (`"index":7`, `sessions=7`, `choice=to_be_continued` on s1_06, `v0.6.0->v0.7.0`) and export.ps1's `7 sessions … 30 reactions` | rewritten by id and data counts (M43) |
| The legacy loop | stays until Phase 8 (v8 9) |
| Rework, rollback charges | parked |

- **Look timing (ROADMAP 6.5, v8 9):** **asked in S8's debrief, after the AI tries all three on you live**; lands in S9. This **changes the ROADMAP's plan** (the end of Stage 1, "so Stage 2 is already yours"), so G14a asks it (7.9). Reasons: S7 already hosts the path, two big picks back to back are heavy, and the preview uses your own S8 frame in your path. Alternative A: ask it at the end of S6 with stills. Alternative B: ask it with the path in S7.
- **The three looks (ROADMAP 6.6; v9.1, owner-polish-vision):** Pixel / Minimal / Splat (pixel-art gaussian splats), every entity drawn in code, as 1.7. Spares: `look_voxel` for Splat (1.7's decision rule), `look_terminal` (green-on-black CRT) for any other look that fails G14b. (v9's Neon / Paper and the "darker palette" alternative are dropped.)
- **In-run 1-of-3 picks** for Arsenal and Auto (0.4): kept, as ROADMAP 1.7 says, and asked at G14a against dev-session-big-only. Alternative: Arsenal's cards come as drops you walk over, and Auto's level-ups are chosen by the AI from your taste ("Here, take the laser") with no panel.
- **Power stays in the session:** every session starts at the pistol, rank 1 (ROADMAP 1.9.7). Alternative: each session starts with the previous session's weapons in hand at rank 1. Asked at G14a.
- **The tryout order:** fixed Arsenal → Survival → Auto (closest to what you know first, the most different last; Auto's taste has no passives so recency and power don't stack). Alternative: by seed. Telemetry logs which taste came last.
- **Five forks in Stage 2** (path, look, layout, HUD, boss): ROADMAP 2's four plus the look moved from Stage 1. 13 across the campaign counting Stages 4–5 and the owner-added dash (14 with dash aim), against "about 10–12". **`fork_hud` kept: answered by the owner (v9.1, owner-polish-vision: "UI polish with 3 styles").**
- **No second enemy fork:** the splitter (S8) and the shielder, sniper and swarm (S10) are announced and **all stay** (enemy type was Stage 1's fork, ROADMAP 2; content = sum). Alternative: `fork_enemy_2` on the same data (S10's debrief, one stays, two shelved), +1 fork.
- **Gravity through the screen as presentation** (frozen play, proxies), not physics. Alternative: real growth and downward gravity in the live simulation (riskier: collisions grow near walls, enemies keep attacking). The crack persists to S12 (alternative: gone with the undo).
- **Event seen at start** (v8's rule; G13 7b unanswered): kept for the new event. Alternative: seen at its punchline (the smack), so a death before the smack replays it.
- **Boss hints and help:** from the 2nd loss, a hint for the phase that got you plus the help offer; then an offer 2 losses after each accept; 3 levels (+2 HP, boss HP ×0.8, the AI joins). Alternative: help from the 3rd loss; or automatic help (no button).
- **Boss tries for the bot:** 8 before `boss_unbeaten`, each bounded on its own; the human has no limit. Mortal boss numbers are reported; the only mortal gate is feasibility (help 3 wins ≥ 1 of 8 in 16/16).
- **The boss backstop:** moves to the next phase only; P3 opens a damage window instead of skipping. Alternative: no backstop on P3 at all (a stuck fight then depends on the help ladder alone).
- **The vertical-slice boss is the Patcher** (cheapest: it reuses the rule book). Alternative: the Big Square (fewest moving parts, least AI flavour).
- **Versions:** Stage 2 = v0.8.0–v0.8.5, the boss v0.9.0. Alternative: one minor bump per session (v0.8–v0.14), as Stage 1 did. That runs to 0.14 before Stage 3, which makes the road to 1.0 at Stage 5 awkward. **Asked in the M43 report** (the owner never set the bump rule).
- **Stage 2 length:** ~30–40 min with reading (ROADMAP: ~45; the campaign ≈ 40–47 min of ~2 h 30 so far). **Asked in the M43 report**, with Stage 1's open length question. Default until answered: build at 3.1's lengths. Alternative: add a session in the middle (a second level session between S10 and S11).
- **Move on after the first death** in normal Stage 2 sessions (v8's rule). Alternative: after the second.
- **Weapon count:** 5 shared + 2 Auto-only passives. Alternative: add a flamethrower and a boomerang in a later growth pass ("upgrades can be even more creative").
- **HUD styles:** Clean / Arcade (with score + combo) / On you. Alternative: Arcade without the score. **The mascot sits top-right in every style** (v9.1: no widget overlaps `Mascot.keep_out` / `bubble_rect`). Alternative: On you moves it top-left. The layout is the `fork_hud` pick and the panels follow the look (v9.1).
- **Kill goals by path:** Auto ×2.0, only S9 uses a kills goal. Alternative: Stage 2 avoids kill goals entirely (survive / stages / boss only).
- **Survival swaps on touch** (no "hold E"), so it never clashes with Arsenal's Q/E or the dash bindings. Alternative: hold E to swap, if accidental swaps annoy at G14a.
- **Weapon switching binds:** new `weapon_prev/next` (Q / E, wheel, bumpers) and `weapon_1..3` (keys 1–3 outside panels). E keeps the legacy `wait` too (B2 only). If a bumper is already taken by the dash, the d-pad left / right instead.
- **Rank behaviours:** every rank 2–3 adds a behaviour and rank 4 is a new evolution (1.4). A behaviour too costly in its milestone falls back to a number, logged. Alternative: ranks 2–3 as numbers, behaviours only at rank 4 (cheaper, less creative).
- **Memory lines:** ~8 conditioned lines in Stage 2 (Stage 1's enemy in S10, a death in S10 at S11, the dash in S13, the drawer in S9 and S13). Alternative: more, once the owner has read the lines file.
- **The lines file** ships with the G14a build; the owner's tone edits land by M54. If none come, my drafts stand.
- **Mascot names (ROADMAP 6.1):** still placeholders `Cursor`, `Window`, `Blob`.
- **Lines are English only.** Every line in 3.2 is my draft; the owner edits for tone.
- **G13's open items (PLAYTEST 7a–7j):** their defaults stand (g13-open-questions, g13-feedback). The M43 report and G14a re-ask them in one line each.
- **G14a not answered:** M48 proceeds on these defaults; M47.1 runs only on an explicit pair score ≤ 2. G14b skipped: its defaults stand and its questions move to G14.

- **v9.1 one-liners for the owner (none blocks M47.3; defaults in brackets):**
  1. Splat: soft prebaked sprites for the crowd, live splats on you and on explosions? [yes; fallback voxel blocks]
  2. Pixel / Minimal / Splat replace Neon and Paper? [yes]
  3. A New game shows the raw prototype screen again until S7? [yes, the game version resets]
  4. Stage 1 gets a raw one-line pause? [yes]
  5. Restart from pause counts as a try, with a confirm? [yes]
  6. Controls show your layout's keys now, remapping later? [yes]
  7. The mascot's body follows the look? [yes]
  8. Your save plays the "you missed it" unveil on the next launch? [yes]
  9. "UI polish with 3 styles" = the Clean / Arcade / On you fork, dressed by your look, built right after the looks, still asked in S11 and landing in S12? [yes]

- **v9.2 defaults (my choices; the owner can change any of them):**
  1. **The single boss is the Big Square**, not the Patcher: S12 already builds a square dummy, and an honest, simple boss makes the switch read as something done *to* the AI's work. The Patcher (the AI's own tools turned on you) fits a later boss.
  2. **The earlier accidents stay the AI's own mistakes**; the switch is the first bug that is not.
  3. **The conversion table** of 1.18, with no path-specific turn rules.
  4. **Per-path boss start values:** Auto 1.0 / 1.0, Arsenal 0.85 / 1.2, Survival 0.8 / 1.3 (speed / warning).
  5. **S9 becomes `survive 150`** so the transformation script has fixed times.
  6. **The torn patch-note line** ("turns: enabled") as the first trace of the final boss. Alternative: no trace until Stage 3.
  7. **The three scripted bugs:** swap, tiles, flip.
  8. **The depth pieces** of 1.17 are drafts until the one-page design is answered.
  9. **Look 2 candidates** when the time comes: neon pixel (Pixel's grids, a dark palette and glow: the cheapest), cartoon cutout, minimal, splat.
  10. **The final boss's old plan** (a shooter phase, then an RPG phase; ROADMAP 2) is now used first by this boss. Phase 8 must take it further (for example the genre changing several times, or mid-turn).

**Needed from the owner before M43: nothing.** M43 is plumbing that is identical on Stage 1 whatever the answers. The four M43-report questions (length, versions, fork count, G13 items) are needed by M51 at the latest (`fork_hud`), and the length answer only retunes data.

---

## Appendix: Critiques not taken
The review round (critic 1: owner fit; critic 2: code fit and scope) was taken in full except where listed here. Each entry says what was not taken, or not taken as proposed, and why.

| Critique | Not taken | Why |
|---|---|---|
| C1.1 (fork count) | **Dropping `fork_hud` by default.** `fork_enemy_2` was dropped (the enemy type was Stage 1's fork). | ROADMAP 2 lists the HUD style as a Stage 2 fork. The campaign goes one over "about 10–12" only because of the dash fork the owner added at G13. So the owner decides, asked in one line in the M43 report and needed only before M51. |
| C1.2 (length) | **Holding S7–S13 lengths until the owner answers.** | Session lengths are data (`goal`, `duration`), so building at 3.1's lengths and retuning later costs nothing. Holding them would stall M47 on a question the owner may not answer (they left 7a–7j unanswered). The inference was deleted and the question asked, as proposed. |
| C1.6 (creative upgrades) | **"Every" rank 2–3 is a behaviour, with no exception.** | Taken as the default, with one escape: a behaviour that proves too costly in its milestone falls back to a number, and the report logs it. Without that, one hard trait (the laser's overcharge beam, say) could block M44. |
| C1.8 / C2.31 (mortal gates) | **No mortal gate at all.** One mortal gate stays: *feasibility* (help level 3 + the boss policy wins ≥ 1 of 8 tries in 16/16 seeds). | It does not measure difficulty. It checks that the fight can be won at all, which no invincible run proves (an invincible bot never meets the help ladder). Every other mortal number is reported only, as both critics asked. |
| C1.12 (memory) | **Lines keyed on "how you play".** | The ~8 memory lines use choices (dash, Stage 1 enemy, path) and deaths (`died_in:`). Play-style memory stays with the existing taste reactions (`weapon_unused`, `camping`). The `fp` is report-only by design (bot-runs-as-tuning-proxy) and should not drive lines before a human has checked it. |
| C2.10 (input binds) | **Taking E away from `wait`.** | B2's turn mode in the legacy loop still uses `wait` on E. Two actions on one key are fine in Godot when no context reads both. A test checks that no campaign code reads `wait`. |
| C2.15 (shared caps) | **Splitting the projectile cap everywhere.** | A global player 140 / enemy 60 split could change legacy runs and break the v6 hash compares. It applies only in path sessions; Stage 1 and the legacy loop keep the combined 200. |
| C2.20 (M44 too big) | **Rank-4 evolutions in M46 or M47.** They are in M50.1. | Sessions before S10 are too short to reach rank 4, so the G14a build does not need them. This keeps M46 and M47, the milestones before the owner gate, small. |
| C2.32 (enemy gates) | **Measuring the enemies only after the bot can dodge and flank.** | The sniper `DangerZone` and the flank rule are added in M50, as proposed. The numbers stay "reported, check hit kinds first" anyway, because a flanking bot is still not a human. |
